# WaveBrain v13: does plasticity extend memory? (validated) This document implements the retention experiment recommended after v12: follow the stored state over delays of 0 to 2000 steps with the slow field, the fast field and the hologram separated by lesion, and decode shapes from the modulation map `0.3 s + 0.5 H` under readout noise. It needs **new simulation** (about 10 minutes for both seeds) and extends the package without modifying any v6 to v12 module (checked by hash). Everything below (source, test output, results) comes from running the code in this document. Only `numpy` is required at runtime; `pytest` is needed for the tests. Scope: this is a **state-level** analysis. No probe is run; probe-level retention is not done (section 9). ## 1. Result in one paragraph **The prediction I fixed in advance is refuted at realistic readout noise: the slow field, not the hologram, is the longer and stronger memory over 0 to 2000 steps.** With readout noise equal to the sensor noise, the shape R-squared of the slow field alone goes 0.51 to 0.52 / 0.47 to 0.50 / 0.43 to 0.45 / 0.30 to 0.31 / 0.15 at delays 0, 250, 500, 1000 and 2000 (RBF readout, seeds 0 and 1), while the hologram's part goes 0.16 to 0.19 / 0.15 to 0.17 / 0.12 to 0.16 / 0.09 to 0.12 / 0.05 to 0.08. At delay 2000 the hologram is below the slow field (paired difference -0.099 to -0.072; both intervals entirely below 0). At four times the noise the hologram's part never rises above the noise floor (outcome HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR in both seeds); adding the hologram to the slow field changes nothing (HOLO_SLOW is about equal to SLOW). **A noise-free analysis tells a more nuanced story.** The hologram's information does not decay at all (R-squared 0.34 to 0.36 at every delay), as it must, since H only shrinks by a constant factor and R-squared ignores scale. The slow field is nearly flat to 1000 steps and then starts to fall (0.51 to 0.52 / 0.48 to 0.51 / 0.50 to 0.52 / 0.47 to 0.50 / 0.37 to 0.42), so by delay 2000 the two are close (HOLO minus SLOW -0.080 to -0.009; the interval for seed 1 includes 0). **In information terms the hologram persists; in terms of what survives realistic noise, it does not.** The reason is its amplitude: with `holo_decay = 0.9995` its half-life is only 1386 steps, so over 2000 steps it falls to 37% of its starting amplitude and sinks toward the noise. --- ## 2. What this changes in the story * v12: the part of the state the probe can reach is mostly the slow field, and the hologram's part is small. v13: that picture holds over the whole 0 to 2000 range at realistic noise, and the slow field is long-lived: its half-life under noise is about 1300 and 1281 steps (seeds 0 and 1), not the few hundred steps its damping constant suggests. * The plastic hologram does **not** extend memory beyond the field in this design at the sensor noise level. Whether it would with a lower noise floor or a longer decay time is not tested here, but its information is intact in the noise-free analysis, which says the limit is its **amplitude and decay constant**, not the write rule's ability to store shape. * The experiment covers only about 1.4 hologram half-lives, so it cannot say what happens in the regime where a long-term memory would matter. --- ## 3. Design, corrections and honest caveats ### Lesions (applied at the end of the write phase; the fast field is always zeroed) | lesion | hologram | fast field | slow field | state left | |---|---|---|---|---| | HOLO | on | zeroed | zeroed | H only | | HOLO_SLOW | on | zeroed | kept | H and s | | SLOW | off | zeroed | kept | s only | | NULL | zeros | | | negative control (noise only; no simulation needed) | The fast field is zeroed in every lesion because its linear effect on the probe is removed by the paired baseline subtraction, and because v9 to v11 never separated it from the slow field. Each lesion is followed through delays 0, 250, 500, 1000 and 2000 steps after the write; at each the pooled H and s are captured (361 values over the interior) and the modulation map `0.3 s + 0.5 H` is decoded with the v10 shape targets and regressors, with readout noise at 1 and 4 times the sensor noise (the same realisation for every lesion). Controls: Fourier features (positive), noise-only NULL, and the pooled written map. ### Decision rule (fixed before the reported run) With h(t) and s(t) the best R-squared of HOLO and SLOW at delay t (counted only if the interval versus NULL is above 0) and T the longest delay: **HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR** if h(t) is below 0.10 at every delay; **PLASTICITY_EXTENDS_MEMORY** if h(T) is at least 0.10 and HOLO beats SLOW at T (paired interval above 0); **SLOW_PERSISTS** otherwise if s(T) is at least 0.10; **BOTH_FADE** otherwise. The usual controls come first. Half-lives are reported descriptively. ### What was tuned, and on which data * The tuning pass used **seed 1000** with 60 trials. It exercised the plumbing and showed the same qualitative picture (slow field decays slowly, hologram at the floor). At 60 trials the written-map control read 0.24, just under its 0.25 gate (calibrated at 120 trials), so the tuning verdict was STATE_CONTROL_FAILS; at 240 trials the map reaches 0.43 to 0.44. That is a sample-size effect, and the threshold was not changed. * Seeds **0 and 1** are the reported results. A `--mults` option (so that the noise-free variant could be run) was added after the first reported run; the reported analysis was re-run with the final code and was identical (excluding runtime). The noise-free analysis is **exploratory and post hoc**: it was prompted by the reported result. ### Reconstruction after an environment reset (disclosure) During this step the execution environment was reset: the project directory, the caches and all result files were lost, as were my notes of the numbers (but not the earlier documents). I **rebuilt the project from the v12 document**, re-created the v13 files from the session, re-simulated both seeds and **regenerated every result**. Because the pipeline is deterministic, the regenerated numbers could be compared with those recorded before the reset (slow-field and hologram trajectories at both noise levels, the noise-free trajectories, the paired differences at delay 2000, the verdicts and the controls): **they agree**, which is an independent reproduction. Several background jobs were also killed silently during the run and were restarted; nothing in the results depends on that. ### Caveats 1. **The conclusion depends on the noise model.** At the sensor noise level the hologram's part is weak and decays toward the floor; noise-free it does not decay. The noise model is a calibration (v12), not the real probe path. 2. **The hologram's constant noise-free R-squared is true by construction**: after the write, H only decays by the factor `holo_decay` per step, and R-squared is scale-invariant. It shows the pattern is not lost, not that it is readable. 3. **Why the slow field lasts.** Its damping constant suggests a decay of a few hundred steps, but its low-wavenumber components decay slowly (an overdamped mode); I measured the behaviour, not the mechanism. 4. **Linear readouts fail under noise** (R-squared below zero, also on the noise-only NULL); the verdict uses the best readout, so it is unaffected. 5. **Only the slow-versus-hologram comparison is tested.** The fast field, the probe path and the effect of the noise-equivalence assumption are not examined here. --- ## 4. At x4: 0.50 to 0.51 / 0.39 to 0.42 / 0.26 to 0.29 / 0.14 to 0.15 / 0.12. Half-life under noise (x1): 1300 and 1281 steps. * **Hologram part (HOLO), RBF, noise x1:** 0.16 to 0.19 / 0.15 to 0.17 / 0.12 to 0.16 / 0.09 to 0.12 / 0.05 to 0.08. At x4: 0.01 to 0.04 / 0.01 to 0.03 / -0.00 to 0.02 / -0.01 to 0.01 / -0.01, i.e. at the floor from the start. Half-life (x1): 1623 and 1285 steps. * **Both together (HOLO_SLOW), x1:** 0.50 to 0.52 / 0.44 to 0.47 / 0.39 to 0.42 / 0.26 to 0.28 / 0.13 to 0.14; the hologram adds nothing to the slow field. * **Delay 2000, HOLO minus SLOW (RBF):** x1 -0.099 to -0.072, x4 -0.130 to -0.126, noise-free -0.080 to -0.009. * **Noise-free:** HOLO 0.34 to 0.36 / 0.34 to 0.36 / 0.34 to 0.36 / 0.34 to 0.36 / 0.34 to 0.36 (constant); SLOW 0.51 to 0.52 / 0.48 to 0.51 / 0.50 to 0.52 / 0.47 to 0.50 / 0.37 to 0.42; HOLO_SLOW 0.50 to 0.52 / 0.45 to 0.49 / 0.46 to 0.49 / 0.43 to 0.45 / 0.34. * **Controls:** Fourier features 1.000; pooled written map placement 0.43 to 0.44; NULL (noise only) -0.01. * **Verdicts:** seed 0 SLOW_PERSISTS, seed 1 SLOW_PERSISTS at x1; at x4: HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR and HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR. --- ## 5. What this does and does not show **Shown:** over 0 to 2000 steps, at realistic readout noise, the slow field carries more decodable shape information than the hologram at every delay, and it is long-lived; the hologram adds nothing to it; in a noise-free reading the hologram keeps its information indefinitely (by construction) while the slow field begins to decline after about 1000 steps. **Not shown:** what happens at delays beyond 2000 steps (about 1.4 hologram half-lives); whether a longer hologram decay time or a lower noise floor would let the hologram take over; whether the probe can read what the state holds at these delays (no probe was run); how the fast field behaves. --- ## 6. **Mutation check for the new code**: each defect is injected into a temporary copy and the relevant tests must fail. 14 of 14 were caught on the first run, with the fixed harness (an empty test selection is an error, not a detection). ```text == retention defects == CAUGHT  fast-field lesion zeroes only one time level CAUGHT  'fast' lesion also zeroes the slow field CAUGHT  'both' lesion leaves the slow field CAUGHT  SLOW lesion written with the hologram on CAUGHT  delay stepping runs the cumulative delay each time CAUGHT  modulation coefficients swapped CAUGHT  cache key ignores the delays CAUGHT  half-life not interpolated CAUGHT  interval ignored when counting an R-squared CAUGHT  NULL leak rule disabled CAUGHT  hologram-beats-slow test uses the wrong side of the interval CAUGHT  extension needs no minimum R-squared CAUGHT  hologram floor rule disabled CAUGHT  comparison family level dropped 14/14 injected defects detected ``` The 96 mutations of v6 to v12 were checked in earlier rounds (all caught); those modules are byte-identical to the v12 document. **Mutation-harness audit** (new): pytest exits with code 5 when a selector matches no test, which a naive harness would count as "caught". The harness now treats that as an error, and `python mutation_check.py --audit` verifies that every entry selects at least one test. ```text audited 110 mutation entries across 9 groups entries with an EMPTY test selection: none ``` **Other checks**: the hologram state at 250 steps equals the v11 state capture; the NULL arm (noise only) scores at or below zero; the regenerated results reproduce the numbers recorded before the environment reset. --- ## 8. Limitations * **One medium, one configuration, two seeds** of 240 trials; the seeds agree. * **State level only**: no probe was run, so nothing here says what the probe can read at long delays. * **Delays up to 2000 steps** (about 1.4 hologram half-lives). * **The noise model is a calibration**, and the conclusion depends on it (section 3). * **The hologram's design constants** (`holo_decay` 0.9995, `holo_rate`, `holo_mod`) were not varied. * **The noise-free analysis is exploratory and post hoc.** --- ## 9. Recommended next step The hologram's information is intact but its amplitude is too low and its half-life too short to survive the noise; the slow field wins in the range tested. Three cheap-to-moderate steps follow: 1. **Extend the delay analytically.** H evolves only by decay after the write, so its state at any delay is exact without simulation; only the slow field needs simulating. Run the SLOW lesion alone to about 10000 steps (about 15 to 20 minutes, split by seed) and compute where, in noise-free and noisy terms, the hologram's readable information overtakes the slow field's. 2. **Vary the hologram's design constants** (`holo_decay` 0.9999 or 0.99995, a larger `holo_rate`) in a state-level run, to see whether a hologram with a longer half-life and larger amplitude beats the slow field at realistic noise. 3. **Only then run probe-level retention** (probe at each delay for the three lesions, about 20 minutes extra), to see whether the probe can read the hologram when it is strong enough. --- ## 10. Quick start ```bash pip install numpy pytest                  # coverage is optional python -m pytest                          # about 5 min, 244 tests python -m wavebrain.retention --seeds 0,1 --cache cache_ret --json retention_results.json            # about 10 min the first time (simulates), about 1.5 min afterwards python -m wavebrain.retention --seeds 0,1 --mults 0.0 --cache cache_ret --json retention_noisefree_results.json   # exploratory, no readout noise python mutation_check.py --group retention python mutation_check.py --audit ``` If your environment kills long background jobs, run one seed at a time: `python -m wavebrain.retention --seeds 0 --cache cache_ret`, then `--seeds 1`, then the combined run from the cache. Use another seed (for example 1000) when experimenting so seeds 0 and 1 stay untouched. ## 11. Project layout ```text wavebrain_project/   pytest.ini  mutation_check.py (extended)  seed_check.py   wavebrain/     config.py sources.py sensors.py medium.py classifier.py experiment.py benchmark.py   (v6, unchanged)     stats.py pairtask.py pilot.py                                                          (v7, unchanged)     readouts.py study.py baselines.py                                                      (v8, unchanged)     randshapes.py randstudy.py                                                             (v9, unchanged)     decode.py noisecheck.py                                                                (v10, unchanged)     statedecode.py modmap.py                                                               (v11, v12, unchanged)     retention.py    NEW  lesions, delay snapshots, cache, half-lives, verdict, runner   tests/            244 tests; new: test_retention.py ``` --- ## 12. Source code ### 12.1 New module #### `wavebrain/retention.py` ```python """Does plasticity extend memory?   ``python -m wavebrain.retention`` v12 found that the part of the stored state a probe can couple to, ``slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H``, is carried almost entirely by the SLOW field at a delay of 250 steps, and that the hologram's part is small and fragile under noise.  The hologram is the only memory designed to persist, so the question is what happens over longer delays.  Prediction fixed before the reported run: the slow field fades and the hologram's part persists, so the hologram overtakes it at long delays. Lesions applied at the end of the write phase (the fast field is always zeroed: its linear effect on the probe is removed by the paired baseline subtraction, and v9 to v11 never separated it from the slow field)     HOLO       hologram on,  fast and slow fields zeroed   -> H only     HOLO_SLOW  hologram on,  fast field zeroed             -> H and s     SLOW       hologram off, fast field zeroed             -> s only     NULL       all zero (no simulation needed)             -> negative control (noise only) Each lesion is followed for several delays; at each, the pooled modulation map ``slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H`` is decoded (shape descriptors, v10 targets) with readout noise added as in v12.  STATE level only: no probe is run. Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import hashlib import json import time from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from .decode import (DECODE_READOUTS, bootstrap_r2, make_regressor, position_targets, r2_per_output, shape_basis,                      shape_targets, squared_errors) from .modmap import MIN_R2, add_noise from .pilot import strip_private from .randshapes import build_rand_raw, rand_write_phase from .randstudy import BASELINE_SETS, rand_arm_config from .statedecode import POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2, STATE_CONTROL_MIN, build_written_maps, interior_pool from .study import clear_fields, combine_verdicts DELAYS = (0, 250, 500, 1000, 2000) MULTS = (1.0, 4.0) PRIMARY_MULT = "1.0" LESIONS = {"HOLO": ("FULL", "both"), "HOLO_SLOW": ("FULL", "fast"), "SLOW": ("RING_ONLY", "fast")} DECODED = ("HOLO", "HOLO_SLOW", "SLOW") # ------------------------------------------------------------------- lesions def zero_fast_field(sim) -> None:     """Zero the fast field at all time levels; keep the slow field, the hologram and the clock."""     sim._f[...] = 0.0 def apply_lesion(sim, what: str) -> None:     if what == "both":         clear_fields(sim)     elif what == "fast":         zero_fast_field(sim)     else:         raise ValueError(f"unknown lesion '{what}'") # ----------------------------------------------------------------- snapshots def trial_snapshots(cfg_full, cfg_ring, index: int, delays=DELAYS) -> tuple[dict, float]:     """Write the trial once with the hologram on and once with it off, apply each lesion, follow it     through ``delays`` (steps after the write) and snapshot the pooled H and s at each."""     sp = cfg_full.base.medium.sponge_width     written = {"FULL": rand_write_phase(cfg_full, index)[0], "RING_ONLY": rand_write_phase(cfg_ring, index)[0]}     out, clips = {}, 0.0     for name, (arm, what) in LESIONS.items():         sim = written[arm].clone()         apply_lesion(sim, what)         t, H, s = 0, [], []         for d in delays:             sim.run(d - t)             t = d             H.append(interior_pool(sim.H, sp)); s.append(interior_pool(sim.s, sp))         out[name] = {"H": np.stack(H), "s": np.stack(s)}         clips = max(clips, float(sim.clip_events))     return out, clips def build_retention_arrays(cfg_full, cfg_ring, delays=DELAYS, verbose: bool = False) -> dict:     per, clips = [], 0.0     t0 = time.time()     for i in range(cfg_full.n_trials):         snap, c = trial_snapshots(cfg_full, cfg_ring, i, delays)         per.append(snap); clips = max(clips, c)         if verbose and (i + 1) % 40 == 0:             print(f"    trial {i + 1}/{cfg_full.n_trials} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     arrays = {"clip_events": clips, "delays": np.array(delays)}     for name in LESIONS:         arrays[f"H_{name}"] = np.stack([p[name]["H"] for p in per])        # (n_trials, n_delays, 361)         arrays[f"s_{name}"] = np.stack([p[name]["s"] for p in per])     return arrays def retention_key(cfg, delays) -> str:     return hashlib.sha256((repr(cfg) + f"|retention|{tuple(delays)}").encode()).hexdigest()[:16] def get_retention_arrays(seed: int, n_trials: int, delays=DELAYS, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = False):     """(arrays, from_cache); a stale or missing cache entry is rebuilt, never reused."""     cfg_full, cfg_ring = rand_arm_config("FULL", seed, n_trials), rand_arm_config("RING_ONLY", seed, n_trials)     path = None     if cache_dir is not None:         Path(cache_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)         path = Path(cache_dir) / f"retention_seed{seed}_n{n_trials}.npz"         if path.exists():             with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as z:                 if str(z["key"]) == retention_key(cfg_full, delays):                     return {k: (z[k] if z[k].ndim else float(z[k])) for k in z.files if k != "key"}, True             if verbose:                 print(f"    [cache] stale entry for seed {seed}: rebuilding", flush=True)     arrays = build_retention_arrays(cfg_full, cfg_ring, delays, verbose)     if path is not None:         np.savez(path, key=retention_key(cfg_full, delays), **arrays)     return arrays, False def mod_features(arrays: dict, lesion: str, j: int, medium) -> np.ndarray:     """slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H at delay index ``j`` (the quantity the probe couples to)."""     return medium.slow_mod * arrays[f"s_{lesion}"][:, j] + medium.holo_mod * arrays[f"H_{lesion}"][:, j] # ------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def best_r2(sres: dict, lesion: str, delay, mult: str) -> tuple[float, str]:     """(best R2 over readouts, counted only if its interval versus NULL is above 0; readout)."""     cells = sres["cells"][lesion][str(delay)][mult]     r = max(cells, key=lambda k: cells[k]["r2"])     c = cells[r]     return (c["r2"] if c["diff_vs_null"]["lo"] > 0 else 0.0), r def half_life(sres: dict, lesion: str, mult: str = PRIMARY_MULT):     """Delay at which the best R2 first falls below half of its delay-0 value (linear interpolation);     ``">T"`` if it never does within the sweep, None if the delay-0 value is below MIN_R2.  Descriptive."""     ds = sorted(int(d) for d in sres["cells"][lesion])     vals = [max(c["r2"] for c in sres["cells"][lesion][str(d)][mult].values()) for d in ds]     if vals[0] < MIN_R2:         return None     half = vals[0] / 2.0     for (d1, v1), (d2, v2) in zip(zip(ds, vals), zip(ds[1:], vals[1:])):         if v2 < half <= v1:             return float(d1 + (v1 - half) / (v1 - v2) * (d2 - d1))     return f">{ds[-1]}" def verdict(sres: dict, mult: str = PRIMARY_MULT) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED          the safety clip fired.     -  INCONCLUSIVE             HOLO or SLOW missing.     1. POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS   Fourier features do not decode the descriptors (R2 < 0.95).     2. CONTROL_FAILS            NULL (noise only) has R2 > 0.05 anywhere: a leak.     3. STATE_CONTROL_FAILS      the pooled written map does not decode placement (R2 < 0.25).     With h(t), s(t) the best R2 (counted only if the interval versus NULL is above 0) of HOLO and SLOW at     delay t and T the longest delay:     4. HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR    h(t) < 0.10 at every delay: the hologram's part never rises above the noise.     5. PLASTICITY_EXTENDS_MEMORY h(T) >= 0.10 AND HOLO beats SLOW at T (paired interval of the difference,                                  for HOLO's best readout, above 0).        SLOW_PERSISTS             otherwise, s(T) >= 0.10: the slow field is the longer memory.        BOTH_FADE                 otherwise.     Half-lives (delay at which R2 halves) are reported as a descriptive statistic.     """     if sres["clip_events"] > 0:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": "Safety clip fired.", "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if "HOLO" not in sres["cells"] or "SLOW" not in sres["cells"]:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "Needs HOLO and SLOW.", "next": "Run with both."}     pc = sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"]["shape"]["linear"]["r2"]     if pc < POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2:         return {"outcome": "POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS", "text": f"Fourier features decode with R2 = {pc:.3f} < {POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2}.",                 "next": "Fix the target construction first."}     leaks = [(m, r) for m, d in sres["null"].items() for r, c in d.items() if c["r2"] > 0.05]     if leaks:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS", "text": f"NULL (noise only) has R2 > 0.05 for {leaks[:3]}: a leak.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel first."}     mp = max(c["r2"] for c in sres["baselines"]["map"]["position"].values())     if mp < STATE_CONTROL_MIN:         return {"outcome": "STATE_CONTROL_FAILS", "text": f"The pooled written map decodes placement with R2 = {mp:.2f} < {STATE_CONTROL_MIN}.",                 "next": "Fix the pipeline first."}     ds = sorted(int(d) for d in sres["cells"]["HOLO"])     T = ds[-1]     h = {d: best_r2(sres, "HOLO", d, mult)[0] for d in ds}     s = {d: best_r2(sres, "SLOW", d, mult)[0] for d in ds}     hl = {n: half_life(sres, n, mult) for n in DECODED if n in sres["cells"]}     tail = (f" HOLO R2 by delay: {', '.join(f'{d}: {h[d]:.2f}' for d in ds)}; SLOW: {', '.join(f'{d}: {s[d]:.2f}' for d in ds)}. "             f"Half-lives: {hl}.")     base = {"half_lives": hl}     if max(h.values()) < MIN_R2:         return {"outcome": "HOLO_NEVER_ABOVE_FLOOR", **base,                 "text": f"The hologram's part never reaches R2 {MIN_R2} at noise x{mult}." + tail,                 "next": "The write rule stores too little to survive this noise; redesign it (for example a reference-beam hologram)."}     r = best_r2(sres, "HOLO", T, mult)[1]     beats = sres["hs"][str(T)][mult][r]["lo"] > 0     if h[T] >= MIN_R2 and beats:         return {"outcome": "PLASTICITY_EXTENDS_MEMORY", **base,                 "text": f"At delay {T} the hologram's part decodes (R2 = {h[T]:.2f}) and beats the slow field (paired interval above 0)." + tail,                 "next": "Plasticity extends memory beyond the field; make the hologram readable (probe design) and re-test."}     if s[T] >= MIN_R2:         return {"outcome": "SLOW_PERSISTS", **base,                 "text": f"At delay {T} the slow field still decodes (R2 = {s[T]:.2f}) and the hologram does not beat it." + tail,                 "next": "The slow field is the longer memory in this design; examine its decay law and what it stores."}     return {"outcome": "BOTH_FADE", **base,             "text": f"Neither the hologram's part nor the slow field decodes at delay {T}." + tail,             "next": "Memory is short-lived here; strengthen the write or redesign the plastic rule."} # ------------------------------------------------------------------- the study def run_retention(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), delays=DELAYS, readouts=DECODE_READOUTS, mults=MULTS,                   cache_dir=None, n_boot: int = 2000, k: int = 5, repeats: int = 3, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     basis = shape_basis()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "delays": list(delays), "readouts": list(readouts),                       "mults": list(mults), "n_boot": n_boot}, "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         cfg = rand_arm_config("FULL", seed, n_trials)         Y = shape_targets(cfg, basis)         arrays, _ = get_retention_arrays(seed, n_trials, delays, cache_dir, verbose)         medium = cfg.base.medium         zero = np.zeros((n_trials, arrays["H_HOLO"].shape[2]))         level_null = 1.0 - 0.05 / (len(DECODED) * len(readouts))         level_hs = 1.0 - 0.05 / len(readouts)         E_null = {(str(m), r): squared_errors(add_noise(zero, m, seed, mi), Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)                   for mi, m in enumerate(mults) for r in readouts}         E = {}         for name in DECODED:             for j, d in enumerate(delays):                 X = mod_features(arrays, name, j, medium)                 for mi, m in enumerate(mults):                     Xn = add_noise(X, m, seed, mi)                     for r in readouts:                         E[(name, str(d), str(m), r)] = squared_errors(Xn, Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)         sres = {"cells": {}, "null": {}, "hs": {}, "baselines": {}, "delays": list(delays),                 "clip_events": float(arrays["clip_events"])}         for m in mults:             sres["null"][str(m)] = {r: {"r2": bootstrap_r2(E_null[(str(m), r)], Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)["point"]} for r in readouts}         for name in DECODED:             sres["cells"][name] = {}             for d in delays:                 sres["cells"][name][str(d)] = {}                 for m in mults:                     sres["cells"][name][str(d)][str(m)] = {}                     for r in readouts:                         Ea = E[(name, str(d), str(m), r)]                         ci = bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)                         dn = bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, E_null[(str(m), r)], n_boot, seed, level_null)                         sres["cells"][name][str(d)][str(m)][r] = {                             "r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"],                             "diff_vs_null": {"diff": dn["point"], "lo": dn["lo"], "hi": dn["hi"]},                             "per_output": [float(x) for x in r2_per_output(Ea, Y)]}         for d in delays:             sres["hs"][str(d)] = {str(m): {r: bootstrap_r2(E[("HOLO", str(d), str(m), r)], Y, E[("SLOW", str(d), str(m), r)],                                                             n_boot, seed, level_hs) for r in readouts} for m in mults}         raw, _ = build_rand_raw(cfg)         P = position_targets(cfg)         sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"] = {"shape": {r: {"r2": bootstrap_r2(squared_errors(BASELINE_SETS["fft_concat"](raw), Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed), Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)["point"]} for r in readouts}}         sres["baselines"]["map"] = {"position": {r: {"r2": bootstrap_r2(squared_errors(build_written_maps(cfg), P, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed), P, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)["point"]} for r in readouts}}         primary = PRIMARY_MULT if PRIMARY_MULT in {str(m) for m in mults} else str(mults[0])         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres, primary)         sres["verdict_at"] = {str(m): verdict(sres, str(m))["outcome"] for m in mults}         res["seeds"][seed] = sres         if verbose:             print(f"[seed {seed}] {sres['verdict']['outcome']}: {sres['verdict']['text'][:200]}", flush=True)     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain retention of the stored state (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, delays={c['delays']}, "              f"readout noise = multiple of the sensor noise 0.015; STATE level, no probe)"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         for m in c["mults"]:             lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}, noise x{m}: shape R2 [95% CI] of the modulation map by delay ===",                       f"{'lesion / delay':<18}" + "".join(f"{r:>24}" for r in rd), "-" * (18 + 24 * len(rd))]             for n in DECODED:                 for d in c["delays"]:                     cells = s["cells"][n][str(d)][str(m)]                     lines.append(f"{n + ' ' + str(d):<18}" + "".join(                         f"{cells[r]['r2']:+.2f} [{cells[r]['r2_lo']:+.2f},{cells[r]['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(24) for r in rd))             lines.append(f"{'NULL (noise only)':<18}" + "".join(f"{s['null'][str(m)][r]['r2']:+.2f}".rjust(24) for r in rd))         T = str(c["delays"][-1])         lines += ["", f"HOLO minus SLOW at delay {T} [paired CI]: " + "  ".join(             f"x{m} {r} {s['hs'][T][str(m)][r]['point']:+.3f} [{s['hs'][T][str(m)][r]['lo']:+.3f},{s['hs'][T][str(m)][r]['hi']:+.3f}]"             for m in c["mults"] for r in rd)]         lines.append("controls: Fourier features shape R2 "                      f"{s['baselines']['fft_concat']['shape']['linear']['r2']:+.3f}; pooled written map placement R2 "                      f"{max(v['r2'] for v in s['baselines']['map']['position'].values()):+.2f}")         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}, noise x{PRIMARY_MULT}): {v['outcome']}", f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}",                   f"  outcome at each noise level: {s['verdict_at']}"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {res['combined']['outcome']}  (per seed: {res['combined']['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--delays", default=",".join(str(d) for d in DELAYS))     ap.add_argument("--mults", default=",".join(str(m) for m in MULTS),                     help="readout-noise multipliers; 0.0 = no noise (exploratory: does the information itself decay?)")     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None, help="directory for the cached retention arrays")     ap.add_argument("--boot", type=int, default=2000)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_retention(a.trials, tuple(int(x) for x in a.seeds.split(",")), tuple(int(x) for x in a.delays.split(",")),                         mults=tuple(float(x) for x in a.mults.split(",")), cache_dir=a.cache, n_boot=a.boot)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` ### 12.2 New test and the extended mutation script #### `tests/test_retention.py` ```python """Tests for the retention experiment: lesions, delay stepping, cache, half-life, verdict, runner.""" import json from dataclasses import replace import numpy as np ------------------------------------------------------- half-life and verdict D5 = (0, 250, 500, 1000, 2000) def _cell(r2, lo=0.05):     return {"r2": r2, "r2_lo": r2 - 0.05, "r2_hi": r2 + 0.05, "diff_vs_null": {"diff": r2, "lo": lo, "hi": r2 + 0.1}} def _sres(h=(0.2, 0.2, 0.18, 0.15, 0.12), s=(0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.05, 0.0), lo=0.05, hs_lo=0.05, null=-0.01, pc=0.999,           mp=0.4, clip=0.0, "comparison_levels"), ] def run_group(entries) -> int:     caught = 0     for name, rel, old, new, tests, *rest in entries:         with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:             dst = Path(tmp) / "proj"             shutil.copytree(ROOT, dst, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns(                 "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", "*.json", "*.txt", ".coverage"))             f = dst / rel             src = f.read_text()             assert old in src, f"mutation target not found for: {name}"             f.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1))             cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-x", "-q", *tests.split()]             if rest and rest[0]:                 cmd += ["-k", rest[0]]             r = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=dst, capture_output=True, text=True)             if r.returncode == 5:                      # pytest: "no tests collected" -- NOT a detection                 print(f"ERROR   no tests selected for: {name}", flush=True)                 continue             killed = r.returncode != 0             caught += killed             print(f"{'CAUGHT ' if killed else 'MISSED '} {name}", flush=True)     print(f"{caught}/{len(entries)} injected defects detected")     return len(entries) - caught def audit() -> int:     """Every mutation entry must select at least one test (pytest exits with 5 when none is selected, which a     naive harness would count as 'caught')."""     import re     groups = {"v6": MUTATIONS, "pilot": PILOT_MUTATIONS, "readout": READOUT_MUTATIONS, "rand": RAND_MUTATIONS,               "decode": DECODE_MUTATIONS, "noise": NOISE_MUTATIONS, "state": STATE_MUTATIONS,               "modmap": MODMAP_MUTATIONS, "retention": RETENTION_MUTATIONS}     bad, total = [], 0     for g, entries in groups.items():         for name, rel, old, new, tests, *rest in entries:             total += 1             cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "--collect-only", "-q", *tests.split()]             if rest and rest[0]:                 cmd += ["-k", rest[0]]             r = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True)             n = sum(int(x) for x in re.findall(r": (\d+)\s*$", r.stdout, re.M)) or len(re.findall(r"::", r.stdout))             if r.returncode != 0 or n == 0:                 bad.append((g, name, rest[0] if rest else None))     print(f"audited {total} mutation entries across {len(groups)} groups")     print("entries with an EMPTY test selection:", bad if bad else "none")     return 1 if bad else 0 def main() -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()     ap.add_argument("--audit", action="store_true", help="check that every mutation entry selects at least one test")     ap.add_argument("--group", choices=["v6", "pilot", "readout", "rand", "decode", "noise", "state", "modmap", "retention", "all"], default="all")     a = ap.parse_args()     if a.audit:         return audit()     missed = 0     if a.group in ("v6", "all"):         print("== v6 defects ==")         missed += run_group(MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("pilot", "all"):         print("== pilot defects ==")         missed += run_group(PILOT_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("readout", "all"):         print("== readout-study defects ==")         missed += run_group(READOUT_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("rand", "all"):         print("== random-shape-study defects ==")         missed += run_group(RAND_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("decode", "all"):         print("== shape-decoding defects ==")         missed += run_group(DECODE_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("noise", "all"):         print("== noise-follow-up defects ==")         missed += run_group(NOISE_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("state", "all"):         print("== state-decoding defects ==")         missed += run_group(STATE_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("modmap", "all"):         print("== modulation-map defects ==")         missed += run_group(MODMAP_MUTATIONS)     if a.group in ("retention", "all"):         print("== retention defects ==")         missed += run_group(RETENTION_MUTATIONS)     return 0 if missed == 0 else 1 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` ### 12.3 Unchanged v6 to v12 modules (included so this document is self-contained) #### `wavebrain/__init__.py` ```python """WaveBrain: a validated 2-D wave-medium reservoir with plastic memory.""" from .config import ExperimentConfig, MediumConfig from .medium import WaveMedium from .sensors import SensorArray, check_timing, ring_layout from .sources import PATTERN_KINDS, make_pattern __all__ = ["ExperimentConfig", "MediumConfig", "WaveMedium", "SensorArray",            "check_timing", "ring_layout", "PATTERN_KINDS", "make_pattern"] ``` #### `wavebrain/config.py` ```python """Configuration objects with built-in stability validation. Units: grid spacing dx = 1 cell, time step dt = 1 step.  Field amplitudes are normalised so that "typical" values are O(1). """ from __future__ import annotations from dataclasses import dataclass, field import numpy as np @dataclass(frozen=True) class MediumConfig:     """Parameters of the 2-D nonlinear wave medium.     Fast field u:   u_tt = c2(x,t) * lap(u) - gamma * u_t - beta * u^3 + forcing     Slow field s:   s_tt = c_slow^2 * lap(s) - gamma_s * s_t + slow_drive * lap(u)     Local speed:    c2 = clip(c_fast^2 * (1 + slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H), c2_min, c2_max)     Hologram:       H <- sat * tanh((decay * H + rate * u_n * u_{n-1} / |u|) / sat)     The restoring cubic term (beta > 0) and the clipped wave speed give a     *checkable* stability certificate (see ``stability_number``).     """     ny: int = 112     nx: int = 112     c_fast: float = 0.40     c_slow: float = 0.10     damping: float = 0.008          # gamma (fast); amplitude e-folds in ~2/gamma steps     damping_slow: float = 0.02      # gamma_s     beta: float = 0.05              # restoring (hardening) cubic coefficient     slow_drive: float = 0.10        # forcing of slow field by lap(u)     slow_mod: float = 0.30          # c2 modulation by slow field     holo_rate: float = 0.05         # hologram write rate     holo_decay: float = 0.9995      # per-step hologram retention     holo_sat: float = 1.0           # hologram saturation level     holo_mod: float = 0.50          # c2 modulation by hologram     c2_min_frac: float = 0.20       # c2_min = c2_min_frac * c_fast^2     c2_max: float = 0.38            # hard cap on local c2 (2-D leapfrog limit is 0.5)     u_limit: float = 2.0            # safety clip; clip_events counts violations     sponge_width: int = 18     sponge_strength: float = 0.40   # peak friction gamma of the viscous layer (outer edge)     sponge_power: float = 3.0     dtype: str = "float32"     def __post_init__(self) -> None:         if min(self.nx, self.ny) < 2 * self.sponge_width + 8:             raise ValueError("grid too small for the requested sponge width")         if self.c_fast <= 0 or self.c_slow < 0:             raise ValueError("wave speeds must be positive")         if not (0.0 <= self.damping < 1.0 and 0.0 <= self.damping_slow < 1.0):             raise ValueError("damping must lie in [0, 1)")         if self.beta < 0:             raise ValueError("beta must be >= 0 (restoring); beta < 0 is anti-restoring")         if not (0.0 < self.holo_decay <= 1.0):             raise ValueError("holo_decay must lie in (0, 1]")         if self.holo_sat <= 0 or self.u_limit <= 0:             raise ValueError("holo_sat and u_limit must be positive")         if self.c_fast ** 2 > self.c2_max:             raise ValueError("c_fast**2 exceeds c2_max")         if not (0.0 < self.c2_min_frac <= 1.0):             raise ValueError("c2_min_frac must lie in (0, 1]")         if self.stability_number > 0.95 * 4.0:             raise ValueError(                 f"unstable configuration: 8*c2_max + 3*beta*u_limit^2 = "                 f"{self.stability_number:.3f} must be <= 3.8 (hard limit 4)"             )     @property     def c2_nominal(self) -> float:         return self.c_fast ** 2     @property     def c2_min(self) -> float:         return self.c2_min_frac * self.c_fast ** 2     @property     def stability_number(self) -> float:         """Upper bound on omega_max^2 * dt^2 of the explicit scheme (must be < 4).         8 * c2_max bounds the spectral radius of c2 * Laplacian (|lap| <= 8),         3 * beta * u_limit^2 bounds the stiffness of the cubic term.         """         return 8.0 * self.c2_max + 3.0 * self.beta * self.u_limit ** 2 @dataclass(frozen=True) class ExperimentConfig:     """A pattern-classification experiment on the medium (see experiment.py)."""     medium: MediumConfig = field(default_factory=MediumConfig)     classes: tuple = ("circle", "cross", "ring")     trials_per_class: int = 30     pattern_size: int = 24     jitter: int = 3                 # +/- cells of random placement jitter     # --- write (engraving) phase     write_steps: int = 250     write_omega: float = 0.30     write_amp: float = 0.02     use_hologram: bool = True       # False = ablation (plasticity disabled)     # --- delay between write and read     delay_steps: int = 250     # --- probe / readout     readout_steps: int = 360     probe_omega: float = 0.35     probe_amp: float = 0.02     probe_tau: float = 16.0         # burst envelope width (steps)     n_sensors_per_side: int = 8     sensor_sides: tuple = ("right", "top", "bottom")     downsample: int = 4     sensor_noise: float = 0.015     # std of additive noise per raw sample     # --- optional uncontrolled background activity     background_pacemakers: int = 0     background_amp: float = 0.005     seed: int = 0 ``` #### `wavebrain/sources.py` ```python """Forcing sources, time envelopes, patterns and pacemakers. Envelopes are small frozen dataclasses (not lambdas/closures) so that a simulation can be deep-copied safely: a clone never shares mutable state. """ from __future__ import annotations from dataclasses import dataclass from typing import Callable import numpy as np PATTERN_KINDS = ("circle", "cross", "ring") # ----------------------------------------------------------------- envelopes @dataclass(frozen=True) class Sine:     amp: float     omega: float     phase: float = 0.0     def __call__(self, t: float) -> float:         return self.amp * np.sin(self.omega * t + self.phase) @dataclass(frozen=True) class GaussianBurst:     """amp * sin(omega (t - t0)) * exp(-(t - t0)^2 / (2 tau^2))"""     amp: float     omega: float     t0: float     tau: float     def __call__(self, t: float) -> float:         x = t - self.t0         return self.amp * np.sin(self.omega * x) * np.exp(-0.5 * (x / self.tau) ** 2) @dataclass(frozen=True) class GaussianPulse:     """amp * exp(-(t - t0)^2 / (2 tau^2))  (no carrier)"""     amp: float     t0: float     tau: float     def __call__(self, t: float) -> float:         return self.amp * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - self.t0) / self.tau) ** 2) @dataclass(frozen=True) class Gated:     """Run ``inner`` only for t_start <= t < t_stop, with sin^2 ramps of ``ramp`` steps."""     inner: Callable[[float], float]     t_start: float     t_stop: float     ramp: float = 20.0     def __call__(self, t: float) -> float:         if t < self.t_start or t >= self.t_stop:             return 0.0         edge = min(t - self.t_start, self.t_stop - t)         w = 1.0 if edge >= self.ramp else float(np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * edge / self.ramp) ** 2)         return w * self.inner(t) # ------------------------------------------------------------------- sources @dataclass class Source:     """A spatial ``profile`` placed with its corner at (y0, x0), driven by ``envelope(t)``.     Sources are *forcing terms* of the PDE (not hard overwrites of the state).     """     profile: np.ndarray     y0: int     x0: int     envelope: Callable[[float], float]     name: str = ""     def __post_init__(self) -> None:         self.profile = np.asarray(self.profile, dtype=float)         if self.profile.ndim != 2:             raise ValueError("profile must be 2-D")     @property     def y1(self) -> int:         return self.y0 + self.profile.shape[0]     @property     def x1(self) -> int:         return self.x0 + self.profile.shape[1]     def check_inside(self, ny: int, nx: int) -> None:         if self.y0 < 1 or self.x0 < 1 or self.y1 > ny - 1 or self.x1 > nx - 1:             raise ValueError(                 f"source '{self.name}' [{self.y0}:{self.y1}, {self.x0}:{self.x1}] must lie "                 f"inside the interior [1:{ny - 1}, 1:{nx - 1}]"             ) def line_profile(length: int, thickness: int = 2) -> np.ndarray:     """Hann-tapered vertical line (tapering removes edge-diffraction artefacts)."""     taper = np.hanning(length + 2)[1:-1]     return taper[:, None] * np.ones((1, thickness)) def gaussian_profile(radius: int) -> np.ndarray:     yy, xx = np.mgrid[-radius:radius + 1, -radius:radius + 1]     return np.exp(-(xx ** 2 + yy ** 2) / (2.0 * (radius / 2.0) ** 2)) def make_pacemakers(n: int, shape: tuple[int, int], margin: int, rng: np.random.Generator,                     amp: float, omega_range: tuple[float, float] = (0.15, 0.40),                     radius: int = 3) -> list[Source]:     """Random sinusoidal point-like emitters (uncontrolled background activity)."""     ny, nx = shape     out = []     for i in range(n):         cy = int(rng.integers(margin + radius, ny - margin - radius))         cx = int(rng.integers(margin + radius, nx - margin - radius))         env = Sine(amp=amp, omega=float(rng.uniform(*omega_range)),                    phase=float(rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi)))         out.append(Source(gaussian_profile(radius), cy - radius, cx - radius, env, f"pacemaker{i}"))     return out # ------------------------------------------------------------------ patterns def _smooth(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     for ax in (0, 1):         p = np.pad(a, [(1, 1) if i == ax else (0, 0) for i in range(2)])         if ax == 0:             a = (p[:-2, :] + 2 * p[1:-1, :] + p[2:, :]) / 4.0         else:             a = (p[:, :-2] + 2 * p[:, 1:-1] + p[:, 2:]) / 4.0     return a def make_pattern(kind: str, size: int = 24, energy: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:     """Binary shape, lightly smoothed, normalised to a common energy sum(p^2).     Equal energy means the classes cannot be told apart by total drive alone:     any class information must come from the *shape*.     """     if kind not in PATTERN_KINDS:         raise ValueError(f"unknown pattern '{kind}'")     c = (size - 1) / 2.0     yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:size, 0:size]     r2 = (xx - c) ** 2 + (yy - c) ** 2     if kind == "circle":         p = (r2 < (0.45 * size) ** 2).astype(float)     elif kind == "cross":         arm = 0.30 * size / 2.0         p = ((np.abs(yy - c) < arm) | (np.abs(xx - c) < arm)).astype(float)     else:         p = ((r2 > (0.23 * size) ** 2) & (r2 < (0.47 * size) ** 2)).astype(float)     p = _smooth(p)     target = 0.25 * size * size if energy is None else energy     return p * np.sqrt(target / np.sum(p ** 2)) ``` #### `wavebrain/sensors.py` ```python """Sensor layout, placement validation and probe-timing checks. These checks exist because the original readout (a) sampled the outermost grid column, which the stencil never updates and the absorber zeroes, so it only contained 1e-4 noise, and (b) used a 220-step window although the probe needs ~576 steps to cross the domain. """ from __future__ import annotations from dataclasses import dataclass import numpy as np from .config import MediumConfig @dataclass(frozen=True) class SensorArray:     rows: np.ndarray     cols: np.ndarray     def __post_init__(self) -> None:         object.__setattr__(self, "rows", np.asarray(self.rows, dtype=int))         object.__setattr__(self, "cols", np.asarray(self.cols, dtype=int))         if self.rows.shape != self.cols.shape or self.rows.ndim != 1 or len(self.rows) == 0:             raise ValueError("rows and cols must be equal-length, non-empty 1-D arrays")     def __len__(self) -> int:         return len(self.rows)     def validate(self, cfg: MediumConfig, extra_margin: int = 1) -> None:         """Raise if any sensor lies in the boundary ring or inside the absorbing layer."""         m = cfg.sponge_width + extra_margin         ok = ((self.rows >= m) & (self.rows < cfg.ny - m) &               (self.cols >= m) & (self.cols < cfg.nx - m))         if not ok.all():             bad = np.flatnonzero(~ok)[:5].tolist()             raise ValueError(                 f"sensors {bad}... lie within {m} cells of the edge (boundary ring / absorber); "                 f"signals there are attenuated or never updated"             ) def ring_layout(cfg: MediumConfig, n_per_side: int = 8, inset: int = 3,                 sides: tuple = ("right", "top", "bottom")) -> SensorArray:     """Sensors on lines just inside the absorbing layer (probe enters from the left)."""     m = cfg.sponge_width + inset     lo_r, hi_r = m, cfg.ny - m - 1     lo_c, hi_c = m, cfg.nx - m - 1     along_r = np.linspace(lo_r, hi_r, n_per_side).round().astype(int)     # top/bottom lines skip the corner cells, which already belong to the left/right lines     along_c = np.linspace(lo_c, hi_c, n_per_side + 2)[1:-1].round().astype(int)     rows, cols = [], []     for side in sides:         if side == "right":             rows += along_r.tolist(); cols += [hi_c] * n_per_side         elif side == "left":             rows += along_r.tolist(); cols += [lo_c] * n_per_side         elif side == "top":             rows += [lo_r] * n_per_side; cols += along_c.tolist()         elif side == "bottom":             rows += [hi_r] * n_per_side; cols += along_c.tolist()         else:             raise ValueError(f"unknown side '{side}'")     arr = SensorArray(np.array(rows), np.array(cols))     arr.validate(cfg)     return arr def probe_geometry(cfg: MediumConfig, inset: int = 3) -> tuple[int, int, int]:     """(column, row_start, row_stop) of the vertical probe line source (2 columns thick)."""     col = cfg.sponge_width + inset     return col, cfg.sponge_width + inset, cfg.ny - cfg.sponge_width - inset def arrival_steps(cfg: MediumConfig, sensors: SensorArray, probe_col: int,                   probe_rows: tuple[int, int], slowest: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:     """Straight-line travel time (steps) from the probe line to every sensor."""     r0, r1 = probe_rows     dy = np.maximum(np.maximum(r0 - sensors.rows, sensors.rows - (r1 - 1)), 0)     dx = np.abs(sensors.cols - probe_col)     dist = np.hypot(dx, dy)     c = np.sqrt(cfg.c2_min) if slowest else cfg.c_fast     return dist / c def check_timing(cfg: MediumConfig, sensors: SensorArray, probe_col: int,                  probe_rows: tuple[int, int], readout_steps: int,                  burst_steps: float, margin: float = 1.2) -> dict:     """Raise ValueError unless the whole probe burst can reach every sensor in the window.     ``margin`` (> 1) allows for numerical group-velocity dispersion: measured arrivals     of the narrow-band burst are ~10-15% later than distance / c_fast.     """     arr = arrival_steps(cfg, sensors, probe_col, probe_rows)     needed = float(arr.max() * margin + burst_steps)     if needed > readout_steps:         raise ValueError(             f"readout window too short: the probe needs ~{needed:.0f} steps to reach the farthest "             f"sensor (+burst), but only {readout_steps} steps are recorded"         )     return {"max_arrival": float(arr.max()), "min_arrival": float(arr.min()),             "needed_steps": needed, "window": readout_steps} ``` #### `wavebrain/medium.py` ```python """2-D nonlinear wave medium with a plastic "hologram" memory. Scheme (dt = dx = 1), for the fast field u with gamma = damping:     u^{n+1} = [ 2u^n - (1 - g/2) u^{n-1} + c2 * lap(u^n) - beta * (u^n)^3 + F^n ] / (1 + g/2)     with g = damping + gamma_layer(x) * c2 is clipped to [c2_min, c2_max] so it can never go negative or exceed the   explicit-scheme limit (original code: c2 ranged from -0.44 to 1.06). * The cubic term is *restoring* (beta >= 0).  The original term had a constant   +18.5 outward push for |u| > ~25, which made a single blob invade the whole   domain as a uniform plateau. * Sources are forcing terms inside the update, not state overwrites. * The outer ``sponge_width`` cells form a *viscous* absorbing layer: a smoothly   ramped friction coefficient gamma(x) inside the update.  (The original used a   multiplicative sponge, which acts partly as a spring to zero: it reflected   15-26% of the energy at low frequency and got *worse* when made stronger.)   This is a damping layer, not a true PML.  Sensors must stay outside it. """ from __future__ import annotations import copy import numpy as np from .config import MediumConfig from .sensors import SensorArray from .sources import Source class WaveMedium:     def __init__(self, cfg: MediumConfig | None = None):         self.cfg = cfg or MediumConfig()         c = self.cfg         dt = np.dtype(c.dtype)         shape = (c.ny, c.nx)         self._f = np.zeros((3,) + shape, dtype=dt)   # fast field ring buffer         self._s = np.zeros((3,) + shape, dtype=dt)   # slow field ring buffer         self._k = 0                                   # prev=k%3, cur=(k+1)%3, next=(k+2)%3         self.H = np.zeros(shape, dtype=dt)            # hologram         self.t = 0                                    # absolute step counter         self.plastic = False                          # hologram write enabled?         self.sources: list[Source] = []         self.clip_events = 0         # Viscous absorbing layer: total friction = bulk damping + layer profile gamma_layer(x).         layer = self._build_layer()         self.gamma_map = (c.damping + layer).astype(dt)         self.gamma_map_slow = (c.damping_slow + layer).astype(dt)         self._a, self._b = 1.0 - 0.5 * self.gamma_map, 1.0 + 0.5 * self.gamma_map         self._a_s, self._b_s = 1.0 - 0.5 * self.gamma_map_slow, 1.0 + 0.5 * self.gamma_map_slow     # ----------------------------------------------------------- state access     @property     def u(self) -> np.ndarray:         """Current fast field (state n)."""         return self._f[(self._k + 1) % 3]     @property     def u_prev(self) -> np.ndarray:         return self._f[self._k % 3]     @property     def s(self) -> np.ndarray:         """Current slow field."""         return self._s[(self._k + 1) % 3]     def clone(self) -> "WaveMedium":         """Independent deep copy (arrays, sources, hologram, clock)."""         return copy.deepcopy(self)     # -------------------------------------------------------------- utilities     def _build_layer(self) -> np.ndarray:         """Friction coefficient gamma_layer(y, x): 0 inside, ramps up (power law) to         ``sponge_strength`` at the outer edge over ``sponge_width`` cells."""         c = self.cfg         w = c.sponge_width         def profile(n: int) -> np.ndarray:             sig = np.zeros(n)             if w > 0 and c.sponge_strength > 0:                 ramp = c.sponge_strength * ((w - np.arange(w)) / w) ** c.sponge_power                 sig[:w] = ramp                 sig[-w:] = np.maximum(sig[-w:], ramp[::-1])             return sig         sy, sx = profile(c.ny), profile(c.nx)         return np.maximum(sy[:, None], sx[None, :])     def add_source(self, src: Source) -> Source:         src.check_inside(self.cfg.ny, self.cfg.nx)         self.sources.append(src)         return src     def remove_source(self, src: Source) -> None:         self.sources = [s for s in self.sources if s is not src]     def _c2(self, s_cur: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:         c = self.cfg         mod = 1.0 + c.slow_mod * s_cur[1:-1, 1:-1] + c.holo_mod * self.H[1:-1, 1:-1]         return np.clip(c.c2_nominal * mod, c.c2_min, c.c2_max)     # ------------------------------------------------------------------- step     def step(self) -> None:         c = self.cfg         ip, ic, inx = self._k % 3, (self._k + 1) % 3, (self._k + 2) % 3         fp, fc, fn = self._f[ip], self._f[ic], self._f[inx]         sp, sc, sn = self._s[ip], self._s[ic], self._s[inx]         I = (slice(1, -1), slice(1, -1))         lap_f = fc[:-2, 1:-1] + fc[2:, 1:-1] + fc[1:-1, :-2] + fc[1:-1, 2:] - 4.0 * fc[I]         lap_s = sc[:-2, 1:-1] + sc[2:, 1:-1] + sc[1:-1, :-2] + sc[1:-1, 2:] - 4.0 * sc[I]         # fast field         cur = fc[I]         fn[I] = (2.0 * cur - self._a[I] * fp[I] + self._c2(sc) * lap_f                  - c.beta * (cur * cur * cur)) / self._b[I]         # forcing         for src in self.sources:             amp = src.envelope(self.t)             if amp != 0.0:                 region = (slice(src.y0, src.y1), slice(src.x0, src.x1))                 fn[region] += (src.profile * amp / self._b[region]).astype(fn.dtype)         # slow field (driven by the fast field's curvature)         sn[I] = (2.0 * sc[I] - self._a_s[I] * sp[I] + (c.c_slow ** 2) * lap_s                  + c.slow_drive * lap_f) / self._b_s[I]         # safety net: must stay at zero in nominal operation (tests assert this)         m = float(np.abs(fn).max())         if not np.isfinite(m):             raise FloatingPointError(f"non-finite field at step {self.t}")         if m > c.u_limit:             self.clip_events += 1             np.clip(fn, -c.u_limit, c.u_limit, out=fn)         # hologram: leaky, saturating accumulation of u^{n+1} * u^n / |u|         if self.plastic:             prod = fn * fc             norm = np.sqrt(fn * fn + fc * fc + 1e-8)             h = c.holo_decay * self.H + c.holo_rate * prod / norm             self.H = (c.holo_sat * np.tanh(h / c.holo_sat)).astype(self.H.dtype)         else:             self.H *= c.holo_decay         self.t += 1         self._k += 1     def run(self, n: int, sensors: SensorArray | None = None) -> np.ndarray | None:         """Advance ``n`` steps; if ``sensors`` is given return the (n_sensors, n) record."""         if sensors is None:             for _ in range(n):                 self.step()             return None         sensors.validate(self.cfg)         out = np.empty((len(sensors), n), dtype=self.u.dtype)         for i in range(n):             self.step()             out[:, i] = self.u[sensors.rows, sensors.cols]         return out     # ------------------------------------------------------------ diagnostics     def staggered_energy(self) -> float:         """Exactly conserved energy of the *linear, homogeneous, undamped* scheme.         E = 1/2 |u^{n+1} - u^n|^2 - 1/2 c^2 <u^{n+1}, lap(u^n)>.         Only meaningful when gamma = beta = 0, mods = 0, no sponge, no sources.         """         cur, prev = self.u.astype(float), self.u_prev.astype(float)         lap_prev = (prev[:-2, 1:-1] + prev[2:, 1:-1] + prev[1:-1, :-2] + prev[1:-1, 2:]                     - 4.0 * prev[1:-1, 1:-1])         kinetic = 0.5 * np.sum((cur - prev) ** 2)         potential = -0.5 * self.cfg.c2_nominal * np.sum(cur[1:-1, 1:-1] * lap_prev)         return float(kinetic + potential)     def stability_report(self) -> dict:         c = self.cfg         c2 = self._c2(self.s)         umax = float(np.abs(self.u).max())         return {             "finite": bool(np.isfinite(self.u).all() and np.isfinite(self.s).all()),             "max_abs_u": umax,             "c2_min_now": float(c2.min()),             "c2_max_now": float(c2.max()),             "stiffness_bound": 8.0 * float(c2.max()) + 3.0 * c.beta * umax ** 2,             "clip_events": self.clip_events,             "config_stability_number": c.stability_number,         } ``` #### `wavebrain/classifier.py` ```python """Linear readout: ridge classifier, stratified CV, permutation test, Wilson CI. Pure numpy.  A linear readout is the standard choice for reservoir computing and, unlike the original 57k-parameter MLP trained on a handful of samples, cannot silently memorise a tiny dataset (alpha is chosen by closed-form leave-one-out error). """ from __future__ import annotations import numpy as np class RidgeClassifier:     """One-vs-rest ridge regression on +/-1 targets (dual form, LOO-selected alpha)."""     def __init__(self, alphas=None, scale: str = "global"):         self.alphas = np.logspace(-3, 5, 17) if alphas is None else np.asarray(alphas, float)         if scale not in ("global", "feature"):             raise ValueError("scale must be 'global' or 'feature'")         self.scale = scale     def fit(self, X, y) -> "RidgeClassifier":         X = np.asarray(X, dtype=float)         y = np.asarray(y)         n = len(y)         if X.ndim != 2 or X.shape[0] != n:             raise ValueError("X must be 2-D with one row per label")         self.classes_ = np.unique(y)         if len(self.classes_) < 2 or n < 4:             raise ValueError("need >= 2 classes and >= 4 samples")         idx = np.searchsorted(self.classes_, y)         self.mean_ = X.mean(axis=0)         Xc = X - self.mean_         self.scale_ = (Xc.std(axis=0) + 1e-12) if self.scale == "feature" else (Xc.std() + 1e-12)         Xs = Xc / self.scale_         Y = -np.ones((n, len(self.classes_)))         Y[np.arange(n), idx] = 1.0         self.y_mean_ = Y.mean(axis=0)         Yc = Y - self.y_mean_         evals, V = np.linalg.eigh(Xs @ Xs.T)         evals = np.clip(evals, 0.0, None)         VtY = V.T @ Yc         best_err, best_alpha = np.inf, self.alphas[0]         self.loo_errors_ = []         for a in self.alphas:             d = evals / (evals + a)             fitted = V @ (d[:, None] * VtY)             h = (V ** 2) @ d             loo = (Yc - fitted) / np.maximum(1.0 - h, 1e-8)[:, None]             err = float(np.mean(loo ** 2))             self.loo_errors_.append(err)             if err < best_err:                 best_err, best_alpha = err, a         self.alpha_ = float(best_alpha)         dual = V @ (VtY / (evals + self.alpha_)[:, None])         self.coef_ = Xs.T @ dual         return self     def decision_function(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         Xs = (np.asarray(X, dtype=float) - self.mean_) / self.scale_         return Xs @ self.coef_ + self.y_mean_     def predict(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         return self.classes_[np.argmax(self.decision_function(X), axis=1)]     def score(self, X, y) -> float:         return float(np.mean(self.predict(X) == np.asarray(y))) def stratified_folds(y, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0) -> list[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]]:     """(train_idx, test_idx) pairs; every sample is tested exactly once."""     y = np.asarray(y)     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     parts: list[list[np.ndarray]] = [[] for _ in range(k)]     for cls in np.unique(y):         members = rng.permutation(np.flatnonzero(y == cls))         for i, chunk in enumerate(np.array_split(members, k)):             parts[i].append(chunk)     tests = [np.concatenate(p) for p in parts]     everything = np.arange(len(y))     return [(np.setdiff1d(everything, t), t) for t in tests] def cross_val_predict(X, y, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0, make=RidgeClassifier) -> np.ndarray:     X, y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y)     pred = np.empty_like(y)     for tr, te in stratified_folds(y, k, seed):         pred[te] = make().fit(X[tr], y[tr]).predict(X[te])     return pred def cross_val_accuracy(X, y, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0, make=RidgeClassifier) -> float:     return float(np.mean(cross_val_predict(X, y, k, seed, make) == np.asarray(y))) def wilson_interval(successes: int, n: int, z: float = 1.96) -> tuple[float, float]:     """95% Wilson score interval for a binomial proportion."""     if n <= 0:         raise ValueError("n must be positive")     p = successes / n     denom = 1.0 + z * z / n     centre = (p + z * z / (2 * n)) / denom     half = z * np.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) / denom     return float(max(0.0, centre - half)), float(min(1.0, centre + half)) def permutation_test(X, y, observed: float, n_perm: int = 100, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0,                      make=RidgeClassifier) -> tuple[float, np.ndarray]:     """p-value of ``observed`` CV accuracy under shuffled labels, plus the null samples."""     X, y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y)     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     null = np.array([cross_val_accuracy(X, rng.permutation(y), k, seed + 1 + i, make)                      for i in range(n_perm)])     p = (1.0 + float(np.sum(null >= observed - 1e-12))) / (n_perm + 1.0)     return p, null def class_separability(X, y) -> float:     """Mean between-centroid distance / RMS within-class distance (> 1 means separable)."""     X, y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y)     classes = np.unique(y)     cents = np.stack([X[y == c].mean(axis=0) for c in classes])     between = np.mean([np.linalg.norm(cents[i] - cents[j])                        for i in range(len(classes)) for j in range(i + 1, len(classes))])     resid = X - cents[np.searchsorted(classes, y)]     within = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.sum(resid ** 2, axis=1)))     return float(between / within) if within > 0 else float("inf") ``` #### `wavebrain/experiment.py` ```python """Pattern-memory experiment: write -> delay -> probe (with paired baseline) -> read. Protocol per trial ------------------ 1. WRITE   a pattern (circle/cross/ring, equal energy, random +/-jitter placement) is            driven into the medium for ``write_steps`` while the hologram is plastic. 2. DELAY   sources off, plasticity off.  Waves radiate into the absorber; only the            hologram (and a little slow-field activity) still "remembers". 3. PROBE   the medium is cloned twice.  Clone A receives a probe burst, clone B does            not.  The feature is the paired difference A - B at interior sensors, which            removes ring-down and uncontrolled background activity (baseline subtraction). 4. READ    additive sensor noise, block-average downsampling, linear readout. """ from __future__ import annotations import time from dataclasses import dataclass import numpy as np from .config import ExperimentConfig from .medium import WaveMedium from .sensors import SensorArray, check_timing, probe_geometry, ring_layout from .sources import (GaussianBurst, Gated, Sine, Source, line_profile, make_pacemakers,                       make_pattern) RAW_CANVAS = 40  # size of the canvas used for the raw-pixel reference baseline @dataclass class Dataset:     cfg: ExperimentConfig     responses: np.ndarray   # (N, n_sensors, readout_steps) clean paired responses     y: np.ndarray           # (N,) integer class ids     raw: np.ndarray         # (N, RAW_CANVAS**2) raw pattern pixels (reference baseline)     stats: dict     def features(self, noise_std: float | None = None, seed: int = 12345) -> np.ndarray:         """Add sensor noise to raw samples, block-average in time, flatten."""         std = self.cfg.sensor_noise if noise_std is None else noise_std         r = self.responses.astype(float)         if std > 0:             r = r + np.random.default_rng(seed).normal(0.0, std, r.shape)         ds = self.cfg.downsample         t = (r.shape[2] // ds) * ds         r = r[:, :, :t].reshape(r.shape[0], r.shape[1], t // ds, ds).mean(axis=3)         return r.reshape(r.shape[0], -1)     def noise_per_feature(self, noise_std: float | None = None) -> float:         """Std of the sensor noise on one (block-averaged) feature."""         std = self.cfg.sensor_noise if noise_std is None else noise_std         return float(std / np.sqrt(self.cfg.downsample))     def detectability(self, noise_std: float | None = None) -> float:         """Matched-filter d' between classes: mean pairwise centroid distance / noise std.         This is the step-1 sanity statistic.  It is computed on noise-free features and         compared against the sensor-noise floor, so it answers "does the pattern leave a         trace above the sensor noise?".  d' near 0 means no (the original code's readout         carried only ~1e-4 noise regardless of pattern); d' >> 1 means yes.  It ignores         placement jitter, so it is an optimistic upper bound on classifiability.         """         X = self.features(0.0)         classes = np.unique(self.y)         cents = np.stack([X[self.y == c].mean(axis=0) for c in classes])         dists = [np.linalg.norm(cents[i] - cents[j])                  for i in range(len(classes)) for j in range(i + 1, len(classes))]         return float(np.mean(dists) / self.noise_per_feature(noise_std)) def build_sensors(cfg: ExperimentConfig) -> SensorArray:     return ring_layout(cfg.medium, cfg.n_sensors_per_side, sides=cfg.sensor_sides) def validate_experiment(cfg: ExperimentConfig) -> dict:     """Static checks: sensors are in the interior and the probe can reach them in time."""     sensors = build_sensors(cfg)     col, r0, r1 = probe_geometry(cfg.medium)     info = check_timing(cfg.medium, sensors, col, (r0, r1), cfg.readout_steps,                         burst_steps=6.0 * cfg.probe_tau)     half = cfg.pattern_size // 2 + cfg.jitter + 2     m = cfg.medium     if (m.ny // 2 - half < m.sponge_width) or (m.nx // 2 - half < m.sponge_width):         raise ValueError("pattern (with jitter) would overlap the absorbing layer")     return info def _probe_source(cfg: ExperimentConfig, t_now: int) -> Source:     col, r0, r1 = probe_geometry(cfg.medium)     env = GaussianBurst(cfg.probe_amp, cfg.probe_omega, t0=t_now + 3.0 * cfg.probe_tau,                         tau=cfg.probe_tau)     return Source(line_profile(r1 - r0, 2), r0, col, env, "probe") def simulate_trial(cfg: ExperimentConfig, kind: str, rng: np.random.Generator,                    sensors: SensorArray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, dict]:     m = cfg.medium     sim = WaveMedium(m)     if cfg.background_pacemakers > 0:         for src in make_pacemakers(cfg.background_pacemakers, (m.ny, m.nx), m.sponge_width + 2,                                    rng, cfg.background_amp):             sim.add_source(src)     dy, dx = (int(v) for v in rng.integers(-cfg.jitter, cfg.jitter + 1, size=2))     pat = make_pattern(kind, cfg.pattern_size)     y0 = m.ny // 2 - cfg.pattern_size // 2 + dy     x0 = m.nx // 2 - cfg.pattern_size // 2 + dx     # 1. write     write = sim.add_source(Source(pat, y0, x0,                                   Gated(Sine(cfg.write_amp, cfg.write_omega), 0, cfg.write_steps),                                   "write"))     sim.plastic = cfg.use_hologram     sim.run(cfg.write_steps)     sim.remove_source(write)     sim.plastic = False     h_after_write = float(np.abs(sim.H).max())     # 2. delay     sim.run(cfg.delay_steps)     # 3. paired probe / baseline     a, b = sim.clone(), sim.clone()     a.add_source(_probe_source(cfg, a.t))     resp = a.run(cfg.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float) - b.run(cfg.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float)     canvas = np.zeros((RAW_CANVAS, RAW_CANVAS))     ry, rx = RAW_CANVAS // 2 - cfg.pattern_size // 2 + dy, RAW_CANVAS // 2 - cfg.pattern_size // 2 + dx     canvas[ry:ry + cfg.pattern_size, rx:rx + cfg.pattern_size] = pat     info = {"hologram_max": h_after_write,             "clip_events": a.clip_events + b.clip_events + sim.clip_events,             "max_abs_u": max(float(np.abs(a.u).max()), float(np.abs(sim.u).max()))}     return resp, canvas.ravel(), info def build_dataset(cfg: ExperimentConfig, verbose: bool = False) -> Dataset:     validate_experiment(cfg)     sensors = build_sensors(cfg)     n_cls = len(cfg.classes)     order = [(i, c) for i in range(cfg.trials_per_class) for c in range(n_cls)]     resp, raw, ys = [], [], []     clip, hmax = 0, []     t0 = time.time()     for n, (i, c) in enumerate(order):         rng = np.random.default_rng([cfg.seed, c, i])     # reproducible, order-independent         r, canvas, info = simulate_trial(cfg, cfg.classes[c], rng, sensors)         resp.append(r); raw.append(canvas); ys.append(c)         clip += info["clip_events"]; hmax.append(info["hologram_max"])         if verbose and (n + 1) % 15 == 0:             print(f"    trial {n + 1}/{len(order)}  ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     stats = {"clip_events": int(clip), "hologram_max_mean": float(np.mean(hmax)),              "seconds": time.time() - t0,              "response_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(resp))))}     return Dataset(cfg, np.stack(resp), np.array(ys), np.stack(raw), stats) ``` #### `wavebrain/benchmark.py` ```python """Headless benchmark: ``python -m wavebrain.benchmark [--quick]``. Conditions ---------- A  reservoir, hologram ON,  short delay        (headline result) B  reservoir, hologram OFF, short delay        (ablation) C  reservoir, hologram ON,  long delay         (memory retention) D  reservoir, hologram OFF, long delay         (ablation of C) E  reservoir + uncontrolled background pacemakers (baseline subtraction at work) R  raw pattern pixels -> same linear readout   (reference, not a competitor) N  shuffled labels                              (null / chance) """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time from dataclasses import replace import numpy as np from .classifier import (class_separability, cross_val_predict, permutation_test,                          wilson_interval) from .config import ExperimentConfig from .experiment import Dataset, build_dataset, validate_experiment def evaluate(X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, n_perm: int, seed: int, folds: int = 5,              repeats: int = 3) -> dict:     """Repeated stratified CV accuracy + Wilson CI + permutation p-value."""     accs = [float(np.mean(cross_val_predict(X, y, k=folds, seed=seed + 100 * r) == y))             for r in range(repeats)]     acc = float(np.mean(accs))     # Repeats re-use the same samples, so they are NOT independent: the interval uses n = len(y).     lo, hi = wilson_interval(int(round(acc * len(y))), len(y))     p, null = permutation_test(X, y, acc, n_perm=n_perm, k=folds, seed=seed + 7)     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": lo, "ci_high": hi, "p_perm": p,             "null_mean": float(null.mean()), "null_max": float(null.max())} def run_benchmark(trials_per_class: int = 30, seed: int = 0, n_perm: int = 100,                   long_delay: int = 1000, noise_levels=(0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0),                   verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     base = ExperimentConfig(trials_per_class=trials_per_class, seed=seed)     validate_experiment(base)     conds = {         "A  reservoir, hologram ON,  delay 250": base,         "B  ablation, hologram OFF,  delay 250": replace(base, use_hologram=False),         f"C  reservoir, hologram ON,  delay {long_delay}": replace(base, delay_steps=long_delay),         f"D  ablation, hologram OFF,  delay {long_delay}": replace(base, delay_steps=long_delay,                                                                     use_hologram=False),         "E  reservoir + background pacemakers": replace(base, background_pacemakers=3),     }     out: dict = {"config": {"trials_per_class": trials_per_class, "seed": seed,                             "sensor_noise": base.sensor_noise, "n_perm": n_perm},                  "conditions": {}, "noise_sweep": {}, "reference": {}}     datasets: dict[str, Dataset] = {}     for name, cfg in conds.items():         if verbose:             print(f"[build] {name}", flush=True)         ds = build_dataset(cfg, verbose=False)         datasets[name] = ds         res = evaluate(ds.features(), ds.y, n_perm, seed)         res.update({"dprime": ds.detectability(), "clip_events": ds.stats["clip_events"],                     "hologram_max": ds.stats["hologram_max_mean"],                     "seconds": ds.stats["seconds"]})         out["conditions"][name] = res         if verbose:             print(f"        acc={res['acc']:.3f}  95%CI=[{res['ci_low']:.2f},{res['ci_high']:.2f}]  "                   f"p={res['p_perm']:.3f}  d'={res['dprime']:.1f}  ({res['seconds']:.0f}s)", flush=True)     first = next(iter(datasets.values()))     ref = evaluate(first.raw, first.y, n_perm, seed)     out["reference"]["R  raw pattern pixels (linear readout)"] = ref     names = list(datasets)     for label, name in (("A", names[0]), ("B", names[1])):         ds = datasets[name]         sweep = {}         for mult in noise_levels:             std = base.sensor_noise * mult             accs = [float(np.mean(cross_val_predict(ds.features(std, seed=s), ds.y, 5, s) == ds.y))                     for s in range(3)]             sweep[f"{std:.4f}"] = float(np.mean(accs))         out["noise_sweep"][label] = sweep     out["chance"] = 1.0 / len(base.classes)     return out def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     n = res["config"]["trials_per_class"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain benchmark  (trials/class={n}, classes=3, chance={res['chance']:.2f}, "              f"sensor noise={res['config']['sensor_noise']}, permutations={res['config']['n_perm']})",              "", f"{'condition':<46}{'acc':>7}{'95% CI':>15}{'p(perm)':>9}{'d-prime':>9}{'clips':>7}",              "-" * 93]     rows = dict(res["conditions"])     rows.update(res["reference"])     for name, r in rows.items():         dp = f"{r['dprime']:.1f}" if "dprime" in r else "-"         clips = f"{r['clip_events']}" if "clip_events" in r else "-"         lines.append(f"{name:<46}{r['acc']:>7.3f}{'[%.2f, %.2f]' % (r['ci_low'], r['ci_high']):>15}"                      f"{r['p_perm']:>9.3f}{dp:>9}{clips:>7}")     lines += ["", "Accuracy vs sensor-noise std (A = hologram ON, B = hologram OFF, delay 250):",               f"{'noise std':>12}" + "".join(f"{k:>9}" for k in res['noise_sweep']['A'])]     for label in ("A", "B"):         lines.append(f"{label:>12}" + "".join(f"{v:>9.2f}" for v in res["noise_sweep"][label].values()))     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials-per-class", type=int, default=30)     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)     ap.add_argument("--perms", type=int, default=100)     ap.add_argument("--long-delay", type=int, default=1000)     ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="12 trials/class, 30 permutations")     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None, help="write raw results to this JSON file")     args = ap.parse_args(argv)     if args.quick:         args.trials_per_class, args.perms = 12, 30     t0 = time.time()     res = run_benchmark(args.trials_per_class, args.seed, args.perms, args.long_delay)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {time.time() - t0:.0f}s)")     if args.json:         with open(args.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(res, fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/stats.py` ```python """Small statistical helpers for paired comparisons between models.""" from __future__ import annotations import numpy as np def
