paired_bootstrap_diff(a, b, n_boot: int = 4000, seed: int = 0, level: float = 0.95) -> dict:     """Bootstrap CI for mean(a) - mean(b) on the SAME samples (paired resampling).     ``a`` and ``b`` are per-sample scores (e.g. correctness in [0, 1]).  Resampling the     sample indices jointly keeps the pairing, which is far more powerful than comparing     two marginal confidence intervals.     """     a, b = np.asarray(a, float), np.asarray(b, float)     if a.shape != b.shape or a.ndim != 1 or len(a) < 2:         raise ValueError("a and b must be 1-D arrays of equal length >= 2")     if not (0.0 < level < 1.0):         raise ValueError("level must lie in (0, 1)")     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     idx = rng.integers(0, len(a), size=(n_boot, len(a)))     diffs = (a[idx] - b[idx]).mean(axis=1)     alpha = 1.0 - level     lo, hi = np.quantile(diffs, [alpha / 2.0, 1.0 - alpha / 2.0])     return {"diff": float(a.mean() - b.mean()), "lo": float(lo), "hi": float(hi)} ``` #### `wavebrain/pairtask.py` ```python """Two-pattern SAME / DIFFERENT task (the nonlinearity pilot). Two shapes are written simultaneously at two places (one above the other) into the medium.  The label is 1 if the shapes are identical, 0 otherwise.  A linear readout of raw pixels cannot solve this (it is an XOR-like comparison), so any success has to come from what the medium computes. Design choices that keep comparisons between media fair ------------------------------------------------------- * Trial composition is a deterministic, exactly balanced function of the trial index   (half "same", half "different"; every shape / ordered pair equally often). * Placement jitter comes from ``default_rng([seed, trial_index])`` and does not depend   on the medium: every medium sees the identical trials (paired design). * Diagnostic targets are provided as well: which shape is on top, which is at the bottom,   and the joint pair identity.  They separate "the medium remembers" from "the medium   compares". """ from __future__ import annotations import time from dataclasses import dataclass, field import numpy as np from .config import ExperimentConfig, MediumConfig from .experiment import Dataset, _probe_source, build_sensors, validate_experiment from .medium import WaveMedium from .sensors import SensorArray from .sources import PATTERN_KINDS, Gated, Sine, Source, make_pattern RAW_H, RAW_W = 80, 40       # canvas for the raw-pixel reference (two stacked patterns) @dataclass(frozen=True) class PairConfig:     base: ExperimentConfig = field(default_factory=ExperimentConfig)     n_trials: int = 120     separation: int = 34          # rows between the two pattern centres     seed: int = 0     def __post_init__(self) -> None:         k = len(self.base.classes)         block = 2 * k * (k - 1)         if self.n_trials <= 0 or self.n_trials % block:             raise ValueError(f"n_trials must be a positive multiple of {block} for exact balance")         b, m = self.base, self.base.medium         gap = self.separation - b.pattern_size - 2 * b.jitter         if gap < 2:             raise ValueError("patterns (with jitter) would touch: increase separation")         half = b.pattern_size // 2 + b.jitter         top = m.ny // 2 - self.separation // 2         bottom = top + self.separation         margin = m.sponge_width + 1         if top - half < margin or bottom + half >= m.ny - margin:             raise ValueError("patterns would overlap the absorbing layer")         probe_right = m.sponge_width + 3 + 2         if m.nx // 2 - half <= probe_right:             raise ValueError("patterns would overlap the probe line")         if m.nx // 2 - half < margin or m.nx // 2 + half >= m.nx - margin:             raise ValueError("patterns would overlap the absorbing layer")         if 20 - half < 0 or 60 + half > RAW_H or RAW_W // 2 - half < 0 or RAW_W // 2 + half > RAW_W:             raise ValueError("patterns (with jitter) do not fit on the raw-pixel reference canvas")     @property     def centres(self) -> tuple[int, int]:         m = self.base.medium         top = m.ny // 2 - self.separation // 2         return top, top + self.separation def trial_kinds(cfg: PairConfig, index: int) -> tuple[str, str]:     """Deterministic, balanced composition: even index = SAME, odd index = DIFFERENT."""     kinds = cfg.base.classes     if index % 2 == 0:         k = kinds[(index // 2) % len(kinds)]         return k, k     pairs = [(a, b) for a in kinds for b in kinds if a != b]     return pairs[(index // 2) % len(pairs)] def draw_offsets(cfg: PairConfig, index: int) -> np.ndarray:     """(2, 2) integer jitter (dy, dx) for the top and bottom pattern; medium-independent."""     rng = np.random.default_rng([cfg.seed, index])     j = cfg.base.jitter     return rng.integers(-j, j + 1, size=(2, 2)) def raw_canvas(cfg: PairConfig, kinds: tuple[str, str], offsets: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     """Pixel image of the two written patterns (reference baseline, no PDE needed)."""     size = cfg.base.pattern_size     canvas = np.zeros((RAW_H, RAW_W))     for (kind, (dy, dx), cy) in zip(kinds, offsets, (20, 60)):         y0, x0 = cy - size // 2 + int(dy), RAW_W // 2 - size // 2 + int(dx)         canvas[y0:y0 + size, x0:x0 + size] = make_pattern(kind, size)     return canvas.ravel() def _write_phase(cfg: PairConfig, index: int) -> tuple[WaveMedium, dict]:     """Write both patterns; return the medium (plasticity off, writers removed) and stats."""     b, m = cfg.base, cfg.base.medium     kinds, offs = trial_kinds(cfg, index), draw_offsets(cfg, index)     rng = np.random.default_rng([cfg.seed, index, 7])          # only for optional background     sim = WaveMedium(m)     if b.background_pacemakers > 0:         from .sources import make_pacemakers         for src in make_pacemakers(b.background_pacemakers, (m.ny, m.nx), m.sponge_width + 2,                                    rng, b.background_amp):             sim.add_source(src)     writers = []     for kind, (dy, dx), cy in zip(kinds, offs, cfg.centres):         y0 = cy - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dy)         x0 = m.nx // 2 - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dx)         env = Gated(Sine(b.write_amp, b.write_omega), 0, b.write_steps)         writers.append(sim.add_source(Source(make_pattern(kind, b.pattern_size), y0, x0, env,                                              f"write-{kind}")))     sim.plastic = b.use_hologram     peak, rms_acc, rms_n = 0.0, 0.0, 0     for step in range(b.write_steps):         sim.step()         peak = max(peak, float(np.abs(sim.u).max()))         if step >= b.write_steps - 100:             rms_acc += float(np.mean(sim.u.astype(float) ** 2)); rms_n += 1     for w in writers:         sim.remove_source(w)     sim.plastic = False     info = {"peak_u": peak, "write_rms": float(np.sqrt(rms_acc / rms_n)),             "hologram_max": float(np.abs(sim.H).max())}     return sim, info def write_phase_stats(cfg: PairConfig, index: int) -> dict:     """Cheap (write phase only) amplitude statistics, used for drive calibration."""     sim, info = _write_phase(cfg, index)     info["clip_events"] = sim.clip_events     return info def simulate_pair_trial(cfg: PairConfig, index: int, sensors: SensorArray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, dict]:     b = cfg.base     sim, info = _write_phase(cfg, index)     sim.run(b.delay_steps)     a_sim, b_sim = sim.clone(), sim.clone()     a_sim.add_source(_probe_source(b, a_sim.t))     resp = (a_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float)             - b_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float))     info["clip_events"] = sim.clip_events + a_sim.clip_events + b_sim.clip_events     return resp, info @dataclass class PairDataset(Dataset):     """``y`` is SAME(1)/DIFFERENT(0); ``kinds`` holds the class index of each pattern."""     kinds: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=lambda: np.zeros((0, 2), int))     def targets(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:         k = len(self.cfg.classes)         return {"same_diff": self.y, "top_kind": self.kinds[:, 0],                 "bottom_kind": self.kinds[:, 1], "pair_id": self.kinds[:, 0] * k + self.kinds[:, 1]} def labels_and_kinds(cfg: PairConfig) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:     names = list(cfg.base.classes)     kinds = np.array([[names.index(k) for k in trial_kinds(cfg, i)] for i in range(cfg.n_trials)])     return (kinds[:, 0] == kinds[:, 1]).astype(int), kinds def build_raw_only(cfg: PairConfig) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:     """(raw pixels, y, kinds) without running any PDE: cheap, used for baselines and tests."""     y, kinds = labels_and_kinds(cfg)     raw = np.stack([raw_canvas(cfg, trial_kinds(cfg, i), draw_offsets(cfg, i))                     for i in range(cfg.n_trials)])     return raw, y, kinds def build_pair_dataset(cfg: PairConfig, verbose: bool = False) -> PairDataset:     validate_experiment(cfg.base)     sensors = build_sensors(cfg.base)     raw, y, kinds = build_raw_only(cfg)     resp, infos = [], []     t0 = time.time()     for i in range(cfg.n_trials):         r, info = simulate_pair_trial(cfg, i, sensors)         resp.append(r); infos.append(info)         if verbose and (i + 1) % 30 == 0:             print(f"    trial {i + 1}/{cfg.n_trials} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     stats = {"clip_events": int(sum(d["clip_events"] for d in infos)),              "peak_u": float(np.mean([d["peak_u"] for d in infos])),              "write_rms": float(np.mean([d["write_rms"] for d in infos])),              "hologram_max_mean": float(np.mean([d["hologram_max"] for d in infos])),              "seconds": time.time() - t0,              "response_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(resp))))}     return PairDataset(cfg.base, np.stack(resp), y, raw, stats, kinds) ``` #### `wavebrain/pilot.py` ```python """Nonlinearity pilot:  ``python -m wavebrain.pilot [--quick]``. Question -------- Does a *dynamically* nonlinear medium (cubic coefficient beta) help a linear readout solve the two-pattern SAME / DIFFERENT task, compared with an otherwise identical linear medium (beta = 0)? Media (identical in everything except ``beta``; same drive, same trials) ------------------------------------------------------------------------ L   beta = 0     no cubic term D   beta = 0.05  the v6 default,  equal drive to L S   beta = 0.10  strongly nonlinear, equal drive to L Dm  beta = 0.05  drive calibrated so the write-phase RMS field equals L's Sm  beta = 0.10  same, for beta = 0.10 Why the matched arms: a stiffening medium is detuned from the drive, so at equal drive its fields (and hologram) are less than half as strong as L's.  Equal-drive differences would mix "nonlinearity" with "weaker signal".  The matched arms are the primary test. Caveat that shapes the interpretation: L is NOT a linear system.  Reading the memory out requires parametric coupling (the hologram and the slow field modulate c^2), and writing it is a saturating product u^{n+1} u^n.  What differs between L and D/S is only the additional *cubic* nonlinearity of the wave dynamics. The decision rule (``verdict``) was fixed BEFORE the final run; see its docstring. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time from dataclasses import replace import numpy as np from .classifier import cross_val_predict, permutation_test, wilson_interval from .config import ExperimentConfig, MediumConfig from .pairtask import PairConfig, PairDataset, build_pair_dataset, build_raw_only from .stats import paired_bootstrap_diff VARIANTS = {"L": 0.0, "D": 0.05, "S": 0.10, "Dm": 0.05, "Sm": 0.10} VARIANT_LABEL = {"L": "L  no cubic term (beta=0)", "D": "D  beta=0.05, equal drive",                  "S": "S  beta=0.10, equal drive", "Dm": "Dm beta=0.05, matched amp.",                  "Sm": "Sm beta=0.10, matched amp."} MATCHED = ("Dm", "Sm")     # drive calibrated so write-phase RMS equals the linear medium's CAL_SEED = 9999            # calibration trials never overlap with evaluation seeds BASE_AMP = 0.02            # v6 write / probe amplitude; multiplied by ``scale`` DEFAULT_SCALE = 1.5        # common drive; chosen so the beta=0 control never clips (see tests) TARGET_NAMES = ("same_diff", "top_kind", "bottom_kind", "pair_id") def variant_medium(name: str) -> MediumConfig:     """All variants share every parameter except ``beta`` (a controlled comparison)."""     if name not in VARIANTS:         raise ValueError(f"unknown variant '{name}'")     return replace(MediumConfig(), beta=VARIANTS[name], c2_max=0.25, u_limit=2.4) def pilot_config(name: str, seed: int = 0, n_trials: int = 120,                  scale: float = DEFAULT_SCALE) -> PairConfig:     base = ExperimentConfig(medium=variant_medium(name), write_amp=BASE_AMP * scale,                             probe_amp=BASE_AMP * scale, seed=seed)     return PairConfig(base=base, n_trials=n_trials, seed=seed) # ----------------------------------------------------------------- calibration def mean_write_rms(name: str, scale: float, n_cal: int = 4, seed: int = CAL_SEED) -> float:     from .pairtask import write_phase_stats     cfg = pilot_config(name, seed, 12, scale)     return float(np.mean([write_phase_stats(cfg, i)["write_rms"] for i in range(n_cal)])) def calibrate_scale(name: str, base_scale: float = DEFAULT_SCALE, n_cal: int = 4,                     max_iter: int = 14, tol: float = 0.02, bounds=(0.5, 3.0)) -> dict:     """Find the drive scale for which ``name`` reaches the linear medium's write RMS.     Bracketed bisection in log-scale.  A simple fixed-point iteration overshoots because     a stiffening medium is detuned from the drive and its response curve is steep (and     can jump); bisection only needs the response to be monotone in the drive.     Calibration uses its own seed and never touches evaluation trials.     """     target = mean_write_rms("L", base_scale, n_cal)     lo, hi = bounds[0] * base_scale, bounds[1] * base_scale     r_lo, r_hi = mean_write_rms(name, lo, n_cal), mean_write_rms(name, hi, n_cal)     if not (r_lo <= target <= r_hi):         raise ValueError(f"cannot bracket the target write RMS {target:.3f} for '{name}' "                          f"(RMS at bounds: {r_lo:.3f}, {r_hi:.3f})")     scale, achieved = lo, r_lo     for _ in range(max_iter):         scale = float(np.sqrt(lo * hi))         achieved = mean_write_rms(name, scale, n_cal)         if abs(achieved - target) / target < tol:             break         if achieved < target:             lo = scale         else:             hi = scale     return {"scale": scale, "target_rms": target, "achieved_rms": achieved,             "rel_error": abs(achieved - target) / target} # ----------------------------------------------------------------- evaluation def effective_folds(y, folds: int) -> int:     """CV folds actually usable: at most the size of the smallest class (and >= 2)."""     counts = np.unique(np.asarray(y), return_counts=True)[1]     k = int(min(folds, counts.min()))     if k < 2:         raise ValueError("every class needs at least 2 samples for cross-validation "                          f"(smallest class has {int(counts.min())}); use more trials")     return k def evaluate_target(X, y, seed: int, n_perm: int, folds: int = 5, repeats: int = 3) -> dict:     """Repeated stratified CV + Wilson CI (n = len(y)) + permutation p-value."""     y = np.asarray(y)     k = effective_folds(y, folds)     correct = np.mean([cross_val_predict(X, y, k=k, seed=seed + 100 * r) == y                        for r in range(repeats)], axis=0)     acc = float(correct.mean())     lo, hi = wilson_interval(int(round(acc * len(y))), len(y))     p, null = permutation_test(X, y, acc, n_perm=n_perm, k=k, seed=seed + 7)     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": lo, "ci_high": hi, "p_perm": p,             "null_mean": float(null.mean()), "n_classes": int(len(np.unique(y))),             "folds": k, "_correct": correct} def composite_same_diff(X, kinds: np.ndarray, y_same: np.ndarray, n_kinds: int,                         seed: int, folds: int = 5, repeats: int = 3) -> dict:     """Decode the joint pair identity linearly, then compare the two decoded shapes.     The comparison step is an explicit, non-linear post-processing outside the medium.     A high value with a low direct ``same_diff`` accuracy means the medium REMEMBERS the     shapes but does not COMPARE them.     """     pair = kinds[:, 0] * n_kinds + kinds[:, 1]     k = effective_folds(pair, folds)     hits = []     for r in range(repeats):         pred = cross_val_predict(X, pair, k=k, seed=seed + 100 * r)         hits.append(((pred // n_kinds) == (pred % n_kinds)).astype(int) == y_same)     correct = np.mean(hits, axis=0)     acc = float(correct.mean())     lo, hi = wilson_interval(int(round(acc * len(y_same))), len(y_same))     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": lo, "ci_high": hi} def evaluate_dataset(ds: PairDataset, seed: int, n_perm: int) -> dict:     X = ds.features()     out = {t: evaluate_target(X, y, seed, n_perm) for t, y in ds.targets().items()}     out["composite"] = composite_same_diff(X, ds.kinds, ds.y, len(ds.cfg.classes), seed)     out["stats"] = {k: float(v) for k, v in ds.stats.items()}     out["detectability"] = float(ds.detectability())     return out # -------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def verdict(res: dict, alpha: float = 0.05) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the final run).     ``res["media"][m]["same_diff"]`` has ``p_perm``; ``res["paired"]["D-L"|"S-L"]`` has the     paired-bootstrap ``lo``/``hi`` (95% CI of the accuracy difference).  Rules, in order:     0. INVALID_CLIPPED  the safety clip fired anywhere (a hard nonlinearity) -> rerun.     1. TOO_HARD     no medium beats chance on same/different (all p >= alpha).                     Diagnosis: shapes decodable (top/bottom kind p < alpha) -> the medium                     remembers but does not compare; otherwise memory/readout is too weak.     2. PROMISING    a nonlinear medium is above chance AND beats L with the lower end of the                     paired 95% CI > 0.   -> run the full sweep.     3. HURTS        a nonlinear medium is worse than L with the upper end of the CI < 0.     4. NO_BENEFIT   L is above chance and no CI excludes 0 -> the task is solvable without                     dynamical nonlinearity.     "Nonlinear medium" means the amplitude-matched arms Dm / Sm when present (they hold the     internal field strength fixed, so the comparison is not confounded by SNR); otherwise D / S.     5. INCONCLUSIVE otherwise.     """     media = res["media"]     above = {m: media[m]["same_diff"]["p_perm"] < alpha for m in media}     paired = res["paired"]     clipped = [m for m in media if media[m]["stats"]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED",                 "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}; the clip is a hard "                         f"nonlinearity and contaminates the comparison.",                 "next": "Lower the common drive scale and rerun."}     primary = [m for m in MATCHED if m in media] or [m for m in ("D", "S") if m in media]     if "L" not in media or not primary:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE",                 "text": "The comparison needs the L control and at least one nonlinear arm.",                 "next": "Run with media including L and D/S (or Dm/Sm)."}     if not any(above.values()):         decodable = any(media[m][t]["p_perm"] < alpha for m in media                         for t in ("top_kind", "bottom_kind"))         if decodable:             return {"outcome": "TOO_HARD_REMEMBERS_NOT_COMPARES",                     "text": "No medium solves same/different, but the individual shapes are "                             "decodable: the medium remembers shapes yet a linear readout cannot "                             "compare them.",                     "next": "Make the comparison easier before sweeping: more sensors, a "                             "longer write phase, or a sequential-write variant."}         return {"outcome": "TOO_HARD_WEAK_MEMORY",                 "text": "No medium solves same/different and the individual shapes are not "                         "decodable either: memory or readout is too weak for this task.",                 "next": "Strengthen memory/readout (drive, sensors, noise) before sweeping."}     better = [m for m in primary if above[m] and paired[f"{m}-L"]["lo"] > 0]     if better:         return {"outcome": "PROMISING",                 "text": f"Nonlinear medium {', '.join(better)} beats the linear control "                         f"(paired 95% CI excludes 0) and is above chance.",                 "next": "Run the full sweep (beta x amplitude, 5+ seeds)."}     worse = [m for m in primary if paired[f"{m}-L"]["hi"] < 0]     if worse:         return {"outcome": "HURTS",                 "text": f"Nonlinear medium {', '.join(worse)} is worse than the linear control.",                 "next": "Treat dynamical nonlinearity as a cost; explore weaker beta."}     if above["L"] and not any(paired[f"{m}-L"]["lo"] > 0 or paired[f"{m}-L"]["hi"] < 0                               for m in primary):         return {"outcome": "NO_BENEFIT",                 "text": "The no-cubic-term control already solves the task and no "                         "nonlinear medium differs from it.",                 "next": "The memory (incl. its quadratic write) suffices here; use a harder "                         "task or reframe the project around the memory mechanism."}     return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE",             "text": "Some media are above chance but no comparison meets the criteria.",             "next": "Increase trials (and seeds) before drawing a conclusion."} # ------------------------------------------------------------------- the pilot def run_pilot(n_trials: int = 120, seed: int = 0, scale: float = DEFAULT_SCALE,               n_perm: int = 100, media=("L", "D", "S"), verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     res: dict = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seed": seed, "scale": scale, "n_perm": n_perm},                  "media": {}, "paired": {}, "reference": {}}     t0 = time.time()     first_cfg = pilot_config(media[0], seed, n_trials, scale)     raw, y, kinds = build_raw_only(first_cfg)     k = len(first_cfg.base.classes)     ref_targets = {"same_diff": y, "top_kind": kinds[:, 0], "pair_id": kinds[:, 0] * k + kinds[:, 1]}     res["reference"] = {t: evaluate_target(raw, v, seed, n_perm) for t, v in ref_targets.items()}     res["scales"] = {}     for name in media:         if name in MATCHED:             cal = calibrate_scale(name, scale)             res["scales"][name] = cal["scale"]; res.setdefault("calibration", {})[name] = cal             if verbose:                 print(f"[calibrate] {name}: drive scale {cal['scale']:.3f} "                       f"(write-RMS error {100 * cal['rel_error']:.1f}%)", flush=True)         else:             res["scales"][name] = scale         if verbose:             print(f"[build] {VARIANT_LABEL[name]}", flush=True)         ds = build_pair_dataset(pilot_config(name, seed, n_trials, res["scales"][name]))         res["media"][name] = evaluate_dataset(ds, seed, n_perm)         if verbose:             r = res["media"][name]             print(f"        same/diff acc={r['same_diff']['acc']:.3f} p={r['same_diff']['p_perm']:.3f} | "                   f"top-kind acc={r['top_kind']['acc']:.3f} | pair-id acc={r['pair_id']['acc']:.3f} "                   f"| peak|u|={r['stats']['peak_u']:.2f} clips={int(r['stats']['clip_events'])} "                   f"({r['stats']['seconds']:.0f}s)", flush=True)     if "L" in res["media"]:         for m in ("D", "S", "Dm", "Sm"):             if m in res["media"]:                 res["paired"][f"{m}-L"] = paired_bootstrap_diff(                     res["media"][m]["same_diff"]["_correct"], res["media"]["L"]["same_diff"]["_correct"],                     seed=seed)         if "D" in res["media"] and "S" in res["media"]:             res["paired"]["S-D"] = paired_bootstrap_diff(                 res["media"]["S"]["same_diff"]["_correct"], res["media"]["D"]["same_diff"]["_correct"],                 seed=seed)     res["verdict"] = verdict(res)     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def strip_private(obj):     """Recursively drop numpy payloads (keys starting with '_') so the result is JSON-safe."""     if isinstance(obj, dict):         return {k: strip_private(v) for k, v in obj.items() if not str(k).startswith("_")}     return obj def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain nonlinearity pilot  (trials={c['n_trials']}, seed={c['seed']}, "              f"drive scale={c['scale']}, permutations={c['n_perm']})", "",              "Accuracy [95% CI] (permutation p).  Chance: same/diff 0.50, kind 0.33, pair ~0.12", "",              f"{'medium':<28}{'same/diff':>22}{'top kind':>10}{'bottom':>9}{'pair-id':>9}"              f"{'compose':>9}{'peak|u|':>9}{'wrms':>7}{'drive':>7}{'clips':>7}", "-" * 119]     for m, r in res["media"].items():         sd = r["same_diff"]         cell = f"{sd['acc']:.2f} [{sd['ci_low']:.2f},{sd['ci_high']:.2f}] p={sd['p_perm']:.2f}"         lines.append(f"{VARIANT_LABEL[m]:<28}{cell:>22}{r['top_kind']['acc']:>10.2f}"                      f"{r['bottom_kind']['acc']:>9.2f}{r['pair_id']['acc']:>9.2f}"                      f"{r['composite']['acc']:>9.2f}{r['stats']['peak_u']:>9.2f}"                      f"{r['stats']['write_rms']:>7.2f}{res['scales'][m]:>7.2f}"                      f"{int(r['stats']['clip_events']):>7}")     ref = res["reference"]     cell = f"{ref['same_diff']['acc']:.2f} [{ref['same_diff']['ci_low']:.2f},{ref['same_diff']['ci_high']:.2f}] p={ref['same_diff']['p_perm']:.2f}"     lines.append(f"{'raw pixels, linear readout':<28}{cell:>22}{ref['top_kind']['acc']:>10.2f}"                  f"{'-':>9}{ref['pair_id']['acc']:>9.2f}{'-':>9}{'-':>9}{'-':>7}{'-':>7}{'-':>7}")     lines += ["", "Paired accuracy differences on same/diff (bootstrap 95% CI):"]     for k, d in res["paired"].items():         lines.append(f"  {k:<5} {d['diff']:+.3f}  [{d['lo']:+.3f}, {d['hi']:+.3f}]")     v = res["verdict"]     lines += ["", f"VERDICT: {v['outcome']}", f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=120, help="multiple of 12")     ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)     ap.add_argument("--scale", type=float, default=DEFAULT_SCALE)     ap.add_argument("--media", default="L,D,S,Dm,Sm", help="comma-separated subset")     ap.add_argument("--perms", type=int, default=100)     ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="48 trials, 20 permutations")     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     if a.quick:         a.trials, a.perms = 48, 20     res = run_pilot(a.trials, a.seed, a.scale, a.perms, tuple(a.media.split(",")))     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(res), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/readouts.py` ```python """Nonlinear readouts (pure numpy) and a learning-curve helper. All readouts share the ``fit / decision_function / predict / score`` interface of ``RidgeClassifier`` so they plug into ``cross_val_predict`` and ``permutation_test``. Model selection never sees the outer test fold: hyper-parameters (kernel width, ridge alpha) are chosen INSIDE ``fit`` by the closed-form leave-one-out error of the training samples only, which is a nested cross-validation by construction. Targets are +/-1 one-vs-rest with the training mean removed (same convention as v6), so with the ``linear`` kernel ``KernelRidgeClassifier`` reproduces ``RidgeClassifier``. """ from __future__ import annotations import numpy as np from .classifier import RidgeClassifier, stratified_folds DEFAULT_ALPHAS = np.logspace(-3, 5, 17) DEFAULT_GAMMAS = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0)      # RBF widths, relative to the median squared distance READOUTS = ("linear", "rbf", "poly2", "quadpca") NONLINEAR_READOUTS = ("rbf", "poly2", "quadpca") # ------------------------------------------------------------------- kernels def squared_distances(A: np.ndarray, B: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     d2 = (A ** 2).sum(1)[:, None] + (B ** 2).sum(1)[None, :] - 2.0 * A @ B.T     return np.maximum(d2, 0.0) def rbf_kernel(d2: np.ndarray, gamma: float, median_d2: float) -> np.ndarray:     """K = exp(-gamma * d2 / median_d2): ``gamma`` is relative to the typical distance."""     return np.exp(-gamma * d2 / median_d2) def poly2_kernel(G: np.ndarray, scale: float) -> np.ndarray:     """K = (1 + <x, y> / scale)^2 -- all products of degree <= 2, including the linear terms."""     return (1.0 + G / scale) ** 2 def poly2_feature_map(X: np.ndarray, scale: float) -> np.ndarray:     """Explicit feature map of ``poly2_kernel`` (used by tests to validate the kernel)."""     Z = X / np.sqrt(scale)     n, d = Z.shape     iu = np.triu_indices(d, k=1)     cross = np.sqrt(2.0) * (Z[:, :, None] * Z[:, None, :])[:, iu[0], iu[1]]     return np.hstack([np.ones((n, 1)), np.sqrt(2.0) * Z, Z ** 2, cross]) def loo_residuals(K: np.ndarray, Yc: np.ndarray, alpha: float, eig=None) -> np.ndarray:     """Closed-form leave-one-out residuals of kernel ridge: (y - yhat) / (1 - H_ii)."""     evals, V = eig if eig is not None else np.linalg.eigh(K)     evals = np.clip(evals, 0.0, None)     d = evals / (evals + alpha)     fitted = V @ (d[:, None] * (V.T @ Yc))     h = (V ** 2) @ d     return (Yc - fitted) / np.maximum(1.0 - h, 1e-8)[:, None] # --------------------------------------------------------- kernel ridge classifier class KernelRidgeClassifier:     """One-vs-rest kernel ridge with LOO-selected (kernel width, alpha)."""     def __init__(self, kernel: str = "rbf", alphas=None, gammas=DEFAULT_GAMMAS):         if kernel not in ("linear", "rbf", "poly2"):             raise ValueError("kernel must be 'linear', 'rbf' or 'poly2'")         self.kernel = kernel         self.alphas = DEFAULT_ALPHAS if alphas is None else np.asarray(alphas, float)         self.gammas = tuple(gammas)     def _train_kernels(self, Xs):         """Yield (param, K) candidates for the training data."""         G = Xs @ Xs.T         if self.kernel == "linear":             yield None, G         elif self.kernel == "poly2":             self.poly_scale_ = float(np.mean(np.diag(G))) + 1e-12             yield None, poly2_kernel(G, self.poly_scale_)         else:             d2 = squared_distances(Xs, Xs)             off = d2[~np.eye(len(Xs), dtype=bool)]             self.median_d2_ = float(np.median(off)) + 1e-12             for g in self.gammas:                 yield g, rbf_kernel(d2, g, self.median_d2_)     def fit(self, X, y) -> "KernelRidgeClassifier":         X, y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y)         n = len(y)         if X.ndim != 2 or X.shape[0] != n:             raise ValueError("X must be 2-D with one row per label")         self.classes_ = np.unique(y)         if len(self.classes_) < 2 or n < 4:             raise ValueError("need >= 2 classes and >= 4 samples")         self.mean_ = X.mean(axis=0)         Xc = X - self.mean_         self.scale_ = Xc.std() + 1e-12         Xs = Xc / self.scale_         idx = np.searchsorted(self.classes_, y)         Y = -np.ones((n, len(self.classes_)))         Y[np.arange(n), idx] = 1.0         self.y_mean_ = Y.mean(axis=0)         Yc = Y - self.y_mean_         best = (np.inf, None, None, None)         for param, K in self._train_kernels(Xs):             eig = np.linalg.eigh(K)             for a in self.alphas:                 err = float(np.mean(loo_residuals(K, Yc, a, eig) ** 2))                 if err < best[0]:                     best = (err, param, float(a), (K, eig))         self.loo_error_, self.param_, self.alpha_, (K, (evals, V)) = best         evals = np.clip(evals, 0.0, None)         self.dual_ = V @ ((V.T @ Yc) / (evals + self.alpha_)[:, None])         self.Xs_ = Xs         return self     def decision_function(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         Xs = (np.asarray(X, float) - self.mean_) / self.scale_         G = Xs @ self.Xs_.T         if self.kernel == "linear":             Kt = G         elif self.kernel == "poly2":             Kt = poly2_kernel(G, self.poly_scale_)         else:             Kt = rbf_kernel(squared_distances(Xs, self.Xs_), self.param_, self.median_d2_)         return Kt @ self.dual_ + self.y_mean_     def predict(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         return self.classes_[np.argmax(self.decision_function(X), axis=1)]     def score(self, X, y) -> float:         return float(np.mean(self.predict(X) == np.asarray(y))) # --------------------------------------------- explicit pairwise-product features class QuadraticPCARidge:     """PCA (fitted on the training data only) -> all pairwise products -> ridge.     With m components the feature vector is [z_i] + [z_i z_j, i <= j]: m + m(m+1)/2 terms.     """     def __init__(self, n_components: int = 30, alphas=None):         if n_components < 2:             raise ValueError("n_components must be >= 2")         self.n_components = n_components         self.alphas = alphas     def _features(self, Z: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:         iu = np.triu_indices(Z.shape[1])         quad = (Z[:, :, None] * Z[:, None, :])[:, iu[0], iu[1]]         return np.hstack([Z, quad])     def transform(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         """Project onto the TRAINING components (never refits)."""         Z = (np.asarray(X, float) - self.mean_) @ self.components_.T         return self._features(Z / self.z_std_)     def fit(self, X, y) -> "QuadraticPCARidge":         X = np.asarray(X, float)         self.mean_ = X.mean(axis=0)         m = int(min(self.n_components, X.shape[0] - 1, X.shape[1]))         _, _, Vt = np.linalg.svd(X - self.mean_, full_matrices=False)         self.components_ = Vt[:m]         Z = (X - self.mean_) @ self.components_.T         self.z_std_ = Z.std(axis=0) + 1e-12         self.ridge_ = RidgeClassifier(alphas=self.alphas, scale="feature").fit(self._features(Z / self.z_std_), y)         self.classes_ = self.ridge_.classes_         return self     def decision_function(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         return self.ridge_.decision_function(self.transform(X))     def predict(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         return self.ridge_.predict(self.transform(X))     def score(self, X, y) -> float:         return float(np.mean(self.predict(X) == np.asarray(y))) def make_readout(name: str):     """Zero-argument factory (the ``make`` argument of ``cross_val_predict``)."""     if name == "linear":         return RidgeClassifier     if name == "rbf":         return lambda: KernelRidgeClassifier("rbf")     if name == "poly2":         return lambda: KernelRidgeClassifier("poly2")     if name == "quadpca":         return QuadraticPCARidge     raise ValueError(f"unknown readout '{name}'") # ------------------------------------------------------------- learning curve def stratified_subsample(y, size: int, rng: np.random.Generator, min_per_class: int = 2) -> np.ndarray:     """Indices of a class-proportional subsample of exactly ``size`` items (>= 2 per class)."""     y = np.asarray(y)     classes, counts = np.unique(y, return_counts=True)     if size > len(y):         raise ValueError("size exceeds the number of samples")     if size < min_per_class * len(classes):         raise ValueError("size too small for min_per_class")     quota = np.maximum(min_per_class, np.floor(size * counts / counts.sum()).astype(int))     quota = np.minimum(quota, counts)     while quota.sum() < size:                                  # distribute the remainder         room = counts - quota         quota[int(np.argmax(room))] += 1     while quota.sum() > size:                                  # (only after the floor of min)         quota[int(np.argmax(quota))] -= 1     picks = [rng.choice(np.flatnonzero(y == c), size=q, replace=False) for c, q in zip(classes, quota)]     return np.sort(np.concatenate(picks)) def learning_curve(X, y, make, sizes, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0) -> dict:     """Mean held-out accuracy when each CV training fold is cut down to ``size`` samples."""     X, y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y)     folds = stratified_folds(y, k, seed)     out = {}     for size in sizes:         hits = total = 0         rng = np.random.default_rng([seed, size])         for tr, te in folds:             if size > len(tr):                 continue             sub = tr[stratified_subsample(y[tr], size, rng)]             pred = make().fit(X[sub], y[sub]).predict(X[te])             hits += int(np.sum(pred == y[te])); total += len(te)         out[int(size)] = hits / total if total else float("nan")     return out ``` #### `wavebrain/study.py` ```python """Nonlinear-readout study:  ``python -m wavebrain.study [--quick]``. Question -------- The pilot found that the medium REMEMBERS the two shapes (pair identity decodable, "decode then compare" 0.93) but a LINEAR readout compares them with only ~0.70. Can a NONLINEAR readout of the same sensor data close that gap -- and which memory channel carries the information? Arms: lesions of the pilot's no-cubic-term medium "L" (same trials, same drive) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- FULL       hologram ON,  fields kept        the pilot's L arm (primary) HOLO_ONLY  hologram ON,  fields zeroed      only the plastic memory H remains RING_ONLY  hologram OFF, fields kept        only the field ring-down remains NULL       hologram OFF, fields zeroed      NEGATIVE CONTROL: every trial is physically                                             identical, so any accuracy above chance is a leak Why lesions: a first version used "hologram OFF + long delay" as the control and it was NOT memory-free -- the fast field's ring-down (still ~2% at step 1000) is read out through the parametric coupling.  Zeroing the fields separates the channels cleanly. Readouts: linear (the pilot's), rbf, poly2 (kernel ridge) and quadpca (PCA + pairwise products + ridge).  A raw-pixel row is a weak reference only: placement jitter wrecks pixel distances, so it is NOT a ceiling (see ``baselines.py`` for translation-tolerant engineered features). Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import hashlib import json import time from dataclasses import replace from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from .classifier import cross_val_predict, permutation_test, wilson_interval from .experiment import _probe_source, build_sensors, validate_experiment from .medium import WaveMedium from .pairtask import PairConfig, PairDataset, _write_phase, build_raw_only from .pilot import effective_folds, pilot_config, strip_private from .readouts import NONLINEAR_READOUTS, READOUTS, learning_curve, make_readout from .stats import paired_bootstrap_diff # arm -> (hologram plastic during the write, zero the fields after the write) ARMS = {"FULL": (True, False), "HOLO_ONLY": (True, True), "RING_ONLY": (False, False),         "NULL": (False, True)} ARM_LABEL = {"FULL": "FULL  hologram+fields", "HOLO_ONLY": "HOLO_ONLY  hologram only",              "RING_ONLY": "RING_ONLY  fields only", "NULL": "NULL  neg. control"} PRIMARY_ARM, CONTROL_ARM = "FULL", "NULL" ACC_THRESHOLD = 0.85 CURVE_SIZES = (24, 48, 96, 192) CURVE_ARMS = ("FULL", "HOLO_ONLY") # -------------------------------------------------------- lesion simulation def clear_fields(sim: WaveMedium) -> None:     """Zero the fast and slow fields (all time levels); keep the hologram H and the clock."""     sim._f[...] = 0.0     sim._s[...] = 0.0 def simulate_lesion_trial(cfg: PairConfig, index: int, sensors, clear: bool):     """Pilot trial (write -> delay -> paired probe), optionally zeroing the fields after the write.     With ``clear=False`` this reproduces ``pairtask.simulate_pair_trial`` exactly.     """     b = cfg.base     sim, info = _write_phase(cfg, index)     if clear:         clear_fields(sim)     sim.run(b.delay_steps)     a_sim, b_sim = sim.clone(), sim.clone()     a_sim.add_source(_probe_source(b, a_sim.t))     resp = (a_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float)             - b_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float))     info["clip_events"] = sim.clip_events + a_sim.clip_events + b_sim.clip_events     return resp, info def build_lesion_dataset(cfg: PairConfig, clear: bool, verbose: bool = False) -> PairDataset:     validate_experiment(cfg.base)     sensors = build_sensors(cfg.base)     raw, y, kinds = build_raw_only(cfg)     resp, infos = [], []     t0 = time.time()     for i in range(cfg.n_trials):         r, info = simulate_lesion_trial(cfg, i, sensors, clear)         resp.append(r); infos.append(info)         if verbose and (i + 1) % 30 == 0:             print(f"    trial {i + 1}/{cfg.n_trials} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     stats = {"clip_events": int(sum(d["clip_events"] for d in infos)),              "peak_u": float(np.mean([d["peak_u"] for d in infos])),              "write_rms": float(np.mean([d["write_rms"] for d in infos])),              "hologram_max_mean": float(np.mean([d["hologram_max"] for d in infos])),              "seconds": time.time() - t0,              "response_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(resp))))}     return PairDataset(cfg.base, np.stack(resp), y, raw, stats, kinds) # ---------------------------------------------------------- arms and dataset cache def arm_config(arm: str, seed: int, n_trials: int) -> PairConfig:     if arm not in ARMS:         raise ValueError(f"unknown arm '{arm}'")     hologram, _ = ARMS[arm]     cfg = pilot_config("L", seed, n_trials)     return replace(cfg, base=replace(cfg.base, use_hologram=hologram)) def dataset_key(cfg: PairConfig, clear: bool = False) -> str:     """Fingerprint of everything that determines a dataset (configuration, seed, lesion)."""     return hashlib.sha256((repr(cfg) + f"|clear={clear}").encode()).hexdigest()[:16] def save_dataset(ds: PairDataset, path, cfg: PairConfig, clear: bool = False) -> None:     np.savez(path, responses=ds.responses, y=ds.y, raw=ds.raw, kinds=ds.kinds,              stats=json.dumps(ds.stats), key=dataset_key(cfg, clear)) def load_dataset(path, cfg: PairConfig, clear: bool = False) -> PairDataset:     """Load a cached dataset; raise ValueError if it belongs to a different configuration."""     with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as z:         if str(z["key"]) != dataset_key(cfg, clear):             raise ValueError(f"stale cache {path}: configuration fingerprint differs")         return PairDataset(cfg.base, z["responses"], z["y"], z["raw"],                            json.loads(str(z["stats"])), z["kinds"]) def get_dataset(arm: str, seed: int, n_trials: int, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = False):     """Return (dataset, from_cache).  A stale or missing cache entry is rebuilt, never reused."""     cfg = arm_config(arm, seed, n_trials)     clear = ARMS[arm][1]     path = None     if cache_dir is not None:         Path(cache_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)         path = Path(cache_dir) / f"{arm}_seed{seed}_n{n_trials}.npz"         if path.exists():             try:                 return load_dataset(path, cfg, clear), True             except ValueError:                 if verbose:                     print(f"    [cache] stale entry for {arm}/seed {seed}: rebuilding", flush=True)     ds = build_lesion_dataset(cfg, clear)     if path is not None:         save_dataset(ds, path, cfg, clear)     return ds, False # ------------------------------------------------------------------ evaluation def evaluate_readout(X, y, name: str, seed: int, n_perm: int, folds: int = 5, repeats: int = 3) -> dict:     """Repeated stratified CV accuracy + Wilson CI (n = len(y)) + optional permutation p."""     y = np.asarray(y)     make, k = make_readout(name), effective_folds(y, folds)     correct = np.mean([cross_val_predict(X, y, k=k, seed=seed + 100 * r, make=make) == y                        for r in range(repeats)], axis=0)     acc = float(correct.mean())     lo, hi = wilson_interval(int(round(acc * len(y))), len(y))     p = None     if n_perm > 0:         p, _ = permutation_test(X, y, acc, n_perm=n_perm, k=k, seed=seed + 7, make=make)     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": lo, "ci_high": hi, "p_perm": p, "folds": k, "_correct": correct} def evaluate_arm(ds: PairDataset, readouts, seed: int, n_perm: int) -> dict:     X = ds.features()     t = ds.targets()     return {"same_diff": {r: evaluate_readout(X, t["same_diff"], r, seed, n_perm) for r in readouts},             "top_kind": {r: evaluate_readout(X, t["top_kind"], r, seed, 0) for r in readouts},             "heldout": {r: heldout_pair_types(X, t["same_diff"], ds.kinds, r, len(ds.cfg.classes))                         for r in readouts},             "stats": {**{k: float(v) for k, v in ds.stats.items()},                       "clean_spread": float(ds.features(0.0).std(axis=0).max())}} def paired_vs_linear(arm_res: dict, seed: int, readouts=NONLINEAR_READOUTS) -> dict:     """Paired bootstrap of (readout - linear) on the same samples, Bonferroni-adjusted level."""     level = 1.0 - 0.05 / len(readouts)     lin = arm_res["same_diff"]["linear"]["_correct"]     return {r: paired_bootstrap_diff(arm_res["same_diff"][r]["_correct"], lin, seed=seed, level=level)             for r in readouts if r in arm_res["same_diff"]} # ------------------------------------------- does the readout learn a RULE or a LOOKUP? def heldout_pair_types(X, y, kinds, name: str, n_kinds: int = 3) -> dict:     """Leave-one-pair-type-out: train on 8 of the 9 (shape, shape) combinations, test on the 9th.     A readout that merely recognises which of the 9 pair types it sees (a lookup table) has     nothing to say about an unseen type.  A readout that has learned something like "same"     transfers.  Every trial is tested exactly once, so n = len(y) for the Wilson interval.     """     X, y, kinds = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(y), np.asarray(kinds)     pair = kinds[:, 0] * n_kinds + kinds[:, 1]     make = make_readout(name)     pred = np.empty_like(y)     per_type = {}     for t in np.unique(pair):         te = pair == t         pred[te] = make().fit(X[~te], y[~te]).predict(X[te])         per_type[int(t)] = float(np.mean(pred[te] == y[te]))     acc = float(np.mean(pred == y))     lo, hi = wilson_interval(int(round(acc * len(y))), len(y))     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": lo, "ci_high": hi, "per_type": per_type} def generalizes(ho: dict, min_acc: float = 0.70) -> bool:     """Rule-like transfer: held-out-type accuracy >= min_acc and its CI excludes chance (0.5)."""     return ho["acc"] >= min_acc and ho["ci_low"] > 0.5 # --------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def attribution(sres: dict, threshold: float = ACC_THRESHOLD) -> dict:     """Which memory channel supports a high-accuracy readout?  (best readout per lesion arm)     'joint' = neither channel alone reaches the threshold; only the combination does.     """     best = {}     for arm in ("FULL", "HOLO_ONLY", "RING_ONLY"):         if arm in sres["arms"]:             cells = sres["arms"][arm]["same_diff"]             name = max(cells, key=lambda r: cells[r]["acc"])             best[arm] = {"readout": name, "acc": cells[name]["acc"]}     holo = best.get("HOLO_ONLY", {}).get("acc", 0.0) >= threshold     ring = best.get("RING_ONLY", {}).get("acc", 0.0) >= threshold     channel = ("both" if holo and ring else "hologram" if holo else "ring-down" if ring else "joint")     return {"best": best, "channel": channel} def verdict(sres: dict, alpha: float = 0.05, threshold: float = ACC_THRESHOLD) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED  the safety clip fired in any arm.     -  INCONCLUSIVE     FULL or NULL is missing, OR too few permutations for the control                         test to be able to fire at all (needs 1/(n_perm+1) <= alpha/n_readouts;                         a rule that cannot fire must not be allowed to pass silently).     1. CONTROL_FAILS    any readout beats chance on NULL (every trial physically identical,                         so this can only be a pipeline leak).     2. NO_GAP           the linear readout already reaches ``threshold`` on FULL.     3. PROMISING        a nonlinear readout on FULL beats the linear one (paired CI at a                         Bonferroni-adjusted level, lower end > 0), reaches ``threshold`` and is                         above chance (p < alpha).  The text reports WHICH memory channel                         (hologram / ring-down / both / joint) supports it (see ``attribution``).     4. PARTIAL          a nonlinear readout beats the linear one but stays below ``threshold``.     5. GAP_STAYS        otherwise.     """     arms = sres["arms"]     clipped = [a for a in arms if arms[a]["stats"]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if PRIMARY_ARM not in arms or CONTROL_ARM not in arms:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": f"Needs both {PRIMARY_ARM} and {CONTROL_ARM}.",                 "next": "Run with both arms."}     ctrl = arms[CONTROL_ARM]["same_diff"]     a_ctrl = alpha / len(ctrl)     if 1.0 / (sres.get("n_perm", 0) + 1.0) > a_ctrl:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE",                 "text": f"Too few permutations ({sres.get('n_perm', 0)}) for the control test to "                         f"reach p < {a_ctrl:.4f}.",                 "next": f"Rerun with at least {int(np.ceil(1 / a_ctrl))} permutations."}     failing = [r for r, v in ctrl.items() if v["p_perm"] is not None and v["p_perm"] < a_ctrl]     if failing:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"On the NULL arm (identical trials) {', '.join(failing)} beat chance: "                         f"the pipeline leaks label information.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel before interpreting any readout."}     prim = arms[PRIMARY_ARM]["same_diff"]     if prim["linear"]["acc"] >= threshold:         return {"outcome": "NO_GAP", "text": "The linear readout already reaches the threshold.",                 "next": "Nothing to close; move to a harder task."}     paired = sres["paired"][PRIMARY_ARM]     better = [r for r in NONLINEAR_READOUTS if r in paired and paired[r]["lo"] > 0]     promising = [r for r in better if prim[r]["acc"] >= threshold                  and prim[r]["p_perm"] is not None and prim[r]["p_perm"] < alpha]     att = attribution(sres, threshold)     if promising:         ho = arms[PRIMARY_ARM].get("heldout", {})         rule = [r for r in promising if r in ho and generalizes(ho[r])]         kind = "unknown" if not ho else ("rule-like" if rule else "type-lookup")         return {"outcome": "PROMISING", "channel": att["channel"], "generalization": kind,                 "text": f"{', '.join(promising)} beat the linear readout (paired CI above 0) and "                         f"reach >= {threshold:.2f}; the NULL control is at chance. Supporting memory "                         f"channel (best readout >= {threshold:.2f} per arm): {att['channel']}. "                         f"Transfer to held-out pair types: {kind}.",                 "next": "If 'type-lookup', the readout recognises the 9 pair types but does not "                         "compute 'same'; test with unseen shapes before claiming comparison. If "                         "'rule-like', test robustness (noise, delay, seeds)."}     if better:         return {"outcome": "PARTIAL",                 "text": f"{', '.join(better)} improve on the linear readout but stay below "                         f"{threshold:.2f}.",                 "next": "Check the learning curve: if still rising, collect more trials."}     return {"outcome": "GAP_STAYS",             "text": "No nonlinear readout beats the linear one: the information is present but "                     "not reachable by these readouts at this sample size.",             "next": "Inspect the learning curve; if flat, the physics (not the readout) must change."} def combine_verdicts(per_seed: dict) -> dict:     outcomes = {s: v["outcome"] for s, v in per_seed.items()}     if len(set(outcomes.values())) == 1:         o = next(iter(outcomes.values()))         return {"outcome": o, "agree": True, "per_seed": outcomes}     return {"outcome": "MIXED", "agree": False, "per_seed": outcomes} # --------------------------------------------------- learning curve & cross-checks def pooled_learning_curve(datasets: dict, arm: str, readouts, sizes=CURVE_SIZES, seed: int = 0) -> dict:     """Learning curves on the trials of several seeds pooled together (more data)."""     Xs, ys = [], []     for s, ds in datasets.items():         Xs.append(ds.features(seed=12345 + int(s)))      # independent noise draw per seed         ys.append(ds.y)     X, y = np.vstack(Xs), np.concatenate(ys)     return {"n_total": int(len(y)),             "curves": {r: learning_curve(X, y, make_readout(r), sizes, k=5, seed=seed) for r in readouts}} def reproduction_check(study_acc: float, pilot_acc: float, tol: float = 1e-9) -> dict:     """The linear readout on FULL must reproduce the pilot's published L accuracy exactly."""     return {"study": study_acc, "pilot": pilot_acc, "delta": study_acc - pilot_acc,             "ok": abs(study_acc - pilot_acc) < tol} # -------------------------------------------------------------------- the study def run_readout_study(n_trials: int = 120, seeds=(0, 1), arms=tuple(ARMS), readouts=READOUTS,                       n_perm: int = 100, cache_dir=None, pilot_files: dict | None = None,                       curve_sizes=CURVE_SIZES, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     res: dict = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "n_perm": n_perm,                             "readouts": list(readouts), "threshold": ACC_THRESHOLD},                  "seeds": {}}     pooled: dict = {}     for seed in seeds:         sres: dict = {"arms": {}, "from_cache": {}, "n_perm": n_perm}         datasets = {}         for arm in arms:             ds, cached = get_dataset(arm, seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)             datasets[arm] = ds             sres["from_cache"][arm] = cached             sres["arms"][arm] = evaluate_arm(ds, readouts, seed, n_perm)             if verbose:                 r = sres["arms"][arm]["same_diff"]                 print(f"[seed {seed}] {arm:<8}" + "  ".join(f"{k}={v['acc']:.3f}" for k, v in r.items())                       + ("  (cached)" if cached else ""), flush=True)         ref = datasets[arms[0]]         sres["raw"] = {r: evaluate_readout(ref.raw, ref.y, r, seed, n_perm) for r in readouts}         sres["paired"] = {a: paired_vs_linear(sres["arms"][a], seed)                           for a in arms if "linear" in readouts}         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres)         for arm in CURVE_ARMS:             if arm in datasets:                 pooled.setdefault(arm, {})[seed] = datasets[arm]         res["seeds"][seed] = sres     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     if pooled:         res["learning_curve"] = {arm: pooled_learning_curve(dsets, arm, readouts, curve_sizes)                                  for arm, dsets in pooled.items()}     if pilot_files:         res["reproduction"] = {}         for seed, path in pilot_files.items():             if seed in res["seeds"] and Path(path).exists():                 pilot_acc = json.loads(Path(path).read_text())["media"]["L"]["same_diff"]["acc"]                 got = res["seeds"][seed]["arms"][PRIMARY_ARM]["same_diff"]["linear"]["acc"]                 res["reproduction"][seed] = reproduction_check(got, pilot_acc)     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res # ---------------------------------------------------------------------- report def _cell(r: dict) -> str:     s = f"{r['acc']:.2f} [{r['ci_low']:.2f},{r['ci_high']:.2f}]"     return s + (f" p={r['p_perm']:.2f}" if r["p_perm"] is not None else "") def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain nonlinear-readout study  (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, "              f"permutations={c['n_perm']}, success threshold={c['threshold']})"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: same/different accuracy [95% CI] (permutation p); chance 0.50 ===",                   f"{'arm':<28}" + "".join(f"{r:>27}" for r in rd), "-" * (28 + 27 * len(rd))]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             lines.append(f"{ARM_LABEL[arm][:27]:<28}" + "".join(f"{_cell(a['same_diff'][r]):>27}" for r in rd))         lines.append(f"{'raw pixels (reference)':<28}" + "".join(f"{_cell(s['raw'][r]):>27}" for r in rd))         lines += ["", "top-shape (3-way) accuracy, chance 0.33:"]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             lines.append(f"  {arm:<10}" + "  ".join(f"{r}={a['top_kind'][r]['acc']:.2f}" for r in rd))         lines += ["", "held-out PAIR TYPES (train on 8 of 9 types, test on the 9th), chance 0.50:"]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             lines.append(f"  {arm:<10}" + "  ".join(f"{r}={a['heldout'][r]['acc']:.2f}" for r in rd))         lines += ["", "paired accuracy gain over the linear readout [Bonferroni 98.3% CI]:"]         for arm, pr in s["paired"].items():             lines.append(f"  {arm:<10}" + "  ".join(f"{r} {d['diff']:+.3f} [{d['lo']:+.3f},{d['hi']:+.3f}]"                                                     for r, d in pr.items()))         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}): {v['outcome']}", f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     for arm, lc in res.get("learning_curve", {}).items():         sizes = sorted(next(iter(lc["curves"].values())))         lines += ["", f"=== learning curve, {arm}, seeds pooled (n={lc['n_total']}): "                       f"held-out accuracy vs training-set size ===",                   f"{'readout':<10}" + "".join(f"{n:>8}" for n in sizes)]         for r, curve in lc["curves"].items():             lines.append(f"{r:<10}" + "".join(f"{curve[n]:>8.3f}" for n in sizes))     if "reproduction" in res:         lines += ["", "reproduction of the pilot's published L accuracy by the linear readout:"]         for seed, d in res["reproduction"].items():             lines.append(f"  seed {seed}: study {d['study']:.6f}  pilot {d['pilot']:.6f}  "                          f"delta {d['delta']:+.1e}  {'OK' if d['ok'] else 'MISMATCH'}")     cmb = res["combined"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {cmb['outcome']}  (per seed: {cmb['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=120, help="multiple of 12")     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--perms", type=int, default=100)     ap.add_argument("--arms", default=",".join(ARMS))     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None, help="directory for cached sensor datasets (.npz)")     ap.add_argument("--pilot-json", action="append", default=[],                     help="SEED=path of a pilot_results json to cross-check, e.g. 0=pilot_results.json")     ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="48 trials, seed 0 only, 20 permutations")     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     if a.quick:         a.trials, a.seeds, a.perms = 48, "0", 20     seeds = tuple(int(s) for s in a.seeds.split(","))     pilot = {int(k): v for k, v in (x.split("=", 1) for x in a.pilot_json)}     res = run_readout_study(a.trials, seeds, tuple(a.arms.split(",")), READOUTS, a.perms,                             a.cache, pilot or None)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(_stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 def _stringify(obj):     """JSON needs string keys: convert int seed keys recursively."""     if isinstance(obj, dict):         return {str(k): _stringify(v) for k, v in obj.items()}     return obj if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/baselines.py` ```python """Post-hoc engineered baselines (EXPLORATORY):  ``python -m wavebrain.baselines``. Added after the main study, because its raw-pixel reference row was weak (nonlinear readouts scored ~0.7, below the medium's 1.00) and a weak baseline proves nothing.  Placement jitter (+/-3 cells) wrecks pixel-wise distances, so this module offers translation-tolerant features computed from the SAME trials, then asks whether the medium still has an advantage. fft_mag   magnitude of the 2-D Fourier transform of each pattern's half-canvas           (exactly invariant to translation of a pattern that lies fully inside its half) pooled    4x4 average pooling of the pixels (tolerates small shifts) """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time import numpy as np from .pairtask import RAW_H, RAW_W, build_raw_only from .pilot import pilot_config, strip_private from .readouts import READOUTS from .study import evaluate_readout, heldout_pair_types def fft_magnitude_features(raw: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     """|rfft2| of the top and bottom half-canvas, concatenated."""     imgs = np.asarray(raw, float).reshape(len(raw), RAW_H, RAW_W)     halves = [imgs[:, : RAW_H // 2], imgs[:, RAW_H // 2:]]     return np.concatenate([np.abs(np.fft.rfft2(h)).reshape(len(raw), -1) for h in halves], axis=1) def pooled_pixel_features(raw: np.ndarray, factor: int = 4) -> np.ndarray:     imgs = np.asarray(raw, float).reshape(len(raw), RAW_H, RAW_W)     if RAW_H % factor or RAW_W % factor:         raise ValueError("factor must divide the canvas size")     n = len(raw)     return imgs.reshape(n, RAW_H // factor, factor, RAW_W // factor, factor).mean(axis=(2, 4)).reshape(n, -1) BASELINES = {"raw": lambda r: np.asarray(r, float), "fft_mag": fft_magnitude_features,              "pooled": pooled_pixel_features} def run_baselines(n_trials: int = 120, seeds=(0, 1), n_perm: int = 100, readouts=READOUTS,                   verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "n_perm": n_perm,                       "readouts": list(readouts)}, "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         raw, y, kinds = build_raw_only(pilot_config("L", seed, n_trials))   # the study's trials         sres = {}         for name, fn in BASELINES.items():             X = fn(raw)             sres[name] = {"same_diff": {r: evaluate_readout(X, y, r, seed, n_perm) for r in readouts},                           "heldout": {r: heldout_pair_types(X, y, kinds, r) for r in readouts},                           "n_features": int(X.shape[1])}             if verbose:                 print(f"[seed {seed}] {name:<8}" + "  ".join(                     f"{r}={v['acc']:.3f}" for r, v in sres[name]["same_diff"].items()), flush=True)         res["seeds"][seed] = sres     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     rd = res["config"]["readouts"]     lines = [f"Engineered baselines on the study's trials (EXPLORATORY, post hoc; "              f"trials={res['config']['n_trials']}, permutations={res['config']['n_perm']})"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: same/different accuracy [95% CI] (permutation p); chance 0.50 ===",                   f"{'features':<20}" + "".join(f"{r:>27}" for r in rd), "-" * (20 + 27 * len(rd))]         for name, b in s.items():             cells = []             for r in rd:                 v = b["same_diff"][r]                 cells.append(f"{v['acc']:.2f} [{v['ci_low']:.2f},{v['ci_high']:.2f}] p={v['p_perm']:.2f}")             lines.append(f"{name + ' (' + str(b['n_features']) + ')':<20}" + "".join(f"{c:>27}" for c in cells))         lines += ["", "held-out PAIR TYPES accuracy (leave-one-type-out):"]         for name, b in s.items():             lines.append(f"  {name:<8}" + "  ".join(f"{r}={b['heldout'][r]['acc']:.2f}" for r in rd))     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=120)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--perms", type=int, default=100)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_baselines(a.trials, tuple(int(s) for s in a.seeds.split(",")), a.perms)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         out = {"config": res["config"], "seconds": res["seconds"],                "seeds": {str(k): v for k, v in res["seeds"].items()}}         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(out), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/randshapes.py` ```python """Random-shape same/different task: a NEW random shape on every trial. The v8 study used three shapes, so only nine (shape, shape) types existed and a readout could succeed by recognising the type (a lookup).  Here each trial draws fresh random smooth shapes: "same" writes one shape twice, "different" writes two independent shapes.  No type ever repeats, so a readout must carry a comparison rule to unseen shapes -- cross-validation itself tests generalisation. Design (paired across media and arms, like the pilot) ----------------------------------------------------- * label = 1 (same) for even trial index, 0 for odd: exactly balanced, no randomness. * shapes depend only on (seed, trial index): every medium and every arm sees identical trials. * all shapes have the same energy, so total drive carries no label information. * "different" pairs are rejection-sampled to be clearly dissimilar (correlation < max_corr). """ from __future__ import annotations import time from dataclasses import dataclass, field import numpy as np from .config import ExperimentConfig from .experiment import Dataset, _probe_source, build_sensors, validate_experiment from .medium import WaveMedium from .pairtask import RAW_H, RAW_W, PairConfig from .sensors import SensorArray from .sources import Gated, Sine, Source from .study import clear_fields # ------------------------------------------------------------------ shapes def gaussian_lowpass(noise: np.ndarray, sigma: float) -> np.ndarray:     """Gaussian low-pass filter in the Fourier domain (sigma in cells)."""     fy = np.fft.fftfreq(noise.shape[0])[:, None]     fx = np.fft.fftfreq(noise.shape[1])[None, :]     filt = np.exp(-2.0 * (np.pi * sigma) ** 2 * (fx ** 2 + fy ** 2))     return np.real(np.fft.ifft2(np.fft.fft2(noise) * filt)) def radial_taper(size: int) -> np.ndarray:     """1 at the centre, smoothly 0 at radius size/2 (keeps shapes inside their window)."""     c = (size - 1) / 2.0     yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:size, 0:size]     r = np.hypot(yy - c, xx - c) / (size / 2.0)     return np.where(r < 1.0, 0.5 * (1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * r)), 0.0) def random_shape(rng: np.random.Generator, size: int = 24, smooth: float = 3.0,                  energy: float | None = None) -> np.ndarray:     """Half-wave-rectified smooth random field under a radial taper, normalised to equal energy."""     pad = size     field_ = gaussian_lowpass(rng.normal(size=(size + pad, size + pad)), smooth)     field_ = field_[pad // 2: pad // 2 + size, pad // 2: pad // 2 + size]     field_ = (field_ - field_.mean()) / (field_.std() + 1e-12)     p = np.maximum(field_, 0.0) * radial_taper(size)     e = float(np.sum(p ** 2))     if e <= 0.0:         raise ValueError("degenerate shape")     target = 0.25 * size * size if energy is None else energy     return p * np.sqrt(target / e) def correlation(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:     a, b = np.asarray(a, float).ravel(), np.asarray(b, float).ravel()     return float(a @ b / (np.linalg.norm(a) * np.linalg.norm(b) + 1e-12)) def draw_shape_pair(rng: np.random.Generator, size: int, smooth: float, max_corr: float,                     same: bool) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:     a = random_shape(rng, size, smooth)     if same:         return a, a.copy()     for _ in range(500):         b = random_shape(rng, size, smooth)         if correlation(a, b) < max_corr:             return a, b     raise RuntimeError("could not draw a sufficiently dissimilar shape; raise max_corr") # -------------------------------------------------------------------- config @dataclass(frozen=True) class RandConfig:     base: ExperimentConfig = field(default_factory=ExperimentConfig)     n_trials: int = 240     separation: int = 34     seed: int = 0     smooth: float = 3.0     max_corr: float = 0.5     def __post_init__(self) -> None:         if self.n_trials <= 0 or self.n_trials % 4:             raise ValueError("n_trials must be a positive multiple of 4")         if not (0.0 < self.max_corr < 1.0) or self.smooth <= 0:             raise ValueError("need 0 < max_corr < 1 and smooth > 0")         # geometry (patterns inside the interior, clear of the probe line and each other)         PairConfig(base=self.base, n_trials=12, separation=self.separation, seed=self.seed)     @property     def centres(self) -> tuple[int, int]:         return PairConfig(base=self.base, n_trials=12, separation=self.separation).centres def trial_label(index: int) -> int:     return 1 if index % 2 == 0 else 0 def trial_shapes(cfg: RandConfig, index: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:     rng = np.random.default_rng([cfg.seed, index, 11])     return draw_shape_pair(rng, cfg.base.pattern_size, cfg.smooth, cfg.max_corr, trial_label(index) == 1) def draw_offsets(cfg: RandConfig, index: int) -> np.ndarray:     rng = np.random.default_rng([cfg.seed, index, 22])     j = cfg.base.jitter     return rng.integers(-j, j + 1, size=(2, 2)) def rand_raw_canvas(cfg: RandConfig, shapes, offsets: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     size = cfg.base.pattern_size     canvas = np.zeros((RAW_H, RAW_W))     for pat, (dy, dx), cy in zip(shapes, offsets, (20, 60)):         y0, x0 = cy - size // 2 + int(dy), RAW_W // 2 - size // 2 + int(dx)         canvas[y0:y0 + size, x0:x0 + size] = pat     return canvas.ravel() # ---------------------------------------------------------------- simulation def rand_write_phase(cfg: RandConfig, index: int, patterns=None, offsets=None):     """Write the trial's two shapes; return (medium with plasticity off, stats).     ``patterns`` / ``offsets`` may be overridden (used by a test that checks this routine     against the pilot's ``_write_phase``).     """     b, m = cfg.base, cfg.base.medium     patterns = trial_shapes(cfg, index) if patterns is None else patterns     offsets = draw_offsets(cfg, index) if offsets is None else offsets     sim = WaveMedium(m)     writers = []     for k, (pat, (dy, dx), cy) in enumerate(zip(patterns, offsets, cfg.centres)):         y0 = cy - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dy)         x0 = m.nx // 2 - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dx)         env = Gated(Sine(b.write_amp, b.write_omega), 0, b.write_steps)         writers.append(sim.add_source(Source(pat, y0, x0, env, f"write-{k}")))     sim.plastic = b.use_hologram     peak, rms_acc, rms_n = 0.0, 0.0, 0     for step in range(b.write_steps):         sim.step()         peak = max(peak, float(np.abs(sim.u).max()))         if step >= b.write_steps - 100:             rms_acc += float(np.mean(sim.u.astype(float) ** 2)); rms_n += 1     for w in writers:         sim.remove_source(w)     sim.plastic = False     return sim, {"peak_u": peak, "write_rms": float(np.sqrt(rms_acc / rms_n)),                  "hologram_max": float(np.abs(sim.H).max())} def simulate_rand_trial(cfg: RandConfig, index: int, sensors: SensorArray, clear: bool):     b = cfg.base     sim, info = rand_write_phase(cfg, index)     if clear:         clear_fields(sim)     sim.run(b.delay_steps)     a_sim, b_sim = sim.clone(), sim.clone()     a_sim.add_source(_probe_source(b, a_sim.t))     resp = (a_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float)             - b_sim.run(b.readout_steps, sensors).astype(float))     info["clip_events"] = sim.clip_events + a_sim.clip_events + b_sim.clip_events     return resp, info @dataclass class RandDataset(Dataset):     """``y`` is SAME(1)/DIFFERENT(0); ``raw`` holds the written pixel canvases."""     def targets(self) -> dict:         return {"same_diff": self.y} def build_rand_raw(cfg: RandConfig) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:     """(raw canvases, labels) without any PDE: cheap, used for baselines and tests."""     raw = np.stack([rand_raw_canvas(cfg, trial_shapes(cfg, i), draw_offsets(cfg, i))                     for i in range(cfg.n_trials)])     return raw, np.array([trial_label(i) for i in range(cfg.n_trials)]) def build_rand_dataset(cfg: RandConfig, clear: bool, verbose: bool = False) -> RandDataset:     validate_experiment(cfg.base)     sensors = build_sensors(cfg.base)     raw, y = build_rand_raw(cfg)     resp, infos = [], []     t0 = time.time()     for i in range(cfg.n_trials):         r, info = simulate_rand_trial(cfg, i, sensors, clear)         resp.append(r); infos.append(info)         if verbose and (i + 1) % 60 == 0:             print(f"    trial {i + 1}/{cfg.n_trials} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     stats = {"clip_events": int(sum(d["clip_events"] for d in infos)),              "peak_u": float(np.mean([d["peak_u"] for d in infos])),              "write_rms": float(np.mean([d["write_rms"] for d in infos])),              "hologram_max_mean": float(np.mean([d["hologram_max"] for d in infos])),              "seconds": time.time() - t0,              "response_rms": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(resp))))}     return RandDataset(cfg.base, np.stack(resp), y, raw, stats) ``` #### `wavebrain/randstudy.py` ```python """Random-shape same/different study:  ``python -m wavebrain.randstudy [--quick]``. Question -------- With a NEW random shape on every trial (no lookup possible), does a readout of the wave medium compare shapes better than a readout of simple engineered features of the written pixels?  Lesion arms separate the memory channels; a NULL arm (every trial physically identical) guards against pipeline leaks. Feature sets ------------ FULL, HOLO_ONLY, RING_ONLY, NULL   medium responses (the v8 lesion arms) raw, pooled, fft_concat            GENERIC engineered features: a readout must LEARN the rule fft_absdiff                        hard-wired comparator |F_top| - |F_bottom| (an ORACLE, the                                    ceiling of what is knowable; never a competitor) Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import hashlib import json import time from dataclasses import replace from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from .baselines import fft_magnitude_features, pooled_pixel_features from .pairtask import RAW_H, RAW_W from .pilot import pilot_config, strip_private from .randshapes import RandConfig, RandDataset, build_rand_dataset, build_rand_raw from .readouts import READOUTS, learning_curve, make_readout from .stats import paired_bootstrap_diff from .study import ARMS, combine_verdicts, evaluate_readout PRIMARY_ARM, CONTROL_ARM = "FULL", "NULL" GENERIC_BASELINE = "fft_concat"          # the pre-registered comparison for the medium MIN_ACC = 0.65 CURVE_SIZES = (24, 48, 96, 192, 384) CURVE_READOUTS = ("poly2", "quadpca") # Random smooth shapes concentrate energy into taller peaks than the three fixed shapes # (hologram on: mean peak 2.5, max 3.4; hologram off, which lacks the detuning feedback: # mean 3.4, max 4.5; both measured on tuning seed 1000 at the shared drive), so the pilot's # safety clip of 2.4 would fire in most trials.  The medium has beta = 0, so its stability # bound (8 * c2_max) does not depend on the clip level; raising the clip changes nothing # unless a clip would occur (tested bit for bit).  The clip must still never fire. RAND_U_LIMIT = 8.0 ARM_LABEL = {"FULL": "FULL  hologram+fields", "HOLO_ONLY": "HOLO_ONLY  hologram only",              "RING_ONLY": "RING_ONLY  fields only", "NULL": "NULL  neg. control",              "raw": "raw pixels", "pooled": "pooled pixels (4x4)", "fft_concat": "FFT magnitude (generic)",              "fft_absdiff": "FFT |top-bottom| (ORACLE)"} # ------------------------------------------------------- engineered features def fft_absdiff_features(raw: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     """|  |rfft2(top)| - |rfft2(bottom)|  |: exactly 0 for identical shapes at any placement."""     imgs = np.asarray(raw, float).reshape(len(raw), RAW_H, RAW_W)     top = np.abs(np.fft.rfft2(imgs[:, : RAW_H // 2]))     bot = np.abs(np.fft.rfft2(imgs[:, RAW_H // 2:]))     return np.abs(top - bot).reshape(len(raw), -1) BASELINE_SETS = {"raw": lambda r: np.asarray(r, float), "pooled": pooled_pixel_features,                  "fft_concat": fft_magnitude_features, "fft_absdiff": fft_absdiff_features} # --------------------------------------------------------- arms and cache def rand_arm_config(arm: str, seed: int, n_trials: int) -> RandConfig:     if arm not in ARMS:         raise ValueError(f"unknown arm '{arm}'")     base = pilot_config("L", seed, 12).base     base = replace(base, use_hologram=ARMS[arm][0], medium=replace(base.medium, u_limit=RAND_U_LIMIT))     return RandConfig(base=base, n_trials=n_trials, seed=seed) def rand_key(cfg: RandConfig, clear: bool) -> str:     return hashlib.sha256((repr(cfg) + f"|clear={clear}|rand").encode()).hexdigest()[:16] def save_rand(ds: RandDataset, path, cfg: RandConfig, clear: bool) -> None:     np.savez(path, responses=ds.responses, y=ds.y, raw=ds.raw, stats=json.dumps(ds.stats),              key=rand_key(cfg, clear)) def load_rand(path, cfg: RandConfig, clear: bool) -> RandDataset:     with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as z:         if str(z["key"]) != rand_key(cfg, clear):             raise ValueError(f"stale cache {path}: configuration fingerprint differs")         return RandDataset(cfg.base, z["responses"], z["y"], z["raw"], json.loads(str(z["stats"]))) def get_rand_dataset(arm: str, seed: int, n_trials: int, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = False):     """(dataset, from_cache); stale or missing cache entries are rebuilt, never reused."""     cfg, clear = rand_arm_config(arm, seed, n_trials), ARMS[arm][1]     path = None     if cache_dir is not None:         Path(cache_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)         path = Path(cache_dir) / f"rand_{arm}_seed{seed}_n{n_trials}.npz"         if path.exists():             try:                 return load_rand(path, cfg, clear), True             except ValueError:                 if verbose:                     print(f"    [cache] stale entry for {arm}/seed {seed}: rebuilding", flush=True)     ds = build_rand_dataset(cfg, clear)     if path is not None:         save_rand(ds, path, cfg, clear)     return ds, False #
