------------------------------------------------------------------ evaluation def evaluate_features(X, y, readouts, seed: int, n_perm: int) -> dict:     return {r: evaluate_readout(X, y, r, seed, n_perm) for r in readouts} def paired_vs(a_res: dict, b_res: dict, seed: int, readouts) -> dict:     """Paired bootstrap of acc(a) - acc(b) per readout, level Bonferroni-adjusted over readouts."""     level = 1.0 - 0.05 / len(readouts)     return {r: paired_bootstrap_diff(a_res[r]["_correct"], b_res[r]["_correct"], seed=seed, level=level)             for r in readouts} def verdict(sres: dict, alpha: float = 0.05, min_acc: float = MIN_ACC) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED   the safety clip fired in any arm.     -  INCONCLUSIVE      FULL, NULL or the generic baseline is missing, or too few permutations for                          the tests to be able to fire at all (needs 1/(n_perm+1) <= alpha/n_readouts).     1. CONTROL_FAILS     any readout beats chance on NULL (identical trials: a pipeline leak).     2. MEDIUM_NO_SIGNAL  no readout of FULL beats chance (p < alpha / n_readouts).     3. MEDIUM_WINS       some readout of FULL is above chance, reaches ``min_acc`` and beats the SAME                          readout on the generic engineered baseline (``fft_concat``) with the lower end                          of the Bonferroni-adjusted paired interval above 0.     4. BASELINE_BETTER   a generic baseline beats the medium (paired interval entirely below 0) for                          every above-chance readout.     5. NO_ADVANTAGE      otherwise: the medium may carry signal, but is not shown to beat engineered                          features.     The hard-wired comparator (``fft_absdiff``) is reported as the ceiling, never as a competitor.     """     arms, base = sres["arms"], sres["baselines"]     clipped = [a for a in arms if arms[a]["stats"]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if PRIMARY_ARM not in arms or CONTROL_ARM not in arms or GENERIC_BASELINE not in base:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE",                 "text": f"Needs {PRIMARY_ARM}, {CONTROL_ARM} and the {GENERIC_BASELINE} baseline.",                 "next": "Run with those arms."}     full, ctrl = arms[PRIMARY_ARM]["same_diff"], arms[CONTROL_ARM]["same_diff"]     a_r = alpha / len(full)     if 1.0 / (sres.get("n_perm", 0) + 1.0) > a_r:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE",                 "text": f"Too few permutations ({sres.get('n_perm', 0)}) to reach p < {a_r:.4f}.",                 "next": f"Rerun with at least {int(np.ceil(1 / a_r))} permutations."}     leak = [r for r, v in ctrl.items() if v["p_perm"] is not None and v["p_perm"] < a_r]     if leak:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"On the NULL arm {', '.join(leak)} beat chance: the pipeline leaks.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel first."}     oracle = base.get("fft_absdiff", {}).get("linear", {}).get("acc")     tail = "" if oracle is None else f" Hard-wired comparator (ceiling): {oracle:.2f}."     above = [r for r in full if full[r]["p_perm"] is not None and full[r]["p_perm"] < a_r]     if not above:         return {"outcome": "MEDIUM_NO_SIGNAL",                 "text": "No readout of the medium beats chance on random shapes." + tail,                 "next": "The medium does not compare unseen shapes; redesign the physics or the task."}     paired = sres["paired"][GENERIC_BASELINE]     wins = [r for r in above if full[r]["acc"] >= min_acc and paired[r]["lo"] > 0]     if wins:         return {"outcome": "MEDIUM_WINS",                 "text": f"{', '.join(wins)} on the medium beat the same readout on generic FFT features "                         f"(paired CI above 0) at >= {min_acc:.2f}; NULL at chance." + tail,                 "next": "Test robustness (noise, delay, seeds) and which channel carries it."}     if all(paired[r]["hi"] < 0 for r in above):         return {"outcome": "BASELINE_BETTER",                 "text": "Generic engineered features beat the medium for every above-chance readout." + tail,                 "next": "The medium adds nothing here; look for tasks where memory over time matters."}     return {"outcome": "NO_ADVANTAGE",             "text": f"{', '.join(above)} are above chance on the medium but none beats generic FFT "                     f"features by the pre-registered criterion." + tail,             "next": "More trials or a task where engineered features are weaker."} # --------------------------------------------------------------------- runner def pooled_curves(feature_sets: dict, y: np.ndarray, readouts=CURVE_READOUTS, sizes=CURVE_SIZES) -> dict:     """Held-out accuracy vs training-set size on seed-pooled trials, per feature set and readout.     Answers "is a flat result just a lack of data?": the engineered baseline's curve rises with     n; if the medium's stays at chance, sample size is not the explanation.     """     return {"n_total": int(len(y)),             "curves": {name: {r: learning_curve(X, y, make_readout(r), sizes, k=5, seed=0) for r in readouts}                        for name, X in feature_sets.items()}} def run_rand_study(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), arms=tuple(ARMS), readouts=READOUTS,                    n_perm: int = 100, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = True,                    curve_sizes=CURVE_SIZES) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "n_perm": n_perm,                       "readouts": list(readouts), "min_acc": MIN_ACC}, "seeds": {}}     pool = {"FULL": [], "fft_concat": [], "y": []}     for seed in seeds:         sres = {"arms": {}, "baselines": {}, "from_cache": {}, "n_perm": n_perm}         datasets = {}         for arm in arms:             ds, cached = get_rand_dataset(arm, seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)             datasets[arm] = ds             sres["from_cache"][arm] = cached             r = evaluate_features(ds.features(), ds.y, readouts, seed, n_perm)             sres["arms"][arm] = {"same_diff": r,                                  "stats": {**{k: float(v) for k, v in ds.stats.items()},                                            "clean_spread": float(ds.features(0.0).std(axis=0).max())}}             if verbose:                 print(f"[seed {seed}] {arm:<10}" + "  ".join(f"{k}={v['acc']:.3f}" for k, v in r.items())                       + ("  (cached)" if cached else ""), flush=True)         ref = datasets[arms[0]]         for name, fn in BASELINE_SETS.items():             sres["baselines"][name] = evaluate_features(fn(ref.raw), ref.y, readouts, seed, n_perm)             if verbose:                 print(f"[seed {seed}] {name:<10}" + "  ".join(                     f"{k}={v['acc']:.3f}" for k, v in sres["baselines"][name].items()), flush=True)         if PRIMARY_ARM in arms:             full = sres["arms"][PRIMARY_ARM]["same_diff"]             sres["paired"] = {n: paired_vs(full, sres["baselines"][n], seed, readouts)                               for n in ("fft_concat", "pooled", "raw")}         if PRIMARY_ARM in datasets:             pool["FULL"].append(datasets[PRIMARY_ARM].features(seed=12345 + int(seed)))   # independent noise per seed             pool["fft_concat"].append(fft_magnitude_features(ref.raw))             pool["y"].append(ref.y)         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres) if "paired" in sres else {             "outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "FULL arm missing.", "next": "Include FULL."}         res["seeds"][seed] = sres     if pool["y"]:         res["learning_curve"] = pooled_curves({k: np.vstack(pool[k]) for k in ("FULL", "fft_concat")},                                               np.concatenate(pool["y"]), sizes=curve_sizes)     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def _cell(r: dict) -> str:     s = f"{r['acc']:.2f} [{r['ci_low']:.2f},{r['ci_high']:.2f}]"     return s + (f" p={r['p_perm']:.2f}" if r["p_perm"] is not None else "") def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain random-shape same/different study  (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, "              f"permutations={c['n_perm']}, chance 0.50)"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: accuracy [95% CI] (permutation p) ===",                   f"{'features':<28}" + "".join(f"{r:>27}" for r in rd), "-" * (28 + 27 * len(rd))]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             lines.append(f"{ARM_LABEL[arm]:<28}" + "".join(f"{_cell(a['same_diff'][r]):>27}" for r in rd))         for name, b in s["baselines"].items():             lines.append(f"{ARM_LABEL[name]:<28}" + "".join(f"{_cell(b[r]):>27}" for r in rd))         if "paired" in s:             lines += ["", "paired accuracy difference FULL minus baseline [Bonferroni CI] (same readout):"]             for name, pr in s["paired"].items():                 lines.append(f"  vs {name:<11}" + "  ".join(f"{r} {d['diff']:+.3f} [{d['lo']:+.3f},{d['hi']:+.3f}]"                                                            for r, d in pr.items()))         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}): {v['outcome']}", f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     if "learning_curve" in res:         lc = res["learning_curve"]         sizes = sorted(next(iter(next(iter(lc["curves"].values())).values())))         lines += ["", f"=== learning curve, seeds pooled (n={lc['n_total']}): held-out accuracy vs training size ===",                   f"{'features / readout':<28}" + "".join(f"{n:>8}" for n in sizes)]         for name, per in lc["curves"].items():             for r, curve in per.items():                 lines.append(f"{ARM_LABEL[name] + ' / ' + r:<28}" + "".join(f"{curve[n]:>8.3f}" for n in sizes))     cmb = res["combined"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {cmb['outcome']}  (per seed: {cmb['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240, help="multiple of 4")     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--perms", type=int, default=100)     ap.add_argument("--arms", default=",".join(ARMS))     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None)     ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true", help="48 trials, seed 0, 20 permutations")     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     if a.quick:         a.trials, a.seeds, a.perms = 48, "0", 20     res = run_rand_study(a.trials, tuple(int(s) for s in a.seeds.split(",")), tuple(a.arms.split(",")),                          READOUTS, a.perms, a.cache)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/decode.py` ```python """Does the medium's response contain the shapes at all?   ``python -m wavebrain.decode`` v9 found that the medium does not support same/different on unseen random shapes.  A comparison cannot be read out of a response that does not carry the shapes, so this module asks the upstream question directly: can the SHAPE of each written pattern (and, as a sanity target, its PLACEMENT) be decoded from the medium's sensor data? Targets (computed from the generating shapes, never from the features) --------------------------------------------------------------------- shape      leading components of the translation-invariant Fourier magnitude of each shape, in a            basis fitted on an INDEPENDENT pool of random shapes (no trial data leaks into it) position   the placement offsets (dy, dx) of both shapes Readouts are regressions (linear and RBF kernel ridge, hyper-parameters chosen by closed-form leave-one-out inside ``fit``); the score is out-of-fold R-squared averaged over standardised outputs.  Feature sets: the lesion arms FULL / RING_ONLY / HOLO_ONLY, the NULL arm (every trial physically identical: must give R-squared <= 0), and engineered features (a positive control: the Fourier magnitudes encode the shape descriptors exactly).  Sensor noise is re-added to the stored noise-free responses at several multipliers, so no new simulation is needed. Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time from functools import lru_cache import numpy as np from .pairtask import RAW_H, RAW_W from .pilot import strip_private from .randshapes import draw_offsets, random_shape, trial_shapes from .randstudy import BASELINE_SETS, get_rand_dataset, rand_arm_config from .readouts import (DEFAULT_ALPHAS, DEFAULT_GAMMAS, loo_residuals, rbf_kernel,                        squared_distances) from .study import combine_verdicts HALF = RAW_H // 2                        # each shape lives in a 40 x 40 half canvas N_COMPONENTS = 4                         # shape descriptors per shape MIN_R2 = 0.10                            # "clearly above zero" (pre-registered) POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2 = 0.95 NOISE_MULTIPLIERS = (0.25, 1.0, 4.0) DECODE_READOUTS = ("linear", "rbf") DECODE_ARMS = ("FULL", "RING_ONLY", "HOLO_ONLY", "NULL") MEDIUM_ARMS = ("FULL", "RING_ONLY", "HOLO_ONLY") # ----------------------------------------------------------------- targets def padded_magnitude(shape: np.ndarray, half: int = HALF) -> np.ndarray:     """|rfft2| of the shape centred in a ``half`` x ``half`` canvas: invariant to any placement     that keeps the shape inside the canvas (the jitter), and identical to what the engineered     ``fft_concat`` features contain for that half."""     s = shape.shape[0]     canvas = np.zeros((half, half))     o = (half - s) // 2     canvas[o:o + s, o:o + s] = shape     return np.abs(np.fft.rfft2(canvas)).ravel() @lru_cache(maxsize=4) def shape_basis(n_components: int = N_COMPONENTS, n_pool: int = 2000, size: int = 24,                 smooth: float = 3.0, seed: int = 424242) -> dict:     """PCA basis of the Fourier-magnitude descriptors, fitted on an independent shape pool."""     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     M = np.stack([padded_magnitude(random_shape(rng, size, smooth)) for _ in range(n_pool)])     mean = M.mean(axis=0)     _, sv, Vt = np.linalg.svd(M - mean, full_matrices=False)     var = sv ** 2 / (n_pool - 1)     return {"mean": mean, "components": Vt[:n_components], "explained": var[:n_components] / var.sum()} def shape_targets(cfg, basis: dict) -> np.ndarray:     """(n_trials, 2 * k): descriptor scores of the top shape then the bottom shape."""     rows = []     for i in range(cfg.n_trials):         a, b = trial_shapes(cfg, i)         rows.append(np.concatenate([(padded_magnitude(s) - basis["mean"]) @ basis["components"].T                                     for s in (a, b)]))     return np.array(rows) def position_targets(cfg) -> np.ndarray:     """(n_trials, 4): dy and dx of the top shape, then of the bottom shape."""     return np.array([draw_offsets(cfg, i).ravel() for i in range(cfg.n_trials)], dtype=float) # --------------------------------------------------------------- regression class KernelRidgeRegressor:     """Multi-output kernel ridge; outputs are standardised so they weigh equally in the     leave-one-out selection of (kernel width, alpha), which uses the training fold only."""     def __init__(self, kernel: str = "linear", alphas=None, gammas=DEFAULT_GAMMAS):         if kernel not in ("linear", "rbf"):             raise ValueError("kernel must be 'linear' or 'rbf'")         self.kernel = kernel         self.alphas = DEFAULT_ALPHAS if alphas is None else np.asarray(alphas, float)         self.gammas = tuple(gammas)     def _candidates(self, Xs):         if self.kernel == "linear":             yield None, Xs @ Xs.T         else:             d2 = squared_distances(Xs, Xs)             self.median_d2_ = float(np.median(d2[~np.eye(len(Xs), dtype=bool)])) + 1e-12             for g in self.gammas:                 yield g, rbf_kernel(d2, g, self.median_d2_)     def fit(self, X, Y) -> "KernelRidgeRegressor":         X, Y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(Y, float)         if Y.ndim == 1:             Y = Y[:, None]         if X.ndim != 2 or X.shape[0] != Y.shape[0] or X.shape[0] < 4:             raise ValueError("X must be 2-D with >= 4 rows matching Y")         self.mean_ = X.mean(axis=0)         Xc = X - self.mean_         self.scale_ = Xc.std() + 1e-12         Xs = Xc / self.scale_         self.y_mean_, self.y_std_ = Y.mean(axis=0), Y.std(axis=0) + 1e-12         Yc = (Y - self.y_mean_) / self.y_std_         best = (np.inf, None, None, None)         for param, K in self._candidates(Xs):             eig = np.linalg.eigh(K)             for a in self.alphas:                 err = float(np.mean(loo_residuals(K, Yc, a, eig) ** 2))                 if err < best[0]:                     best = (err, param, float(a), eig)         self.loo_error_, self.param_, self.alpha_, (evals, V) = best         self.dual_ = V @ ((V.T @ Yc) / (np.clip(evals, 0.0, None) + self.alpha_)[:, None])         self.Xs_ = Xs         return self     def predict(self, X) -> np.ndarray:         Xs = (np.asarray(X, float) - self.mean_) / self.scale_         if self.kernel == "linear":             Kt = Xs @ self.Xs_.T         else:             Kt = rbf_kernel(squared_distances(Xs, self.Xs_), self.param_, self.median_d2_)         return (Kt @ self.dual_) * self.y_std_ + self.y_mean_ def make_regressor(name: str):     if name not in DECODE_READOUTS:         raise ValueError(f"unknown regressor '{name}'")     return lambda: KernelRidgeRegressor(name) def kfold_oof(X, Y, make, k: int = 5, seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:     """Out-of-fold predictions: every row is predicted by a model that never saw it."""     X, Y = np.asarray(X, float), np.asarray(Y, float)     if Y.ndim == 1:         Y = Y[:, None]     perm = np.random.default_rng(seed).permutation(len(X))     pred = np.empty_like(Y)     for te in np.array_split(perm, k):         tr = np.setdiff1d(perm, te)         pred[te] = make().fit(X[tr], Y[tr]).predict(X[te])     return pred def squared_errors(X, Y, make, k: int = 5, repeats: int = 3, seed: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:     """Per-sample, per-output squared out-of-fold error, averaged over repeated splits."""     Y = np.asarray(Y, float)     return np.mean([(Y - kfold_oof(X, Y, make, k, seed + 100 * r)) ** 2 for r in range(repeats)], axis=0) # --------------------------------------------------------- R-squared and CIs def r2_from_errors(E: np.ndarray, Y: np.ndarray) -> float:     """Mean over outputs of 1 - SSE_j / SST_j (outputs with no variance are ignored)."""     sst = ((Y - Y.mean(axis=0)) ** 2).sum(axis=0)     ok = sst > 1e-12     return float(np.mean(1.0 - E.sum(axis=0)[ok] / sst[ok])) def r2_per_output(E: np.ndarray, Y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     sst = ((Y - Y.mean(axis=0)) ** 2).sum(axis=0)     return 1.0 - E.sum(axis=0) / np.maximum(sst, 1e-12) def bootstrap_r2(E_a: np.ndarray, Y: np.ndarray, E_b: np.ndarray | None = None, n_boot: int = 2000,                  seed: int = 0, level: float = 0.95) -> dict:     """CI of R2(a), or of R2(a) - R2(b) when ``E_b`` is given.  The SAME resampled trials are used     for both, which keeps the pairing."""     Y = np.asarray(Y, float)     if Y.ndim == 1:         Y = Y[:, None]     n = len(Y)     idx = np.random.default_rng(seed).integers(0, n, size=(n_boot, n))     sst_i = (Y - Y.mean(axis=0)) ** 2     sst = sst_i[idx].sum(axis=1)                                   # (B, m)     ok = sst.min(axis=0) > 1e-12     r2 = lambda E: 1.0 - E[idx].sum(axis=1)[:, ok] / sst[:, ok]     boot = r2(E_a) if E_b is None else r2(E_a) - r2(E_b)     boot = boot.mean(axis=1)     point = r2_from_errors(E_a, Y) - (0.0 if E_b is None else r2_from_errors(E_b, Y))     lo, hi = np.quantile(boot, [(1 - level) / 2, 1 - (1 - level) / 2])     return {"point": float(point), "lo": float(lo), "hi": float(hi)} # --------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def decodable(sres: dict, group: str, noise: str, min_r2: float = MIN_R2) -> list:     """(arm, readout) pairs of the medium arms with R2 >= min_r2 AND a paired-bootstrap interval of     (R2 - R2 on NULL) entirely above 0 (Bonferroni-adjusted level)."""     found = []     for arm in MEDIUM_ARMS:         if arm not in sres["arms"]:             continue         for r, cell in sres["arms"][arm]["noise"][noise][group].items():             if cell["r2"] >= min_r2 and cell["diff_vs_null"]["lo"] > 0:                 found.append((arm, r))     return found def verdict(sres: dict, base_noise: str = "1.0", low_noise: str = "0.25") -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED        the safety clip fired in any arm.     -  INCONCLUSIVE           FULL or NULL missing.     1. POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS the engineered Fourier features do not decode the shape descriptors                               (R2 < 0.95): the target construction or the regression is broken.     2. CONTROL_FAILS          the NULL arm (identical trials) has R2 > 0.05 for any readout/target:                               a pipeline leak.     3. SHAPES_DECODABLE       some medium arm / readout has shape R2 >= 0.10 with the adjusted                               interval of (R2 - R2_NULL) above 0, at the base noise.     4. POSITION_ONLY          shapes are not decodable but placement is (same criterion).     5. NOTHING_DECODABLE      neither is.     For outcomes 4 and 5 the qualifier says whether shapes become decodable at 0.25x noise     ("buried-by-noise") or not even then ("absent-at-low-noise").     """     arms = sres["arms"]     clipped = [a for a in arms if arms[a]["stats"]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if "FULL" not in arms or "NULL" not in arms:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "Needs FULL and NULL.", "next": "Run with both."}     pc = sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"]["shape"]["linear"]["r2"]     if pc < POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2:         return {"outcome": "POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"Fourier features decode the shape descriptors with R2 = {pc:.3f} < "                         f"{POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2}: the targets or the regression are broken.",                 "next": "Fix the target construction before interpreting anything."}     leaks = [(g, r) for g in ("shape", "position") for r, c in arms["NULL"]["noise"][base_noise][g].items()              if c["r2"] > 0.05]     if leaks:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"NULL (identical trials) has R2 > 0.05 for {leaks}: the pipeline leaks.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel first."}     shapes = decodable(sres, "shape", base_noise)     if shapes:         best = max(shapes, key=lambda ar: arms[ar[0]]["noise"][base_noise]["shape"][ar[1]]["r2"])         r2 = arms[best[0]]["noise"][base_noise]["shape"][best[1]]["r2"]         return {"outcome": "SHAPES_DECODABLE", "qualifier": "present",                 "text": f"Shapes are decodable from the medium (best: {best[0]}/{best[1]}, R2 = {r2:.2f}; "                         f"arms: {sorted({a for a, _ in shapes})}).",                 "next": "The comparison readout is the weak link: try lower noise, more sensors or a "                         "better probe, and decode the relation between the shapes directly."}     low = decodable(sres, "shape", low_noise)     qualifier = "buried-by-noise" if low else "absent-at-low-noise"     tail = (f" Shapes become decodable at {low_noise}x noise ({sorted({a for a, _ in low})})." if low             else f" Not even at {low_noise}x noise.")     pos = decodable(sres, "position", base_noise)     if pos:         return {"outcome": "POSITION_ONLY", "qualifier": qualifier,                 "text": "Placement is decodable from the medium but shapes are not." + tail,                 "next": "The medium senses where energy went, not what shape it was; stop pursuing "                         "static-pattern comparison and test memory over time."}     return {"outcome": "NOTHING_DECODABLE", "qualifier": qualifier,             "text": "Neither shapes nor placement are decodable from the medium." + tail,             "next": "Check probe, sensors and noise before drawing physical conclusions."} # -------------------------------------------------------------------- runner def _features_at(ds, mult: float) -> np.ndarray:     return ds.features(noise_std=ds.cfg.sensor_noise * mult) def run_decoding(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), arms=DECODE_ARMS, readouts=DECODE_READOUTS,                  noise_multipliers=NOISE_MULTIPLIERS, cache_dir=None, n_boot: int = 2000,                  k: int = 5, repeats: int = 3, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     basis = shape_basis()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "readouts": list(readouts),                       "noise_multipliers": list(noise_multipliers), "n_components": N_COMPONENTS,                       "min_r2": MIN_R2, "n_boot": n_boot,                       "explained_variance": [float(v) for v in basis["explained"]]}, "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         cfg = rand_arm_config("FULL", seed, n_trials)         targets = {"shape": shape_targets(cfg, basis), "position": position_targets(cfg)}         datasets = {}         for arm in arms:             datasets[arm], _ = get_rand_dataset(arm, seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)         level = 1.0 - 0.05 / (max(1, len([a for a in arms if a in MEDIUM_ARMS])) * len(readouts))         # squared errors for every (arm, noise, group, readout)         E = {}         for arm, ds in datasets.items():             for mult in noise_multipliers:                 X = _features_at(ds, mult)                 for g, Y in targets.items():                     for r in readouts:                         E[(arm, str(mult), g, r)] = squared_errors(X, Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)         sres = {"arms": {}, "baselines": {}, "family_level": level}         for arm, ds in datasets.items():             sres["arms"][arm] = {"stats": {**{kk: float(v) for kk, v in ds.stats.items()},                                            "clean_spread": float(ds.features(0.0).std(axis=0).max())},                                  "noise": {}}             for mult in noise_multipliers:                 cells = {}                 for g, Y in targets.items():                     cells[g] = {}                     for r in readouts:                         Ea = E[(arm, str(mult), g, r)]                         ci = bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed, level=0.95)                         d = (bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, E[("NULL", str(mult), g, r)], n_boot, seed, level)                              if "NULL" in datasets and arm != "NULL" else {"point": 0.0, "lo": 0.0, "hi": 0.0})                         cells[g][r] = {"r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"],                                        "diff_vs_null": {"diff": d["point"], "lo": d["lo"], "hi": d["hi"]},                                        "per_output": [float(v) for v in r2_per_output(Ea, Y)]}                 sres["arms"][arm]["noise"][str(mult)] = cells         ref = datasets[arms[0]]         for name in ("fft_concat", "pooled", "raw"):             Xb = BASELINE_SETS[name](ref.raw)             sres["baselines"][name] = {}             for g, Y in targets.items():                 sres["baselines"][name][g] = {}                 for r in readouts:                     Eb = squared_errors(Xb, Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)                     ci = bootstrap_r2(Eb, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)                     sres["baselines"][name][g][r] = {"r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"],                                                      "per_output": [float(v) for v in r2_per_output(Eb, Y)]}         base_key = "1.0" if "1.0" in sres["arms"][arms[0]]["noise"] else str(noise_multipliers[0])         low_key = str(min(noise_multipliers))         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres, base_noise=base_key, low_noise=low_key)         res["seeds"][seed] = sres         if verbose:             v = sres["verdict"]             print(f"[seed {seed}] {v['outcome']}: {v['text']}", flush=True)     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain shape-decoding diagnostic (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, "              f"{c['n_components']} descriptors per shape explaining "              f"{100 * sum(c['explained_variance']):.0f}% of descriptor variance, min R2 {c['min_r2']})"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         base = "1.0" if "1.0" in next(iter(s["arms"].values()))["noise"] else sorted(next(iter(s["arms"].values()))["noise"])[0]         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}, noise x{base}: cross-validated R2 [95% CI]; diff = R2 - R2(NULL) [adj. CI] ===",                   f"{'features':<24}" + "".join(f"{'shape/' + r:>26}" for r in rd) + "".join(f"{'position/' + r:>26}" for r in rd),                   "-" * (24 + 26 * 2 * len(rd))]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             cells = a["noise"][base]             row = f"{arm:<24}"             for g in ("shape", "position"):                 for r in rd:                     x = cells[g][r]                     row += f"{x['r2']:+.2f} [{x['r2_lo']:+.2f},{x['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(26)             lines.append(row)         for name, b in s["baselines"].items():             row = f"{name + ' (engineered)':<24}"             for g in ("shape", "position"):                 for r in rd:                     x = b[g][r]                     row += f"{x['r2']:+.2f} [{x['r2_lo']:+.2f},{x['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(26)             lines.append(row)         lines += ["", "gain over NULL, shape, base noise [adjusted CI]:"]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             if arm != "NULL":                 lines.append(f"  {arm:<10}" + "  ".join(                     f"{r} {a['noise'][base]['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['diff']:+.3f} "                     f"[{a['noise'][base]['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['lo']:+.3f},{a['noise'][base]['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['hi']:+.3f}]"                     for r in rd))         mults = c["noise_multipliers"]         lines += ["", "noise sweep, shape R2 (readout: " + "/".join(rd) + "):",                   f"{'arm':<12}" + "".join(f"{'x' + str(m):>20}" for m in mults)]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             lines.append(f"{arm:<12}" + "".join(                 ("  ".join(f"{a['noise'][str(m)]['shape'][r]['r2']:+.2f}" for r in rd)).rjust(20) for m in mults))         f = s["arms"]["FULL"]["noise"][base]["shape"][rd[0]]["per_output"]         lines += ["", f"FULL/{rd[0]} shape R2 per descriptor (top PC1..{c['n_components']}, bottom PC1..{c['n_components']}): "                   + " ".join(f"{v:+.2f}" for v in f)]         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}): {v['outcome']}" + (f"  [{v['qualifier']}]" if "qualifier" in v else ""),                   f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     cmb = res["combined"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {cmb['outcome']}  (per seed: {cmb['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--arms", default=",".join(DECODE_ARMS))     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None, help="directory holding the random-shape datasets")     ap.add_argument("--boot", type=int, default=2000)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_decoding(a.trials, tuple(int(s) for s in a.seeds.split(",")), tuple(a.arms.split(",")),                        cache_dir=a.cache, n_boot=a.boot)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/noisecheck.py` ```python """Is the v9 same/different failure just sensor noise?   ``python -m wavebrain.noisecheck`` The decoding diagnostic found that shape information IS present in the medium's response but only partly recoverable (R2 about 0.2 at the base noise, about 0.4 at a quarter of it).  If each shape is that noisy, comparing two of them would be weak even if the physics were fine.  This follow-up re-runs the v9 same/different readouts on the cached datasets with the sensor noise re-added at several multipliers (including none at all); no new simulation is needed. Pre-registered rule (``verdict``), fixed before it was run:   NOISE_IS_THE_LIMIT  at a noise multiplier <= 0.25 some readout of FULL or RING_ONLY reaches                       accuracy >= 0.65 with a Wilson 95% lower bound > 0.55;   NOT_NOISE           otherwise: even with little or no sensor noise the comparison cannot be read                       out, so the limit is what the medium keeps or what the readouts can use. The engineered Fourier baseline (noise-free by construction) is shown as the reference. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time from .pilot import strip_private from .randstudy import BASELINE_SETS, get_rand_dataset from .readouts import READOUTS from .study import combine_verdicts, evaluate_readout NOISE_GRID = (0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 4.0) SWEEP_ARMS = ("FULL", "RING_ONLY") MIN_ACC, MIN_LOWER, LOW_NOISE = 0.65, 0.55, 0.25 def verdict(sres: dict) -> dict:     arms = sres["arms"]     clipped = [a for a in arms if arms[a]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if "FULL" not in arms:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "Needs the FULL arm.", "next": "Include FULL."}     hits = [(a, float(m), r) for a, d in arms.items() for m, cells in d["noise"].items() if float(m) <= LOW_NOISE             for r, c in cells.items() if c["acc"] >= MIN_ACC and c["ci_low"] > MIN_LOWER]     if hits:         worst = max(hits, key=lambda h: h[1])         return {"outcome": "NOISE_IS_THE_LIMIT", "hits": hits,                 "text": f"At noise x{worst[1]:g} or lower, {worst[2]} on {worst[0]} reaches the criterion "                         f"({len(hits)} qualifying cells).",                 "next": "Improve the signal-to-noise ratio (more sensors, a stronger probe, averaging) and "                         "re-test the comparison."}     free = max((c["acc"] for d in arms.values() for c in d["noise"].get("0.0", {}).values()), default=float("nan"))     return {"outcome": "NOT_NOISE", "hits": [],             "text": f"No readout reaches the criterion at noise x{LOW_NOISE:g} or lower (best noise-free "                     f"accuracy {free:.2f}).",             "next": "Shape information is present but not in a form these readouts can compare; test "                     "different readouts or tasks where memory over time matters."} def run_noise_sweep(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), arms=SWEEP_ARMS, mults=NOISE_GRID,                     readouts=READOUTS, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "arms": list(arms),                       "mults": list(mults), "readouts": list(readouts)}, "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         sres = {"arms": {}, "reference": {}}         ref = None         for arm in arms:             ds, _ = get_rand_dataset(arm, seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)             ref = ref or ds             sres["arms"][arm] = {"clip_events": float(ds.stats["clip_events"]), "noise": {}}             for m in mults:                 X = ds.features(noise_std=ds.cfg.sensor_noise * m)                 sres["arms"][arm]["noise"][str(m)] = {r: evaluate_readout(X, ds.y, r, seed, 0) for r in readouts}             if verbose:                 best = {str(m): max(c["acc"] for c in sres["arms"][arm]["noise"][str(m)].values()) for m in mults}                 print(f"[seed {seed}] {arm:<10} best accuracy by noise multiplier: {best}", flush=True)         Xf = BASELINE_SETS["fft_concat"](ref.raw)         sres["reference"]["fft_concat"] = {r: evaluate_readout(Xf, ref.y, r, seed, 0) for r in readouts}         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres)         res["seeds"][seed] = sres     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain same/different accuracy vs sensor noise (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, "              f"chance 0.50; criterion: accuracy >= {MIN_ACC} with Wilson lower bound > {MIN_LOWER} at x{LOW_NOISE:g} noise or less)"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: accuracy [95% CI] ===",                   f"{'arm / noise multiplier':<26}" + "".join(f"{r:>22}" for r in rd), "-" * (26 + 22 * len(rd))]         for arm, a in s["arms"].items():             for m, cells in a["noise"].items():                 lines.append(f"{arm + ' x' + m:<26}" + "".join(                     f"{cells[r]['acc']:.2f} [{cells[r]['ci_low']:.2f},{cells[r]['ci_high']:.2f}]".rjust(22) for r in rd))         lines.append(f"{'FFT magnitude (reference)':<26}" + "".join(             f"{s['reference']['fft_concat'][r]['acc']:.2f} [{s['reference']['fft_concat'][r]['ci_low']:.2f},"             f"{s['reference']['fft_concat'][r]['ci_high']:.2f}]".rjust(22) for r in rd))         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}): {v['outcome']}", f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {res['combined']['outcome']}  (per seed: {res['combined']['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--arms", default=",".join(SWEEP_ARMS))     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_noise_sweep(a.trials, tuple(int(s) for s in a.seeds.split(",")), tuple(a.arms.split(",")),                           cache_dir=a.cache)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/statedecode.py` ```python """Can the stored STATE be decoded, bypassing probe and sensors?   ``python -m wavebrain.statedecode`` v10 found that the probe readout recovers shape information only partially (R2 about 0.22) and that the plastic hologram contributes almost nothing to it.  Two explanations fit and call for different fixes: (1) the hologram stores little shape information (the plasticity rule is the problem), or (2) it stores it but 24 noisy sensors cannot read it (the readout is the bottleneck). This module separates them by decoding the same targets from the state itself at the instant the probe would start: the hologram H, the fast field u and the slow field s, pooled over the interior. An UPPER BOUND, not a mechanism: the physical system has no access to H.  There is no readout noise. Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import hashlib import json import time from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from .decode import (DECODE_READOUTS, bootstrap_r2, make_regressor, position_targets, r2_per_output,                      shape_basis, shape_targets, squared_errors) from .pilot import strip_private from .randshapes import build_rand_raw, draw_offsets, rand_write_phase, trial_shapes from .randstudy import BASELINE_SETS, rand_arm_config from .study import combine_verdicts POOL = 4                       # 4 x 4 average pooling of the 76 x 76 interior -> 19 x 19 = 361 values N_SUB = 24                     # bandwidth control: as many values as there were sensors SUB_SEED = 7 GOOD_R2, POOR_R2 = 0.5, 0.2 STATE_CONTROL_MIN = 0.25       # pooled written map must decode placement at least this well (tuning value: 0.35) POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2 = 0.95 STATE_NAMES = ("H", "u_full", "s_full", "u_ring") VARIANTS = ("pool4", "sub24") # ---------------------------------------------------------------- state capture def interior_pool(field: np.ndarray, sponge: int, pool: int = POOL) -> np.ndarray:     """Average-pool the part of ``field`` inside the absorbing layer (the layer itself is excluded)."""     inner = np.asarray(field, float)[sponge:-sponge, sponge:-sponge]     h, w = inner.shape     if h % pool or w % pool:         raise ValueError(f"interior {h}x{w} is not divisible by pool {pool}")     return inner.reshape(h // pool, pool, w // pool, pool).mean(axis=(1, 3)).ravel() def snapshot_trial(cfg, index: int) -> tuple[dict, dict]:     """Write the trial's shapes, let the medium rest for the delay, and snapshot the state at the     moment the probe would start (no probe is run)."""     sim, info = rand_write_phase(cfg, index)     sim.run(cfg.base.delay_steps)     sp = cfg.base.medium.sponge_width     return ({"H": interior_pool(sim.H, sp), "u": interior_pool(sim.u, sp), "s": interior_pool(sim.s, sp)},             {"clip_events": sim.clip_events, "peak_u": info["peak_u"]}) def build_state_arrays(cfg, verbose: bool = False) -> dict:     snaps, infos = [], []     t0 = time.time()     for i in range(cfg.n_trials):         s, info = snapshot_trial(cfg, i)         snaps.append(s); infos.append(info)         if verbose and (i + 1) % 60 == 0:             print(f"    trial {i + 1}/{cfg.n_trials} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)     return {"H": np.stack([s["H"] for s in snaps]), "u": np.stack([s["u"] for s in snaps]),             "s": np.stack([s["s"] for s in snaps]), "clip_events": float(sum(d["clip_events"] for d in infos)),             "peak_u": float(np.mean([d["peak_u"] for d in infos]))} def state_key(cfg) -> str:     return hashlib.sha256((repr(cfg) + f"|state|pool{POOL}").encode()).hexdigest()[:16] def get_state_arrays(arm: str, seed: int, n_trials: int, cache_dir=None, verbose: bool = False):     """(arrays, from_cache); a stale or missing cache entry is rebuilt, never reused."""     cfg = rand_arm_config(arm, seed, n_trials)     path = None     if cache_dir is not None:         Path(cache_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)         path = Path(cache_dir) / f"state_{arm}_seed{seed}_n{n_trials}.npz"         if path.exists():             with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as z:                 if str(z["key"]) == state_key(cfg):                     return {k: (z[k] if z[k].ndim else float(z[k])) for k in z.files if k != "key"}, True             if verbose:                 print(f"    [cache] stale entry for {arm}/seed {seed}: rebuilding", flush=True)     arrays = build_state_arrays(cfg, verbose)     if path is not None:         np.savez(path, key=state_key(cfg), **arrays)     return arrays, False def written_map(cfg, index: int) -> np.ndarray:     """The pooled map of the written shapes themselves: a state that encodes shape and placement     BY CONSTRUCTION.  It validates the pooling + regression chain end to end (a reference, not a     mechanism: the physical system has no such state)."""     m, b = cfg.base.medium, cfg.base     grid = np.zeros((m.ny, m.nx))     for pat, (dy, dx), cy in zip(trial_shapes(cfg, index), draw_offsets(cfg, index), cfg.centres):         y0 = cy - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dy)         x0 = m.nx // 2 - b.pattern_size // 2 + int(dx)         grid[y0:y0 + b.pattern_size, x0:x0 + b.pattern_size] += pat     return interior_pool(grid, m.sponge_width) def build_written_maps(cfg) -> np.ndarray:     return np.stack([written_map(cfg, i) for i in range(cfg.n_trials)]) def sub_indices(n_features: int, n: int = N_SUB, seed: int = SUB_SEED) -> np.ndarray:     return np.sort(np.random.default_rng(seed).choice(n_features, n, replace=False)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def best_shape(sres: dict, name: str, variant: str = "pool4") -> tuple[float, bool]:     """(best shape R2 over readouts, whether its paired interval versus NULL is entirely above 0)."""     cells = sres["states"][name][variant]["shape"]     r = max(cells, key=lambda k: cells[k]["r2"])     return cells[r]["r2"], cells[r]["diff_vs_null"]["lo"] > 0 def verdict(sres: dict) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED          the safety clip fired.     -  INCONCLUSIVE             H or NULL missing.     1. POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS   engineered Fourier features do not decode the descriptors (R2 < 0.95).     2. CONTROL_FAILS            NULL (state all zero) has R2 > 0.05 for any target or readout: a leak.     3. STATE_CONTROL_FAILS      the pooled WRITTEN map (a state that encodes shape and placement by                                 construction) does not decode placement (best R2 < 0.25): the pooling +                                 regression chain is broken.  (The first version of this rule gated on                                 H's own placement R2 >= 0.2, on the assumption that a hologram is an                                 intensity map of the shapes.  On the tuning seed it failed; the capture                                 was verified exact and H turned out to be dense accumulated wave                                 interference whose centre of mass tracks placement only weakly and with                                 the wrong sign.  The gate was replaced before any reported run, and H's                                 own placement R2 is now reported, not gated.)     Then, with h = best shape R2 of H (pool4; counted only if its adjusted interval versus NULL is     above 0) and probe = the v10 probe-based R2 of the same trials:     4. READOUT_IS_BOTTLENECK    h >= 0.5 and probe < 0.5: the hologram stores the shapes, the probe                                 and sensors cannot read them.        H_DECODES                h >= 0.5 and no probe reference given.     5. PLASTICITY_DISCARDS      h < 0.2: even with full access the hologram does not hold the shapes.     6. H_PARTIAL                otherwise.     A qualifier reports the fast field: ``field-decodable`` (>= 0.5 for u_full or u_ring), ``field-partial``     (>= 0.2) or ``field-poor``.     """     st = sres["states"]     clipped = [n for n in st if st[n]["stats"]["clip_events"] > 0]     if clipped:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": f"Safety clip fired in {', '.join(clipped)}.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and rerun."}     if "H" not in st or "NULL" not in st:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "Needs H and NULL.", "next": "Run with both."}     pc = sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"]["shape"]["linear"]["r2"]     if pc < POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2:         return {"outcome": "POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"Fourier features decode the descriptors with R2 = {pc:.3f} < {POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2}.",                 "next": "Fix the target construction first."}     leaks = [(v, g, r) for v in VARIANTS for g in ("shape", "position")              for r, c in st["NULL"][v][g].items() if c["r2"] > 0.05]     if leaks:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS", "text": f"NULL (state all zero) has R2 > 0.05 for {leaks}: a leak.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel first."}     mp = max(c["r2"] for c in sres["baselines"]["map"]["position"].values())     if mp < STATE_CONTROL_MIN:         return {"outcome": "STATE_CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"The pooled written map decodes placement with R2 = {mp:.2f} < {STATE_CONTROL_MIN}: the "                         f"pooling/regression chain is broken.",                 "next": "Fix the pipeline before interpreting any state."}     hp = max(c["r2"] for c in st["H"]["pool4"]["position"].values())     h, ok = best_shape(sres, "H")     h = h if ok else 0.0     fields = [best_shape(sres, n)[0] if best_shape(sres, n)[1] else 0.0 for n in ("u_full", "u_ring") if n in st]     f = max(fields) if fields else 0.0     qualifier = "field-decodable" if f >= GOOD_R2 else "field-partial" if f >= POOR_R2 else "field-poor"     sub = max(c["r2"] for c in st["H"]["sub24"]["shape"].values())     tail = (f" With only {N_SUB} values of H: R2 = {sub:.2f}. Best field R2 = {f:.2f}. H's own placement R2 = {hp:.2f}; "             f"the pooled written map reaches {mp:.2f} and {max(c['r2'] for c in sres['baselines']['map']['shape'].values()):.2f} "             f"for placement and shape.")     probe = sres.get("probe_r2")     if h >= GOOD_R2:         if probe is not None and probe < GOOD_R2:             return {"outcome": "READOUT_IS_BOTTLENECK", "qualifier": qualifier,                     "text": f"H decodes shapes (R2 = {h:.2f}) while the probe readout reached {probe:.2f}: the "                             f"hologram stores the shapes and the probe/sensors cannot read them." + tail,                     "next": "Improve the readout channel (more sensors, stronger holo_mod, a better probe); "                             "then run retention curves over delays."}         return {"outcome": "H_DECODES", "qualifier": qualifier,                 "text": f"H decodes shapes (R2 = {h:.2f}); no probe reference was given." + tail,                 "next": "Compare with the probe-based R2 before concluding."}     if h < POOR_R2:         return {"outcome": "PLASTICITY_DISCARDS", "qualifier": qualifier,                 "text": f"Even with full access H decodes shapes poorly (R2 = {h:.2f})." + tail,                 "next": "Redesign the write rule (for example a reference-beam hologram) before more readout work."}     return {"outcome": "H_PARTIAL", "qualifier": qualifier,             "text": f"H decodes shapes partly (R2 = {h:.2f})." + tail,             "next": "Quantify retention over delays and compare with the field before redesigning."} # ------------------------------------------------------------------- the study def load_probe_reference(path, seed) -> float | None:     """Best probe-based shape R2 (FULL arm, base noise) of the v10 decoding for this seed."""     p = Path(path)     if not p.exists():         return None     cells = json.loads(p.read_text())["seeds"][str(seed)]["arms"]["FULL"]["noise"]["1.0"]["shape"]     return float(max(c["r2"] for c in cells.values())) def run_statedecode(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), readouts=DECODE_READOUTS, cache_dir=None,                     probe_json=None, n_boot: int = 2000, k: int = 5, repeats: int = 3,                     verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     basis = shape_basis()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "readouts": list(readouts), "pool": POOL,                       "n_sub": N_SUB, "good_r2": GOOD_R2, "poor_r2": POOR_R2, "n_boot": n_boot}, "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         cfg = rand_arm_config("FULL", seed, n_trials)         targets = {"shape": shape_targets(cfg, basis), "position": position_targets(cfg)}         full, _ = get_state_arrays("FULL", seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)         ring, _ = get_state_arrays("RING_ONLY", seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)         feats = {"H": full["H"], "u_full": full["u"], "s_full": full["s"], "u_ring": ring["u"],                  "NULL": np.zeros_like(full["H"])}         clips = {"H": full["clip_events"], "u_full": full["clip_events"], "s_full": full["clip_events"],                  "u_ring": ring["clip_events"], "NULL": 0.0}         idx = sub_indices(full["H"].shape[1])         level = 1.0 - 0.05 / (len(STATE_NAMES) * len(readouts))         E = {(n, v, g, r): squared_errors(X if v == "pool4" else X[:, idx], Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)              for n, X in feats.items() for v in VARIANTS for g, Y in targets.items() for r in readouts}         sres = {"states": {}, "baselines": {}, "family_level": level, "probe_r2": None}         if probe_json:             sres["probe_r2"] = load_probe_reference(probe_json, seed)         for n in feats:             sres["states"][n] = {"stats": {"clip_events": float(clips[n])}}             for v in VARIANTS:                 sres["states"][n][v] = {}                 for g, Y in targets.items():                     sres["states"][n][v][g] = {}                     for r in readouts:                         Ea = E[(n, v, g, r)]                         ci = bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)                         d = (bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, E[("NULL", v, g, r)], n_boot, seed, level) if n != "NULL"                              else {"point": 0.0, "lo": 0.0, "hi": 0.0})                         sres["states"][n][v][g][r] = {                             "r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"],                             "diff_vs_null": {"diff": d["point"], "lo": d["lo"], "hi": d["hi"]},                             "per_output": [float(x) for x in r2_per_output(Ea, Y)]}         raw, _ = build_rand_raw(cfg)         for name in ("fft_concat", "pooled", "map"):             Xb = build_written_maps(cfg) if name == "map" else BASELINE_SETS[name](raw)             sres["baselines"][name] = {}             for g, Y in targets.items():                 sres["baselines"][name][g] = {}                 for r in readouts:                     Eb = squared_errors(Xb, Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)                     ci = bootstrap_r2(Eb, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)                     sres["baselines"][name][g][r] = {"r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"]}         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres)         res["seeds"][seed] = sres         if verbose:             print(f"[seed {seed}] {sres['verdict']['outcome']}: {sres['verdict']['text']}", flush=True)     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain state-decoding upper bound (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, "              f"{c['pool']}x{c['pool']} pooling -> 361 values; sub = {c['n_sub']} random values; "              f"good R2 >= {c['good_r2']}, poor < {c['poor_r2']})"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: cross-validated R2 [95% CI] ===",                   f"{'state / variant':<20}" + "".join(f"{'shape/' + r:>25}" for r in rd) + "".join(f"{'position/' + r:>25}" for r in rd),                   "-" * (20 + 25 * 2 * len(rd))]         for n, d in s["states"].items():             for v in VARIANTS:                 row = f"{n + ' ' + v:<20}"                 for g in ("shape", "position"):                     for r in rd:                         x = d[v][g][r]                         row += f"{x['r2']:+.2f} [{x['r2_lo']:+.2f},{x['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(25)                 lines.append(row)         for name, b in s["baselines"].items():             row = f"{name + (' (known content)' if name == 'map' else ' (engineered)'):<20}"             for g in ("shape", "position"):                 for r in rd:                     x = b[g][r]                     row += f"{x['r2']:+.2f} [{x['r2_lo']:+.2f},{x['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(25)             lines.append(row)         if s.get("probe_r2") is not None:             lines.append(f"{'probe readout (v10)':<20}shape R2 of the same trials, FULL arm, base noise: {s['probe_r2']:+.2f}")         lines += ["", "gain over NULL, shape, pool4 [adjusted CI]:"]         for n in STATE_NAMES:             if n in s["states"]:                 lines.append(f"  {n:<8}" + "  ".join(                     f"{r} {s['states'][n]['pool4']['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['diff']:+.3f} "                     f"[{s['states'][n]['pool4']['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['lo']:+.3f},"                     f"{s['states'][n]['pool4']['shape'][r]['diff_vs_null']['hi']:+.3f}]" for r in rd))         h = s["states"]["H"]["pool4"]["shape"]         best = max(h, key=lambda r: h[r]["r2"])         lines += ["", f"H pool4 / {best} shape R2 per descriptor (top PC1..4, bottom PC1..4): "                   + " ".join(f"{v:+.2f}" for v in h[best]["per_output"])]         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}): {v['outcome']}" + (f"  [{v['qualifier']}]" if "qualifier" in v else ""),                   f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {res['combined']['outcome']}  (per seed: {res['combined']['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None, help="directory for the cached state arrays")     ap.add_argument("--probe-json", default=None, help="decode_results.json of the v10 diagnostic")     ap.add_argument("--boot", type=int, default=2000)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_statedecode(a.trials, tuple(int(x) for x in a.seeds.split(",")), cache_dir=a.cache,                           probe_json=a.probe_json, n_boot=a.boot)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` #### `wavebrain/modmap.py` ```python """Does the probe's access to the stored state explain what it recovers?   ``python -m wavebrain.modmap`` v11 decoded shapes from the stored state (hologram H, fast field u, slow field s) with no probe and no noise: H partly (R2 about 0.35), the fields much better (about 0.6), while the probe path recovered only 0.22.  It left a HYPOTHESIS: the probe couples to the medium through the local wave speed, c2 = c0 * (1 + slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H), while the linear effect of the fast field is removed by the paired baseline subtraction.  This module tests it on the cached state arrays (no new simulation) and makes the comparison amplitude-aware. * ``mod``       slow_mod * s + holo_mod * H of the FULL arm: what the probe couples to (before the clip) * ``mod_s``     slow_mod * s alone;   ``mod_H``  holo_mod * H alone * ``mod_ring``  slow_mod * s of the hologram-off arm (what the probe can see in RING_ONLY) * ``u_full``, ``u_ring``  the fast fields (invisible to the probe at first order) * ``NULL``      zeros: the negative control (noise only) Readout noise is added to the pooled values at multiples of the sensor noise (0.015), the same noise realisation for every state, so a small, decaying signal loses information fairly. Pre-registered decision rule: see ``verdict``. """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import time from pathlib import Path import numpy as np from .decode import (DECODE_READOUTS, bootstrap_r2, make_regressor, position_targets, r2_per_output,                      shape_basis, shape_targets, squared_errors) from .pilot import strip_private from .randshapes import build_rand_raw from .randstudy import BASELINE_SETS, rand_arm_config from .statedecode import (POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2, STATE_CONTROL_MIN, build_written_maps, get_state_arrays,                           load_probe_reference, sub_indices) from .study import combine_verdicts NOISE_MULTS = (0.0, 1.0, 4.0, 16.0) BASE_NOISE = 0.015                      # sensor noise std per raw sample (ExperimentConfig.sensor_noise) BAND = 0.10                             # "about equal to the probe" tolerance MIN_R2 = 0.10 STATES = ("mod", "mod_s", "mod_H", "mod_ring", "u_full", "u_ring") VARIANTS = ("pool4", "sub24") PRIMARY_MULT = "1.0" # -------------------------------------------------------------------- features def build_features(full: dict, ring: dict, medium) -> dict:     """The probe-visible modulation maps and the fast fields, from the cached pooled state arrays."""     sm, hm = medium.slow_mod, medium.holo_mod     return {"mod": sm * full["s"] + hm * full["H"], "mod_s": sm * full["s"], "mod_H": hm * full["H"],             "mod_ring": sm * ring["s"], "u_full": full["u"], "u_ring": ring["u"],             "NULL": np.zeros_like(full["H"])} def add_noise(X: np.ndarray, mult: float, seed: int, idx: int) -> np.ndarray:     """Gaussian readout noise of std BASE_NOISE * mult; the SAME realisation for every state at a     given (seed, idx), so differences between states are differences of signal only."""     if mult == 0.0:         return X     return X + BASE_NOISE * mult * np.random.default_rng([seed, 99, idx]).normal(size=X.shape) # --------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def best(sres: dict, name: str, mult: str, variant: str) -> tuple[float, str]:     """(best R2 over readouts, counted only if its paired interval versus NULL is above 0; readout)."""     cells = sres["states"][name][mult][variant]     r = max(cells, key=lambda k: cells[k]["r2"])     c = cells[r]     return (c["r2"] if c["diff_vs_null"]["lo"] > 0 else 0.0), r def verdict(sres: dict, mult: str = PRIMARY_MULT) -> dict:     """Pre-registered decision rule (fixed before the reported runs), applied per seed.     0. INVALID_CLIPPED          the safety clip fired.     -  INCONCLUSIVE             ``mod`` or NULL missing.     1. POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS   Fourier features do not decode the descriptors (R2 < 0.95).     2. CONTROL_FAILS            NULL (zeros plus noise) has R2 > 0.05 anywhere: a leak.     3. STATE_CONTROL_FAILS      the pooled written map does not decode placement (R2 < 0.25).     Then, with m = best R2 of the modulation map ``mod`` (counted only if its interval versus NULL is     above 0) at readout noise ``mult`` x the sensor noise, m_sub the same from only 24 values, and     p = the v10 probe-based R2 of the same trials (BAND = 0.10):     4. PROBE_PATH_LOSSY         m_sub >= p + BAND: even 24 values of the map hold more shape information                                 than the probe recovers: the probe readout is inefficient beyond bandwidth.        BANDWIDTH_LIMITED        m >= p + BAND but m_sub < p + BAND: the probe recovers about what 24 values                                 hold; more sensors are what is missing.        PROBE_EXPLAINED          p - BAND <= m < p + BAND: the probe gets what the modulation map holds.        PROBE_BEATS_MODULATION   m < p - BAND: the probe recovers more than the map holds: it uses other                                 channels, and the first-order picture is incomplete.        NO_PROBE_REFERENCE       no probe R2 supplied.     Qualifiers: ``channel`` (which part of the map carries shape, R2 >= 0.10: slow / hologram / both /     neither) and ``fast`` (fast-visible / fast-buried: u_full at this noise).     """     st = sres["states"]     if sres["clip_events"] > 0:         return {"outcome": "INVALID_CLIPPED", "text": "Safety clip fired in the cached state arrays.",                 "next": "Lower the drive and regenerate the state arrays."}     if "mod" not in st or "NULL" not in st:         return {"outcome": "INCONCLUSIVE", "text": "Needs mod and NULL.", "next": "Run with both."}     pc = sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"]["shape"]["linear"]["r2"]     if pc < POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2:         return {"outcome": "POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"Fourier features decode the descriptors with R2 = {pc:.3f} < {POSITIVE_CONTROL_R2}.",                 "next": "Fix the target construction first."}     leaks = [(m, v, r) for m in st["NULL"] for v in VARIANTS for r, c in st["NULL"][m][v].items() if c["r2"] > 0.05]     if leaks:         return {"outcome": "CONTROL_FAILS", "text": f"NULL (noise only) has R2 > 0.05 for {leaks[:3]}: a leak.",                 "next": "Find and remove the leaking channel first."}     mp = max(c["r2"] for c in sres["baselines"]["map"]["position"].values())     if mp < STATE_CONTROL_MIN:         return {"outcome": "STATE_CONTROL_FAILS",                 "text": f"The pooled written map decodes placement with R2 = {mp:.2f} < {STATE_CONTROL_MIN}.",                 "next": "Fix the pipeline first."}     m, rm = best(sres, "mod", mult, "pool4")     m_sub, _ = best(sres, "mod", mult, "sub24")     s_r = best(sres, "mod_s", mult, "pool4")[0]     h_r = best(sres, "mod_H", mult, "pool4")[0]     channel = ("both" if s_r >= MIN_R2 and h_r >= MIN_R2 else "slow" if s_r >= MIN_R2                else "hologram" if h_r >= MIN_R2 else "neither")     u = best(sres, "u_full", mult, "pool4")[0]     fast = "fast-visible" if u >= MIN_R2 else "fast-buried"     tail = (f" Modulation map at noise x{mult}: R2 = {m:.2f} (24 values: {m_sub:.2f}); slow part {s_r:.2f}, "             f"hologram part {h_r:.2f}; fast field {u:.2f}.")     p = sres.get("probe_r2")     base = {"channel": channel, "fast": fast}     if p is None:         return {"outcome": "NO_PROBE_REFERENCE", **base, "text": "No probe R2 supplied." + tail,                 "next": "Provide the v10 decode_results.json."}     if m_sub >= p + BAND:         return {"outcome": "PROBE_PATH_LOSSY", **base,                 "text": f"24 values of the modulation map hold R2 = {m_sub:.2f}, more than the probe's {p:.2f}: the "                         f"probe readout is inefficient beyond bandwidth." + tail,                 "next": "Improve the probe/readout (longer or stronger probe, better features) before adding sensors."}     if m >= p + BAND:         return {"outcome": "BANDWIDTH_LIMITED", **base,                 "text": f"The map holds R2 = {m:.2f}, but 24 values hold {m_sub:.2f}, about the probe's {p:.2f}: "                         f"channel count is what is missing." + tail,                 "next": "Simulate with more sensors (48 or 96) and re-decode."}     if m >= p - BAND:         return {"outcome": "PROBE_EXPLAINED", **base,                 "text": f"The modulation map holds R2 = {m:.2f}, about the probe's {p:.2f}: the probe gets what "                         f"the map holds." + tail,                 "next": "The limit is the stored information itself; improve what is written (drive, plasticity)."}     return {"outcome": "PROBE_BEATS_MODULATION", **base,             "text": f"The probe recovers {p:.2f}, more than the modulation map holds ({m:.2f}): it uses other "                     f"channels and the first-order picture is incomplete." + tail,             "next": "Decode from the fast field's parametric effects and nonlinear terms."} def effective_noise_multiplier(sres: dict, target: float, name: str = "mod", variant: str = "pool4"):     """Readout-noise multiplier at which a direct readout of ``name`` is exactly as informative as     ``target`` (the probe's R2): interpolated linearly in log(multiplier) between the noisy levels of     the sweep.  Descriptive only (not part of the verdict): it replaces the arbitrary assumption that the     probe is noised like a pooled state value.  Returns a float, ``"<m"`` if even the least noisy positive     level holds less than the target, ``">m"`` if even the noisiest holds more, or None."""     pts = sorted((float(m), max(c["r2"] for c in d[variant].values()))                  for m, d in sres["states"][name].items() if float(m) > 0)     if not pts:         return None     if target > pts[0][1]:         return f"<{pts[0][0]:g}"     if target < pts[-1][1]:         return f">{pts[-1][0]:g}"     for (m1, r1), (m2, r2) in zip(pts, pts[1:]):         if r1 >= target >= r2:             t = 0.0 if r1 == r2 else (r1 - target) / (r1 - r2)             return float(10 ** (np.log10(m1) + t * (np.log10(m2) - np.log10(m1))))     return None # ------------------------------------------------------------------- the study def run_modmap(n_trials: int = 240, seeds=(0, 1), readouts=DECODE_READOUTS, noise_mults=NOISE_MULTS,                cache_dir=None, probe_json=None, probe_r2=None, statedecode_json=None, n_boot: int = 2000,                k: int = 5, repeats: int = 3, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:     t0 = time.time()     basis = shape_basis()     res = {"config": {"n_trials": n_trials, "seeds": list(seeds), "readouts": list(readouts),                       "noise_mults": list(noise_mults), "base_noise": BASE_NOISE, "band": BAND, "n_boot": n_boot},            "seeds": {}}     for seed in seeds:         cfg = rand_arm_config("FULL", seed, n_trials)         Y = shape_targets(cfg, basis)         full, _ = get_state_arrays("FULL", seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)         ring, _ = get_state_arrays("RING_ONLY", seed, n_trials, cache_dir, verbose)         feats = build_features(full, ring, cfg.base.medium)         idx = sub_indices(full["H"].shape[1])         level = 1.0 - 0.05 / (len(STATES) * len(readouts))         E = {}         for mi, mult in enumerate(noise_mults):             for n, X in feats.items():                 Xn = add_noise(X, mult, seed, mi)                 for v in VARIANTS:                     for r in readouts:                         E[(n, str(mult), v, r)] = squared_errors(Xn if v == "pool4" else Xn[:, idx], Y,                                                                  make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed)         sres = {"states": {}, "baselines": {}, "family_level": level, "clip_events": float(full["clip_events"] + ring["clip_events"]),                 "probe_r2": None}         if probe_json:             sres["probe_r2"] = load_probe_reference(probe_json, seed)         if sres["probe_r2"] is None and probe_r2 is not None:             sres["probe_r2"] = float(probe_r2)         for n in feats:             sres["states"][n] = {}             for mult in noise_mults:                 sres["states"][n][str(mult)] = {}                 for v in VARIANTS:                     sres["states"][n][str(mult)][v] = {}                     for r in readouts:                         Ea = E[(n, str(mult), v, r)]                         ci = bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)                         d = (bootstrap_r2(Ea, Y, E[("NULL", str(mult), v, r)], n_boot, seed, level) if n != "NULL"                              else {"point": 0.0, "lo": 0.0, "hi": 0.0})                         sres["states"][n][str(mult)][v][r] = {                             "r2": ci["point"], "r2_lo": ci["lo"], "r2_hi": ci["hi"],                             "diff_vs_null": {"diff": d["point"], "lo": d["lo"], "hi": d["hi"]},                             "per_output": [float(x) for x in r2_per_output(Ea, Y)]}         raw, _ = build_rand_raw(cfg)         maps = build_written_maps(cfg)         P = position_targets(cfg)         sres["baselines"]["fft_concat"] = {"shape": {r: {"r2": bootstrap_r2(squared_errors(BASELINE_SETS["fft_concat"](raw), Y, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed), Y, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)["point"]} for r in readouts}}         sres["baselines"]["map"] = {"position": {r: {"r2": bootstrap_r2(squared_errors(maps, P, make_regressor(r), k, repeats, seed), P, n_boot=n_boot, seed=seed)["point"]} for r in readouts}}         sres["verdict"] = verdict(sres)         sres["verdict_at"] = {str(m): verdict(sres, str(m))["outcome"] for m in noise_mults if m > 0}         if sres["probe_r2"] is not None:             sres["effective_noise"] = {f"{n}/{v}": effective_noise_multiplier(sres, sres["probe_r2"], n, v)                                        for n, v in (("mod", "pool4"), ("mod", "sub24"), ("mod_s", "pool4"), ("u_full", "pool4"))}         if statedecode_json and Path(statedecode_json).exists():             ref = json.loads(Path(statedecode_json).read_text())["seeds"][str(seed)]["states"]             pairs = {"mod_H": "H", "mod_s": "s_full", "u_full": "u_full", "u_ring": "u_ring"}             sres["reproduction"] = {a: max(abs(sres["states"][a]["0.0"]["pool4"][r]["r2"] - ref[b]["pool4"]["shape"][r]["r2"])                                            for r in readouts) for a, b in pairs.items() if "0.0" in sres["states"][a]}         res["seeds"][seed] = sres         if verbose:             print(f"[seed {seed}] {sres['verdict']['outcome']}: {sres['verdict']['text']}", flush=True)     res["combined"] = combine_verdicts({s: r["verdict"] for s, r in res["seeds"].items()})     res["seconds"] = time.time() - t0     return res def format_report(res: dict) -> str:     c = res["config"]     rd = c["readouts"]     lines = [f"WaveBrain modulation-map decoding (trials={c['n_trials']}, seeds={c['seeds']}, readout noise = multiple of "              f"the sensor noise {c['base_noise']}, same realisation for every state; band {c['band']})"]     for seed, s in res["seeds"].items():         lines += ["", f"=== seed {seed}: shape R2 [95% CI] ===",                   f"{'state / noise':<20}" + "".join(f"{v + '/' + r:>24}" for v in VARIANTS for r in rd),                   "-" * (20 + 24 * len(VARIANTS) * len(rd))]         for n, d in s["states"].items():             for m in d:                 row = f"{n + ' x' + m:<20}"                 for v in VARIANTS:                     for r in rd:                         x = d[m][v][r]                         row += f"{x['r2']:+.2f} [{x['r2_lo']:+.2f},{x['r2_hi']:+.2f}]".rjust(24)                 lines.append(row)         lines += ["", f"controls: Fourier features shape R2 {s['baselines']['fft_concat']['shape']['linear']['r2']:+.3f}; "                       f"pooled written map placement R2 {max(v['r2'] for v in s['baselines']['map']['position'].values()):+.2f}"]         if s.get("probe_r2") is not None:             lines.append(f"probe readout (v10) shape R2 of the same trials, FULL arm, base noise: {s['probe_r2']:+.2f}")         if "reproduction" in s:             lines.append("reproduction of v11 (noise-free; max |delta R2|, R2 is scale-invariant): "                          + "  ".join(f"{a} {d:.1e}" for a, d in s["reproduction"].items()))         if "effective_noise" in s:             lines.append("noise multiplier at which a direct readout equals the probe's R2 (descriptive): "                          + "  ".join(f"{k} {v if isinstance(v, str) else format(v, '.1f')}" for k, v in s["effective_noise"].items()))         v = s["verdict"]         lines += ["", f"VERDICT (seed {seed}, noise x{PRIMARY_MULT}): {v['outcome']}"                   + (f"  [{v['channel']}, {v['fast']}]" if "channel" in v else ""), f"  {v['text']}", f"  Next: {v['next']}",                   f"  outcome at other noise levels: {s['verdict_at']}"]     lines += ["", f"COMBINED: {res['combined']['outcome']}  (per seed: {res['combined']['per_seed']})"]     return "\n".join(lines) def main(argv=None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawTextHelpFormatter)     ap.add_argument("--trials", type=int, default=240)     ap.add_argument("--seeds", default="0,1")     ap.add_argument("--cache", default=None, help="directory of cached state arrays (from statedecode)")     ap.add_argument("--probe-json", default=None, help="decode_results.json of the v10 diagnostic")     ap.add_argument("--probe-r2", type=float, default=None, help="probe reference if no json is given")     ap.add_argument("--statedecode-json", default=None, help="v11 statedecode_results.json for the reproduction check")     ap.add_argument("--boot", type=int, default=2000)     ap.add_argument("--json", default=None)     a = ap.parse_args(argv)     res = run_modmap(a.trials, tuple(int(x) for x in a.seeds.split(",")), cache_dir=a.cache, probe_json=a.probe_json,                      probe_r2=a.probe_r2, statedecode_json=a.statedecode_json, n_boot=a.boot)     print("\n" + format_report(res) + f"\n\n(total {res['seconds']:.0f}s)")     if a.json:         def stringify(o):             return {str(k): stringify(v) for k, v in o.items()} if isinstance(o, dict) else o         with open(a.json, "w") as fh:             json.dump(strip_private(stringify(res)), fh, indent=2)     return 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     raise SystemExit(main()) ``` ### 12.4 Unchanged earlier tests and scripts #### `tests/test_config.py` ```python import pytest from wavebrain.config import ExperimentConfig, MediumConfig from wavebrain.experiment import validate_experiment def test_default_medium_has_stability_certificate():     cfg = MediumConfig()     assert cfg.stability_number <= 3.8     assert cfg.c2_min > 0     assert cfg.c2_nominal <= cfg.c2_max <= 0.5 @pytest.mark.parametrize("kwargs", [     dict(c2_max=0.60),                        # above the 2-D leapfrog limit     dict(beta=0.5),                           # cubic stiffness too large for u_limit     dict(beta=-0.1),                          # anti-restoring nonlinearity (original bug)     dict(damping=1.0),     dict(damping_slow=-0.1),     dict(nx=40, ny=40),                       # grid smaller than the sponge allows     dict(c_fast=0.7),                         # c_fast^2 > c2_max     dict(holo_decay=1.5),     dict(holo_sat=0.0),     dict(c2_min_frac=0.0), ]) def test_invalid_medium_configs_are_rejected(kwargs):     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         MediumConfig(**kwargs) def test_default_experiment_is_consistent():     info = validate_experiment(ExperimentConfig())     assert info["needed_steps"] <= info["window"] ``` #### `tests/test_medium.py` ```python """Physics tests for the wave medium, including regression tests for each defect found in the original script (plateau invasion, c^2 < 0 / > CFL, dead boundary).""" import copy import numpy as np import pytest from wavebrain.config import MediumConfig from wavebrain.medium import WaveMedium from wavebrain.sensors import SensorArray from wavebrain.sources import (GaussianPulse, Gated, Sine, Source, line_profile,                                make_pacemakers, make_pattern) def make_cfg(**kw):     base = dict(ny=64, nx=64, sponge_width=12, dtype="float64")     base.update(kw)     return MediumConfig(**base) def linear_cfg(**kw):     """Homogeneous, linear, undamped, no absorber: a pure leapfrog wave equation."""     base = dict(damping=0.0, damping_slow=0.0, beta=0.0, slow_mod=0.0, holo_mod=0.0,                 slow_drive=0.0,
