sponge_strength=0.0)     base.update(kw)     return make_cfg(**base) def gaussian_blob(shape, cy, cx, radius, amp):     yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:shape[0], 0:shape[1]]     return amp * np.exp(-((yy - cy) ** 2 + (xx - cx) ** 2) / (2.0 * radius ** 2)) def set_state(sim, field):     """Zero-velocity initial condition: u^n = u^{n-1} = field."""     sim.u[...] = field     sim.u_prev[...] = field def simple_energy(sim):     cur, prev = sim.u.astype(float), sim.u_prev.astype(float)     gy, gx = np.gradient(cur)     return 0.5 * np.sum((cur - prev) ** 2) + 0.5 * sim.cfg.c2_nominal * np.sum(gx ** 2 + gy ** 2) # ------------------------------------------------------------------ integrator def test_idle_medium_stays_exactly_zero():     sim = WaveMedium(make_cfg())     sim.run(50)     assert not sim.u.any() and not sim.s.any() and not sim.H.any() def test_linear_scheme_conserves_discrete_energy():     sim = WaveMedium(linear_cfg())     set_state(sim, gaussian_blob((64, 64), 32, 32, 3.0, 1.0))     sim.step()     e0 = sim.staggered_energy()     worst = 0.0     for _ in range(600):                     # long enough to reflect off the walls many times         sim.step()         worst = max(worst, abs(sim.staggered_energy() - e0) / e0)     assert worst < 1e-9 def test_wave_speed_matches_c_fast():     cfg = linear_cfg(ny=96, nx=96, sponge_width=14, sponge_strength=0.4)     sim = WaveMedium(cfg)     sim.add_source(Source(line_profile(60, 2), 18, 20, GaussianPulse(0.05, 30.0, 8.0), "line"))     sensors = SensorArray([48, 48], [34, 64])     rec = sim.run(200, sensors)     corr = np.correlate(rec[1], rec[0], mode="full")     lag = int(np.argmax(corr)) - (rec.shape[1] - 1)     speed = 30.0 / lag     assert speed == pytest.approx(cfg.c_fast, rel=0.05) def test_restoring_cubic_term_pulls_a_plateau_down():     """Original code pushed |u| > ~25 *outward* by a constant 18.5 per step."""     cfg = make_cfg(damping=0.0, slow_mod=0.0, holo_mod=0.0, sponge_strength=0.0, beta=0.05)     sim = WaveMedium(cfg)     plateau = np.zeros((64, 64)); plateau[10:54, 10:54] = 1.0   # flat => laplacian = 0 inside     set_state(sim, plateau)     sim.step()     assert sim.u[32, 32] == pytest.approx(1.0 - cfg.beta, abs=1e-12)     set_state(sim, -plateau)     sim.step()     assert sim.u[32, 32] == pytest.approx(-1.0 + cfg.beta, abs=1e-12) def test_no_plateau_invasion_blob_decays_to_zero():     """Regression: in the original, one blob grew into a uniform +48.6 plateau (100% of cells)."""     sim = WaveMedium(MediumConfig())     set_state(sim, gaussian_blob((112, 112), 56, 56, 6.0, 1.5))     energies = []     for block in range(4):         sim.run(500)         energies.append(simple_energy(sim))     assert energies == sorted(energies, reverse=True)           # monotone decay     assert np.abs(sim.u).max() < 1e-2     assert (np.abs(sim.u) > 0.05).mean() == 0.0     assert sim.clip_events == 0 def test_sponge_absorbs_outgoing_waves():     absorbing = WaveMedium(linear_cfg(sponge_strength=0.4))     reflecting = WaveMedium(linear_cfg(sponge_strength=0.0))     field = gaussian_blob((64, 64), 32, 32, 3.0, 1.0)     ratios = []     for sim in (absorbing, reflecting):         set_state(sim, field)         sim.step()         e0 = simple_energy(sim)         sim.run(700)         ratios.append(simple_energy(sim) / e0)     assert ratios[0] < 0.02          # < 2% of the energy survives (effective |R|^2 < 0.02)     assert ratios[1] > 0.5           # without the layer energy stays: the test discriminates # ----------------------------------------------------------------- stability def test_wave_speed_is_clipped_into_safe_range():     """Regression: the original c^2 ranged from -0.44 to 1.06 (stable limit 0.5)."""     sim = WaveMedium(MediumConfig())     cfg = sim.cfg     sim.H[...] = -1e3     sim._s[...] = -1e3     c2 = sim._c2(sim.s)     assert c2.min() >= cfg.c2_min - 1e-9 and c2.min() > 0     sim.H[...] = 1e3     sim._s[...] = 1e3     assert sim._c2(sim.s).max() <= cfg.c2_max + 1e-9 def test_long_stress_run_stays_inside_stability_envelope():     cfg = def test_hologram_is_written_locally_and_bounded():     cfg = MediumConfig()     sim = WaveMedium(cfg)     sim.plastic = True     _write(sim)     centre = sim.H[44:68, 44:68].mean()     corner = sim.plastic = True     _write(sim)     sim.plastic = False     before = sim.H.copy()     sim.add_source(Source(line_profile(60, 2), 25, 21, Sine(0.02, 0.35), "probe"))     n = 120     sim.run(n) test_clone_is_independent():     sim = WaveMedium(MediumConfig())     sim.plastic = True     _write(sim, steps=60)     snapshot_u, snapshot_h, t = sim.u.copy(), sim.H.copy(), sim.t     twin = test_simulation_is_deterministic():     def go():         sim = WaveMedium(MediumConfig())         sim.plastic = True         _write(sim, steps=80)         return sim.u.copy(), sim.H.copy()     (u1, h1), (u2, h2) = go(), go()     np.testing.assert_array_equal(u1, u2)     np.testing.assert_array_equal(h1, h2) # --------------------------------------------------------- sources & sensors def test_sources_overlapping_the_boundary_ring_are_rejected():     sim = WaveMedium(MediumConfig())     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         sim.add_source(Source(np.ones((4, 4)), 0, 10, Sine(1, 1))) ```python """Step-1 tests: the readout must sit where signals actually are, at a time they arrive.""" import numpy as np import pytest from wavebrain.config import ExperimentConfig, MediumConfig from wavebrain.experiment import _probe_source, build_sensors from wavebrain.medium import WaveMedium from wavebrain.sensors import (SensorArray, arrival_steps, check_timing, probe_geometry,                                ring_layout) def test_ring_layout_is_inside_the_interior():     cfg = MediumConfig()     s = ring_layout(cfg, n_per_side=8)     assert len(s) == 24     m = cfg.sponge_width     assert s.rows.min() >= m and s.rows.max() < cfg.ny - m     assert s.cols.min() >= m and s.cols.max() < cfg.nx - m     assert len(set(zip(s.rows.tolist(), s.cols.tolist()))) == 24      # no duplicates @pytest.mark.parametrize("row,col", [(50, 111), (50, 0), (0, 50), (111, 50),   # boundary ring                                       (50, 100), (50, 10), (5, 50)])         # inside the absorber def test_validate_rejects_boundary_and_absorber_sensors(row, col):     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         SensorArray([row], [col]).validate(MediumConfig()) def test_sensor_array_rejects_malformed_input():     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         SensorArray([1, 2], [3])     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         SensorArray([], []) def test_unknown_side_is_rejected():     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         ring_layout(MediumConfig(), sides=("diagonal",)) def test_timing_check_rejects_the_original_220_step_window():     """Original: probe at the left edge, readout column at the right edge of a 380 grid,     c = 0.58 -> ~576 steps needed but only 220 recorded."""     cfg = MediumConfig(nx=380, ny=380, c_fast=0.58, sponge_width=32)     sensors = ring_layout(cfg, n_per_side=4, sides=("right",))     col, r0, r1 = probe_geometry(cfg)     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window too short"):         check_timing(cfg, sensors, col, (r0, r1), readout_steps=220, burst_steps=100)     ok = check_timing(cfg, sensors, col, (r0, r1), readout_steps=800, burst_steps=100)     assert ok["needed_steps"] <= 800 def test_arrival_time_scales_with_distance_and_speed():     cfg = MediumConfig()     s = SensorArray([56, 56], [40, 80])     a = arrival_steps(cfg, s, probe_col=21, probe_rows=(21, 91))     assert a[1] > a[0]     assert a[1] - a[0] == pytest.approx(40 / cfg.c_fast)     slow = arrival_steps(cfg, s, 21, (21, 91), slowest=True)     assert np.all(slow > a) def test_probe_reaches_validated_sensors_inside_the_window_and_the_check_is_conservative():     ec = ExperimentConfig()     m = ec.medium     sensors = build_sensors(ec)     col, r0, r1 = probe_geometry(m)     sim = WaveMedium(m)     sim.add_source(_probe_source(ec, 0))     rec = sim.run(ec.readout_steps, sensors)     peak_time = np.abs(rec).argmax(axis=1)     peak_amp = np.abs(rec).max(axis=1)     assert peak_amp.min() > 5e-3                               # every sensor sees the probe     last_signal = peak_time.max() + 3 * ec.probe_tau           # packet fully past the sensor     assert last_signal < ec.readout_steps     info = check_timing(m, sensors, col, (r0, r1), ec.readout_steps, 6 * ec.probe_tau)     assert info["needed_steps"] >= last_signal                  # the static check bounds reality     # right-hand sensors: arrival close to (distance / c) plus the burst centre offset     right = np.flatnonzero(sensors.cols == sensors.cols.max())     predicted = 3 * ec.probe_tau + arrival_steps(m, sensors, col, (r0, r1))[right]     assert np.all(peak_time[right] >= 0.95 * predicted)     assert np.all(peak_time[right] <= 1.25 * predicted) def test_outermost_column_is_dead_while_validated_sensors_see_the_probe():     """Regression for the original readout, which sampled u[:, -1]."""     ec = ExperimentConfig()     sensors = build_sensors(ec)     sim = WaveMedium(ec.medium)     sim.add_source(_probe_source(ec, 0))     last_col, good_col = 0.0, 0.0     right_col = int(sensors.cols.max())     for _ in range(ec.readout_steps):         sim.step()         last_col = max(last_col, float(np.abs(sim.u[:, -1]).max()))         good_col = max(good_col, float(np.abs(sim.u[:, right_col]).max()))     assert last_col == 0.0     assert good_col > 0.05 def test_absorbing_layer_has_no_low_frequency_reflection_problem():     """Regression: the multiplicative sponge kept 15-26% of the energy at omega = 0.15     and got worse with strength; the viscous layer must not."""     from wavebrain.sources import GaussianBurst, Source, line_profile     from dataclasses import replace     def residual(strength):         cfg = replace(MediumConfig(), damping=0.0, damping_slow=0.0, beta=0.0, slow_mod=0.0,                       holo_mod=0.0, slow_drive=0.0, sponge_strength=strength, dtype="float64")         sim = WaveMedium(cfg)         r0, r1 = cfg.sponge_width + 3, cfg.ny - cfg.sponge_width - 3         sim.add_source(Source(line_profile(r1 - r0, 2), r0, cfg.sponge_width + 3,                               GaussianBurst(0.02, 0.15, 48.0, 16.0), "p"))         def energy():             cur, prev = sim.u, sim.u_prev             gy, gx = np.gradient(cur)             return 0.5 * np.sum((cur - prev) ** 2) + 0.5 * cfg.c2_nominal * np.sum(gx ** 2 + gy ** 2)         peak = 0.0         for _ in range(1100):             sim.step()             peak = max(peak, energy())         return energy() / peak     r_weak, r_mid, r_strong = residual(0.1), residual(0.4), residual(0.8)     assert r_weak < 0.01 and r_mid < 0.01 and r_strong < 0.01     assert r_strong <= r_weak          # stronger layer must not reflect more ``` #### `tests/test_classifier.py` ```python import numpy as np import pytest from wavebrain.classifier import (RidgeClassifier, class_separability, cross_val_accuracy,                                   cross_val_predict, permutation_test, stratified_folds,                                   wilson_interval) def blobs(n_per=30, d=50, sep=3.0, seed=0, classes=3):     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     centres = rng.normal(0, 1, (classes, d))     centres *= sep / np.linalg.norm(centres, axis=1, keepdims=True)     X = np.vstack([centres[c] + rng.normal(0, 1, (n_per, d)) / np.sqrt(d) * 2 for c in range(classes)])     y = np.repeat(np.arange(classes), n_per)     return X, y def test_ridge_separates_clusters_and_generalises():     X, y = blobs(seed=1)     Xt, yt = blobs(seed=2)     # different cluster centres per seed -> evaluate on held-out samples of the SAME centres     rng = np.random.default_rng(0)     idx = rng.permutation(len(y))     tr, te = idx[:60], idx[60:]     clf = RidgeClassifier().fit(X[tr], y[tr])     assert clf.score(X[te], y[te]) >= 0.95     assert clf.alpha_ in clf.alphas     assert len(clf.loo_errors_) == len(clf.alphas) def test_random_labels_give_chance_accuracy():     rng = np.random.default_rng(5)     X = rng.normal(size=(60, 200))     y = np.repeat(np.arange(3), 20)     accs = [cross_val_accuracy(X, rng.permutation(y), k=5, seed=s) for s in range(10)]     assert abs(np.mean(accs) - 1 / 3) < 0.08 def test_dual_form_matches_primal_ridge():     rng = np.random.default_rng(1)     X = rng.normal(size=(40, 15)); y = np.repeat([0, 1], 20)     clf = RidgeClassifier(alphas=[2.5]).fit(X, y)     Xs = (X - X.mean(0)) / (X - X.mean(0)).std()     Y = -np.ones((40, 2)); Y[np.arange(40), y] = 1; Yc = Y - Y.mean(0)     w = np.linalg.solve(Xs.T @ Xs + 2.5 * np.eye(15), Xs.T @ Yc)     expected = Xs @ w + Y.mean(0)     np.testing.assert_allclose(clf.decision_function(X), expected, atol=1e-8) @pytest.mark.parametrize("n,d", [(30, 5), (30, 500)]) def test_works_for_both_wide_and_tall_data(n, d):     X, y = blobs(n_per=n // 3, d=d, sep=4.0)     assert RidgeClassifier().fit(X, y).score(X, y) > 0.9 def test_feature_scaling_option():     X, y = blobs()     assert RidgeClassifier(scale="feature").fit(X, y).score(X, y) > 0.9     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         RidgeClassifier(scale="bogus") def test_fit_validates_input():     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         RidgeClassifier().fit(np.zeros((10, 3)), np.zeros(10))          # single class     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         RidgeClassifier().fit(np.zeros((10, 3)), np.arange(9) % 2)      # length mismatch def test_stratified_folds_partition_the_data():     y = np.repeat([0, 1, 2], [20, 20, 21])     folds = stratified_folds(y, k=5, seed=3)     tests = np.concatenate([te for _, te in folds])     assert sorted(tests.tolist()) == list(range(len(y)))                # each sample tested once     for tr, te in folds:         assert not set(tr) & set(te)         counts = np.bincount(y[te], minlength=3)         assert counts.min() >= 4 and counts.max() <= 5                  # balanced per class def test_cv_predict_is_reproducible():     X, y = blobs()     np.testing.assert_array_equal(cross_val_predict(X, y, seed=4), cross_val_predict(X, y, seed=4)) def test_wilson_interval_properties():     lo, hi = wilson_interval(80, 100)     assert lo == pytest.approx(0.7112, abs=2e-3) and hi == pytest.approx(0.8666, abs=2e-3)     assert wilson_interval(0, 50)[0] == 0.0 and wilson_interval(50, 50)[1] == 1.0     assert (wilson_interval(80, 100)[1] - wilson_interval(80, 100)[0]             > wilson_interval(800, 1000)[1] - wilson_interval(800, 1000)[0])     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         wilson_interval(1, 0) def test_permutation_test_distinguishes_signal_from_noise():     X, y = blobs(sep=4.0)     acc = cross_val_accuracy(X, y)     p_signal, _ = permutation_test(X, y, acc, n_perm=40, seed=0)     assert p_signal < 0.05     rng = np.random.default_rng(9)     Xn = rng.normal(size=(90, 50))     acc_n = cross_val_accuracy(Xn, y)     p_noise, null = permutation_test(Xn, y, acc_n, n_perm=40, seed=0)     assert p_noise > 0.05 and abs(null.mean() - 1 / 3) < 0.06 def test_class_separability_orders_correctly():     X, y = blobs(sep=6.0)     rng = np.random.default_rng(2)     assert class_separability(X, y) > 2.0     assert class_separability(rng.normal(size=(90, 200)), y) < 0.6 ``` #### `tests/test_experiment.py` ```python """Sources/patterns,                                  np.random.default_rng(4), bg_amp):             sim.add_source(s)     sim.run(150)     a, b = sim.clone(), sim.clone()     a.add_source(_probe_source(ec, a.t)) test_paired_baseline_residual_in_nonlinear_medium_is_small_and_grows_with_background():     """Parametric coupling (slow-field c^2 modulation, cubic term) is NOT removed by     subtraction.  Measured: ~3% of the response at amp 0.002, ~15% at 0.005 (benchmark     setting), ~66% at 0.02.  The residual must therefore grow monotonically."""     m = MediumConfig(dtype="float64")     ref = _paired_response(m, 0.0)     rel = [np.linalg.norm(_paired_response(m, a) - ref) / np.linalg.norm(ref)            for a in (0.002, 0.005, 0.02)]     assert rel[0] < rel[1] < rel[2]     assert rel[0] < 0.06 and rel[1] < 0.25 # ----------------------------------------------------------------- dataset @pytest.fixture(scope="module") def tiny():     return ------------------------------------------------------- verdict (pre-registered) def _res(p_same=None, p_kind=0.5, diffs=None, clips=0):     p_same = p_same or {"L": 0.5, "D": 0.5, "S": 0.5}     def block(p, pk):         cell = lambda pv: {"p_perm": pv, "acc": 0.5}         return {"same_diff": cell(p), "top_kind": cell(pk), "bottom_kind": cell(pk),                 "pair_id": cell(pk), "stats": {"clip_events": clips}}     paired = {k: {"diff": 0.0, "lo": lo, "hi": hi} for k, (lo, hi) in (diffs or {}).items()}     return {"media": {m: block(p, p_kind) for m, p in p_same.items()}, "paired": paired} def test_verdict_invalid_when_the_clip_fired():     r = _res({"L": 0.01, "D": 0.01, "S": 0.01}, diffs={"D-L": (0.1, 0.2), "S-L": (0.1, 0.2)}, clips=3)     assert verdict(r)["outcome"] == "INVALID_CLIPPED" def test_verdict_too_hard_distinguishes_memory_from_comparison():     assert verdict(_res(p_kind=0.01, diffs={"D-L": (-0.1, 0.1), "S-L": (-0.1, 0.1)})                    )["outcome"] == "TOO_HARD_REMEMBERS_NOT_COMPARES"     assert verdict(_res(p_kind=0.6, diffs={"D-L": (-0.1, 0.1), "S-L": (-0.1, 0.1)})                    )["outcome"] == "TOO_HARD_WEAK_MEMORY" def test_cli_writes_json(tmp_path):     out = tmp_path / "pilot.json"     assert main(["--trials", "24", "--perms", "3", "--media", "L", "--json", str(out)]) == 0     data = json.loads(out.read_text())     assert data["config"]["n_trials"] == 24 and "verdict" in data and "L" in data["media"] ``` #### `tests/test_readouts.py` ```python """Tests for the nonlinear readouts: kernels, leave-one-out maths, XOR / same-different solvability, absence of selection leakage, learning curves.""" import numpy as np import pytest from wavebrain.classifier import RidgeClassifier, cross_val_accuracy from wavebrain.pairtask import PairConfig, labels_and_kinds from wavebrain.readouts import (NONLINEAR_READOUTS, READOUTS, KernelRidgeClassifier,                                 QuadraticPCARidge, learning_curve, loo_residuals, make_readout,                                 poly2_feature_map, poly2_kernel, rbf_kernel, squared_distances,                                 stratified_subsample) def cv(X, y, name, repeats=3, k=5):     return float(np.mean([cross_val_accuracy(X, y, k, s, make_readout(name)) for s in range(repeats)])) def onehot_pair(n_trials=120, noise=0.05, seed=0):     """Additive memory of WHICH shapes were written (no relation between them)."""     y, kinds = labels_and_kinds(PairConfig(n_trials=n_trials))     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     X = np.zeros((n_trials, 6))     X[np.arange(n_trials), kinds[:, 0]] = 1.0     X[np.arange(n_trials), 3 + kinds[:, 1]] = 1.0     return X + rng.normal(0, noise, X.shape), y, kinds # ------------------------------------------------------------------- kernels def test_squared_distances_match_brute_force():     rng = np.random.default_rng(0)     A, B = rng.normal(size=(7, 5)), rng.normal(size=(4, 5))     brute = np.array([[np.sum((a - b) ** 2) for b in B] for a in A])     np.testing.assert_allclose(squared_distances(A, B), brute, atol=1e-10)     assert squared_distances(A, A).min() >= 0.0 def test_rbf_kernel_is_symmetric_unit_diagonal_and_positive_semidefinite():     X = np.random.default_rng(1).normal(size=(30, 10))     d2 = squared_distances(X, X)     K = rbf_kernel(d2, 1.0, float(np.median(d2[~np.eye(30, dtype=bool)])))     np.testing.assert_allclose(K, K.T)     np.testing.assert_allclose(np.diag(K), 1.0)     assert np.linalg.eigvalsh(K).min() > -1e-10     assert K.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and K.min() > 0.0 def test_poly2_kernel_equals_inner_product_of_the_explicit_feature_map():     X = np.random.default_rng(2).normal(size=(8, 4))     scale = 3.7     K = poly2_kernel(X @ X.T, scale)     Phi = poly2_feature_map(X, scale)     np.testing.assert_allclose(Phi @ Phi.T, K, atol=1e-10)     assert Phi.shape[1] == 1 + 4 + 4 + 6 # ------------------------------------------------------- leave-one-out mathematics def _brute_force_loo(K, Yc, alpha):     n = len(K)     out = np.empty_like(Yc)     for i in range(n):         keep = np.arange(n) != i         dual = np.linalg.solve(K[np.ix_(keep, keep)] + alpha * np.eye(n - 1), Yc[keep])         out[i] = Yc[i] - K[i, keep] @ dual     return out @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["rbf", "poly2"]) def test_closed_form_loo_equals_brute_force(kind):     rng = np.random.default_rng(3)     X = rng.normal(size=(25, 8))     G = X @ X.T     d2 = squared_distances(X, X)     K = rbf_kernel(d2, 1.0, float(np.median(d2[~np.eye(25, dtype=bool)]))) if kind == "rbf" \         else poly2_kernel(G, float(np.mean(np.diag(G))))     Y = -np.ones((25, 3)); Y[np.arange(25), rng.integers(0, 3, 25)] = 1.0     Yc = Y - Y.mean(0)     for alpha in (0.01, 1.0, 50.0):         np.testing.assert_allclose(loo_residuals(K, Yc, alpha), _brute_force_loo(K, Yc, alpha),                                    atol=1e-8) def test_v6_ridge_loo_error_equals_brute_force():     """First direct validation of the v6 RidgeClassifier's leave-one-out formula."""     rng = np.random.default_rng(4)     X = rng.normal(size=(20, 12)); y = np.repeat([0, 1], 10)     clf = RidgeClassifier(alphas=[2.5]).fit(X, y)     Xc = X - X.mean(0)     Xs = Xc / (Xc.std() + 1e-12)     Y = -np.ones((20, 2)); Y[np.arange(20), y] = 1.0     Yc = Y - Y.mean(0)     brute = _brute_force_loo(Xs @ Xs.T, Yc, 2.5)     assert clf.loo_errors_[0] == pytest.approx(float(np.mean(brute ** 2)), rel=1e-9) def test_linear_kernel_ridge_reproduces_the_v6_ridge():     rng = np.random.default_rng(5)     X = rng.normal(size=(40, 30)); y = np.repeat([0, 1, 2, 3], [10, 10, 10, 10])     a = RidgeClassifier(alphas=[2.5]).fit(X, y)     b = KernelRidgeClassifier("linear", alphas=[2.5]).fit(X, y)     Xt = rng.normal(size=(15, 30))     np.testing.assert_allclose(b.decision_function(Xt), a.decision_function(Xt), atol=1e-9)     np.testing.assert_array_equal(b.predict(Xt), a.predict(Xt)) # ------------------------------------------------------------ what each readout can do def test_xor_defeats_the_linear_readout_but_not_the_nonlinear_ones():     rng = np.random.default_rng(0)     n = 120     A = rng.integers(0, 2, (n, 2)); y = A[:, 0] ^ A[:, 1]     X = np.hstack([A * 2.0 - 1.0 + rng.normal(0, 0.3, (n, 2)), rng.normal(0, 0.3, (n, 100))])     assert cv(X, y, "linear") < 0.62     for name in NONLINEAR_READOUTS:         assert cv(X, y, name) > 0.8, name def test_remembers_but_does_not_compare_is_solved_by_nonlinear_readouts():     """Additive memory of the two shapes: linear same/different fails, nonlinear lookup works."""     X, y, _ = onehot_pair()     assert cv(X, y, "linear") < 0.62     for name in NONLINEAR_READOUTS:         assert cv(X, y, name) > 0.9, name @pytest.mark.parametrize("name", READOUTS) def test_random_labels_give_chance_accuracy_no_selection_leakage(name):     """Hyper-parameters are chosen inside fit() from training data only: random labels must     stay at chance even though the selection searches a grid."""     rng = np.random.default_rng(6)     X = rng.normal(size=(60, 80)); y = np.repeat([0, 1], 30)     accs = [cross_val_accuracy(X, rng.permutation(y), 5, s, make_readout(name)) for s in range(8)]     assert abs(np.mean(accs) - 0.5) < 0.08 # --------------------------------------------------------------- model internals def test_selected_hyperparameters_come_from_the_grid():     X, y, _ = onehot_pair()     m = KernelRidgeClassifier("rbf").fit(X, y)     assert m.alpha_ in m.alphas and m.param_ in m.gammas     assert np.isfinite(m.loo_error_) and m.dual_.shape == (len(y), 2) def test_kernel_ridge_input_validation():     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         KernelRidgeClassifier("sigmoid")     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         KernelRidgeClassifier("rbf").fit(np.zeros((10, 3)), np.zeros(10))     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         KernelRidgeClassifier("rbf").fit(np.zeros((10, 3)), np.arange(9) % 2)     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         make_readout("svm")     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         QuadraticPCARidge(n_components=1) def test_quadratic_pca_feature_count_and_training_only_projection():     rng = np.random.default_rng(7)     A, B = rng.normal(size=(20, 10)), rng.normal(size=(6, 10))     y = np.repeat([0, 1], 10)     m = QuadraticPCARidge(n_components=4).fit(A, y)     assert m.transform(A).shape[1] == 4 + 4 * 5 // 2     comps, mean = m.components_.copy(), m.mean_.copy()     t1 = m.transform(B)     m.transform(rng.normal(size=(9, 10)))                      # other data must not refit anything     np.testing.assert_array_equal(m.components_, comps)     np.testing.assert_array_equal(m.mean_, mean)     np.testing.assert_array_equal(m.transform(B), t1) def test_quadratic_pca_features_match_a_manual_computation():     rng = np.random.default_rng(8)     X = rng.normal(size=(15, 6)); y = np.repeat([0, 1, 2], 5)     m = QuadraticPCARidge(n_components=3).fit(X, y)     z = ((X[:2] - m.mean_) @ m.components_.T) / m.z_std_     manual = np.array([[*r, *(r[i] * r[j] for i in range(3) for j in range(i, 3))] for r in z])     np.testing.assert_allclose(m.transform(X[:2]), manual, atol=1e-10) # -------------------------------------------------------------------- learning curve def test_stratified_subsample_is_exact_proportional_and_validated():     y = np.repeat([0, 1, 2], [60, 30, 30])     rng = np.random.default_rng(0)     for size in (12, 25, 60, 120):         idx = stratified_subsample(y, size, rng)         assert len(idx) == size == len(set(idx.tolist()))         counts = np.bincount(y[idx], minlength=3)         assert counts.min() >= 2         assert counts[0] >= counts[1] - 1                      # class 0 is the biggest class     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         stratified_subsample(y, 500, rng)     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         stratified_subsample(y, 3, rng) def test_learning_curve_shape_and_improvement_with_more_data():     rng = np.random.default_rng(9)     n = 200     A = rng.integers(0, 2, (n, 2)); y = A[:, 0] ^ A[:, 1]     X = np.hstack([A * 2.0 - 1.0 + rng.normal(0, 0.5, (n, 2)), rng.normal(0, 0.5, (n, 60))])     lc = learning_curve(X, y, make_readout("rbf"), sizes=(8, 32, 128, 500), k=5, seed=0)     assert set(lc) == {8, 32, 128, 500}     assert np.isnan(lc[500])                                    # larger than any training fold     assert lc[128] > lc[8] + 0.1 ``` #### `tests/test_study.py` ```python """Tests def test_heldout_floor_is_below_chance_for_a_prior_only_model(tiny):     """Leave-one-type-out pushes an uninformative model BELOW 0.5 (the held-out class is always     under-represented in training); test_generalizes_needs_both_accuracy_and_a_confidence_interval_above_chance():     assert generalizes({"acc": 0.8, "ci_low": 0.6})     assert not generalizes({"acc": 0.8, "ci_low": 0.45})     assert not generalizes({"acc": 0.65, "ci_low": 0.55}) # ----------------------------------------------------- verdict (pre-registered) def _cell(acc, p):     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": acc - 0.1, "ci_high": acc + 0.1, "p_perm": p} def _sres(lin=0.70, nl=(0.95, 0.70, 0.70), p_nl=0.01, null_p=0.6, lo=(0.2, -0.1, -0.1),           holo=0.70, ring=0.70, clips=0, n_perm=100, ho_acc=0.40, with_heldout=True):     names = ("rbf", "poly2", "quadpca")     def arm(lin_acc, nl_accs, p, clip=0):         sd = {"linear": _cell(lin_acc, p)}         sd.update({n: _cell(a, p) for n, a in zip(names, nl_accs)})         d = {"same_diff": sd, "stats": {"clip_events": clip}}         if with_heldout:             d["heldout"] = {n: {"acc": ho_acc, "ci_low": ho_acc - 0.1} for n in READOUTS}         return d     arms = {"FULL": arm(lin, nl, p_nl, clips), "HOLO_ONLY": arm(holo, (holo,) * 3, 0.03),             "RING_ONLY": arm(ring, (ring,) * 3, 0.03), "NULL": arm(0.5, (0.5,) * 3, null_p)}     paired = {"FULL": {n: {"diff": 0.0, "lo": l, "hi": l + 0.3} for n, l in zip(names, lo)}}     return {"arms": arms, "paired": paired, "n_perm": n_perm} def     return {"acc": acc, "ci_low": acc - 0.1, "ci_high": acc + 0.1, "p_perm": p} def _sres(full_acc=0.80, full_p=0.01, null_p=0.6, lo=0.1, hi=0.3, clips=0, n_perm=100, oracle=0.95,           drop=()):     rd = ("linear", "rbf", "poly2", "quadpca")     arm = lambda acc, p, clip=0: {"same_diff": {r: _cell(acc, p) for r in rd}, "stats": {"clip_events": clip}}     arms = {"FULL": arm(full_acc, full_p, clips), "NULL": arm(0.5, null_p),             "HOLO_ONLY": arm(0.6, 0.2), "RING_ONLY": arm(0.6, 0.2)}     base = {"fft_concat": {r: _cell(0.6, 0.05) for r in rd}, "fft_absdiff": {r: _cell(oracle, 0.01) for r in rd}}     for d in drop:         (arms if d in arms else base).pop(d)     paired = {"fft_concat": {r: {"diff": 0.0, "lo": lo, "hi": hi} for r in rd}}     return {"arms": arms, "baselines": base, "paired": paired, "n_perm": n_perm} def test_verdict_invalid_missing_and_untestable():     assert verdict(_sres(clips=1))["outcome"] == "INVALID_CLIPPED"     assert test_padded_magnitude_is_exactly_invariant_to_placement_and_discriminates_shapes():     s = random_shape(np.random.default_rng(0))     m = padded_magnitude(s)     assert m.shape == (HALF * (HALF // 2 dy_bot, dx_bot)     raw, _ = build_rand_raw(cfg)     com = lambda img: (np.array([(img * np.arange(img.shape[0])[:, None]).sum(), (img * np.arange(img.shape[1])[None, :]).sum()]) / img.sum())     for regression def _linear_problem(n=120, d=40, seed=0, noise=0.1):     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     X = rng.normal(size=(n, d)); W = rng.normal(size=(d, 3))     return X, X @ W + rng.normal(0, noise, (n, 3)) * np.std(X @ W) def test_regressors_recover_linear_and_nonlinear_targets():     X, Y = _linear_problem()     assert r2_from_errors(squared_errors(X, Y, make_regressor("linear")), Y) > 0.95     rng = np.random.default_rng(1)     X2 = rng.normal(size=(200, 3)); Y2 = np.sin(2 * X2[:, :1]) * np.cos(2 * X2[:, 1:2]) + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=(200, 1))     r_lin = r2_from_errors(squared_errors(X2, Y2, make_regressor("linear")), Y2)     r_rbf = r2_from_errors(squared_errors(X2, Y2, make_regressor("rbf")), Y2)     assert r_lin < 0.2 and r_rbf > 0.7 def test_regressor_is_scale_equivariant_in_the_targets_and_validates_input():     X, Y = _linear_problem(n=60)     a = KernelRidgeRegressor("linear").fit(X, Y)     b =     """Memorises its training rows: any leakage of test rows into training would make it perfect."""     def fit(self, X, Y):         self.X, self.Y = X, Y         return self     def predict(self, X):         return self.Y[np.argmin(((X[:, None, :] - self.X[None]) ** 2).sum(-1), axis=1)] def test_out_of_fold_predictions_never_see_their_own_row():     rng = np.random.default_rng(2)     X, Y = rng.normal(size=(60, 5)), rng.normal(size=(60, 2))     pred = kfold_oof(X, Y, _NearestNeighbour, k=5, seed=0)     assert r2_from_errors((Y - pred) ** 2, Y) < 0.2                      # leaked training would give exactly 1     assert not np.allclose(pred, Y) def test_r2_metric_endpoints_and_noise_floor():     rng = np.random.default_rng(3)     Y = rng.normal(size=(100, 4))     assert r2_from_errors(squared_errors(rng.normal(size=(100, 30)), Y, make_regressor("linear")), Y)     assert -0.15 < noise_r2 < 0.02                                       # noise features: at or just below zero     Yc = Y.copy(); Yc[:, 1] = 5.0                                         # a constant output is ignored     assert np.isfinite(r2_from_errors((Yc - Yc.mean(0)) ** 2 * 0.5, Yc))     np.testing.assert_allclose(r2_per_output(np.zeros_like(Y), Y), 1.0) def test_bootstrap_ci_and_pairing():     rng = np.random.default_rng(4)     Y = rng.normal(size=(100, 2))     E_b = (rng.normal(size=(100, 2)) ** 2) * 1.0     ci = bootstrap_r2(E_b, Y, seed=0)     assert ci["lo"] <= ci["point"] <= ci["hi"]     d = bootstrap_r2(E_b * 0.95, Y, E_b, n_boot=1000, seed=0)            # uniformly 5% lower errors     assert d["point"] > 0 and d["lo"] > 0                                 # only visible if the trials stay paired     same = bootstrap_r2(E_b, Y, E_b, n_boot=200, seed=0)     assert same["lo"] == same["hi"] == 0.0 == same["point"] # ----------------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def _cell(r2, lo=0.05):     return {"r2": r2, "r2_lo": r2 `tests/test_modmap.py` ```python """Tests for the modulation-map decoding (state arrays, amplitude-aware noise, probe comparison).""" import json from dataclasses import replace import numpy as np import pytest import wavebrain.modmap as mm from wavebrain.decode import make_regressor, r2_from_errors, squared_errors from wavebrain.modmap import (BASE_NOISE, STATES, add_noise, best, build_features, effective_noise_multiplier, format_report,                               main, run_modmap, verdict) from wavebrain.randstudy import rand_arm_config from wavebrain.statedecode import get_state_arrays # ------------------------------------------------------------------ features def _arrays(seed=0, n=10):     rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)     full = {"H": rng.normal(size=(n, 361)), "u": rng.normal(size=(n, 361)), "s": rng.normal(size=(n, 361))}     ring = {"H": np.zeros((n, 361)), "u": rng.normal(size=(n, 361)), "s": rng.normal(size=(n, 361))}     return full, ring def test_modulation_maps_use_the_medium_coefficients_and_the_right_arms():     full, ring = _arrays()     med = rand_arm_config("FULL", 0, 24).base.medium     f = build_features(full, ring, med)     assert (med.slow_mod, med.holo_mod) == (0.3, 0.5)     np.testing.assert_allclose(f["mod"], 0.3 * full["s"] + 0.5 * full["H"])     np.testing.assert_allclose(f["mod_s"], 0.3 * full["s"]); np.testing.assert_allclose(f["mod_H"], 0.5 * full["H"])     np.testing.assert_allclose(f["mod_ring"], 0.3 * ring["s"])                      # hologram-off arm: slow field only     np.testing.assert_array_equal(f["u_full"], full["u"]); np.testing.assert_array_equal(f["u_ring"], ring["u"])     assert not f["NULL"].any() and set(f) == set(STATES) | {"NULL"}     f2 = build_features(full, ring, replace(med, slow_mod=0.1, holo_mod=0.2))        # follows the configuration     np.testing.assert_allclose(f2["mod"], 0.1 * full["s"] + 0.2 * full["H"]) # ------------------------------------------------------------------- noise def test_add_noise_scaling_sharing_and_reproducibility():     X = np.zeros((200, 361))     assert add_noise(X, 0.0, 3, 0) is X                                              # no noise at multiplier 0     n1, n4 = add_noise(X, 1.0, 3, 1), add_noise(X, 4.0, 3, 1)     assert n1.std() == pytest.approx(BASE_NOISE, rel=0.05) and n4.std() / n1.std() == pytest.approx(4.0, rel=1e-9)     np.testing.assert_array_equal(n1, add_noise(X, 1.0, 3, 1))                       # reproducible     other = np.random.default_rng(0).normal(size=X.shape)     np.testing.assert_allclose(add_noise(other, 1.0, 3, 1) - other, n1)               # SAME realisation for every state     assert not np.allclose(n1, add_noise(X, 1.0, 3, 2)) and not np.allclose(n1, add_noise(X, 1.0, 4, 1))     assert np.abs(n1).max() > 0                                                        # the NULL features really carry noise def test_r2_is_invariant_to_rescaling_the_features_which_justifies_the_reproduction_check():     rng = np.random.default_rng(1)     X = rng.normal(size=(80, 30)); Y = X[:, :3] @ rng.normal(size=(3, 4)) + 0.3 * rng.normal(size=(80, 4))     for r in ("linear", "rbf"):         a = r2_from_errors(squared_errors(X, Y, make_regressor(r)), Y)         b = r2_from_errors(squared_errors(0.37 * X, Y, make_regressor(r)), Y)         assert a == pytest.approx(b, abs=1e-9) # ----------------------------------------------------------------- verdict def _cell(r2, lo=0.05):     return {"r2": r2, "r2_lo": r2 - 0.05, "r2_hi": r2 + 0.05, "diff_vs_null": {"diff": r2, "lo": lo, "hi": r2 + 0.1}} def _entry(pool, sub, lo=0.05):     return {"pool4": {r: _cell(pool, lo) for r in ("linear", "rbf")}, "sub24": {r: _cell(sub, lo) for r in ("linear", "rbf")}} def _sres(m=0.6, m_sub=0.5, p=0.22, s=0.5, h=0.1, u=0.3, null=-0.01, pc=0.999, mp=0.4, lo=0.05, clip=0.0,           probe="given", drop=()):     mults = ("0.0", "1.0", "4.0")     st = {"mod": {k: _entry(m, m_sub, lo) for k in mults}, "mod_s": {k: _entry(s, s, lo) for k in mults},           "mod_H": {k: _entry(h, h, lo) for k in mults}, "mod_ring": {k: _entry(s, s, lo) for k in mults},           "u_full": {k: _entry(u, u, lo) for k in mults}, "u_ring": {k: _entry(u, u, lo) for k in mults},           "NULL": {k: _entry(null, null) for k in mults}}     for d in drop:         st.pop(d)     return {"states": st, "clip_events": clip, "probe_r2": p if probe == "given" else None,             "baselines": {"fft_concat": {"shape": {"linear": {"r2": pc}}}, "map": {"position": {"linear": {"r2": mp}}}}} def test_verdict_invalid_inconclusive_and_the_three_controls():     assert verdict(_sres(clip=3.0))["outcome"] == "INVALID_CLIPPED"     assert verdict(_sres(drop=("NULL",)))["outcome"] == "INCONCLUSIVE"     assert verdict(_sres(pc=0.5))["outcome"] == "POSITIVE_CONTROL_FAILS"     leaky = _sres()     leaky["states"]["NULL"]["4.0"] = _entry(0.2, -0.01)                                # a leak at ANY noise level counts     assert verdict(leaky)["outcome"] == "CONTROL_FAILS"     assert verdict(_sres(null=0.04))["outcome"] != "CONTROL_FAILS"     assert verdict(_sres(mp=0.1))["outcome"] == "STATE_CONTROL_FAILS"     assert verdict(_sres(mp=0.26))["outcome"] != "STATE_CONTROL_FAILS" def test_verdict_classifies_the_probe_against_the_modulation_map():     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.6, m_sub=0.5, p=0.22))["outcome"] == "PROBE_PATH_LOSSY"            # even 24 values beat the probe     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.6, m_sub=0.25, p=0.22))["outcome"] == "BANDWIDTH_LIMITED"          # only the full map does     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.25, m_sub=0.2, p=0.22))["outcome"] == "PROBE_EXPLAINED"     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.15, m_sub=0.1, p=0.35))["outcome"] == "PROBE_BEATS_MODULATION"     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.311, m_sub=0.311, p=0.2))["outcome"] == "PROBE_PATH_LOSSY"         # just above p + band     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.299, m_sub=0.299, p=0.2))["outcome"] == "PROBE_EXPLAINED"          # just below     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.101, m_sub=0.101, p=0.2))["outcome"] == "PROBE_EXPLAINED"          # just inside the lower band     assert verdict(_sres(m=0.099, m_sub=0.099, p=0.2))["outcome"] == "PROBE_BEATS_MODULATION"   # just below it     r = verdict(_sres(probe=None))     assert r["outcome"] == "NO_PROBE_REFERENCE" and "channel" in r def test_verdict_can_be_evaluated_at_another_noise_level():     s = _sres(m=0.6, m_sub=0.5, p=0.22)     s["states"]["mod"]["4.0"] = _entry(0.25, 0.2)     assert verdict(s, "1.0")["outcome"] == "PROBE_PATH_LOSSY" and verdict(s, "4.0")["outcome"] == "PROBE_EXPLAINED" def test_effective_noise_multiplier_interpolates_in_log_scale_and_flags_out_of_range():     s = _sres()     s["states"]["mod"] = {"0.0": _entry(0.9, 0.9), "1.0": _entry(0.4, 0.3), "4.0": _entry(0.2, 0.1), "16.0": _entry(0.0, 0.0)}     assert effective_noise_multiplier(s, 0.3) == pytest.approx(2.0, rel=1e-9)            # ```python """Seed-sensitivity check for conditions A and D (needs results.json from the benchmark). Usage:  python seed_check.py """ import json from dataclasses import replace from wavebrain.benchmark import evaluate from wavebrain.config import ExperimentConfig from wavebrain.experiment import build_dataset res = json.load(open("results.json"))["conditions"] ka = [k for k in res if k.startswith("A")][0]; kd = [k for k in res if k.startswith("D")][0] lines = [f"{'seed':>5}{'A: hologram ON, delay 250':>30}{'D: hologram OFF, delay 1000':>32}",          f"{0:>5}{res[ka]['acc']:>30.3f}{res[kd]['acc']:>32.3f}"] for seed in (1, 2):     base = ExperimentConfig(trials_per_class=30, seed=seed)     a = build_dataset(base); d = build_dataset(replace(base, delay_steps=1000, use_hologram=False))     ra = evaluate(a.features(), a.y, n_perm=10, seed=seed); rd = evaluate(d.features(), d.y, n_perm=10, seed=seed)     lines.append(f"{seed:>5}{ra['acc']:>30.3f}{rd['acc']:>32.3f}") open("seed_check.txt", "w").write("\n".join(lines)) print("\n".join(lines)) ``` #### `pytest.ini` ```ini [pytest] pythonpath = . testpaths = tests addopts = -q ```
